## ז (id: BDB02513)

ז, *Zâyin*, seventh letter; used as numeral 7 in modern Heb.; no evidence of this usage in OT times.



## זאב (id: BDB02514)

זאב (√ of foll.; acc. to FlBericht d. sächs. G. d. W. i. (1846–47\) 430 f. \= Kleinere Schriften iii. 212 f. i.q. Ar. دَأَنَ *drive away, and despise, drive* or *frighten away*, whence دِيءْبٌ, ܕܺܐܒܳܐ, זְאֵב wolf, as *driven* or *chased away;* cf. HomNS 304 MV al.)

> \[Str2061] † I. זְאֵב **n.m.**:[Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6) wolf (NH *id.,* Ar. دِيءْبٌ (*jackal* according to HomNS 303 ff.; but see RSZMG 1880, 373 and DoughtyArab. Deseria i. 327; ii. 144, 145\) Aram. דֵּיבָה, ܕܺܐܒܳܐ As. *zîbu* DlS 47; Eth. ዝእብ፡ *hyena* DI1056 (on format. cf. LagBN 58\); Ph. in cpd.n.pr. זיבקם)—זְאֵב abs. [Gn 49:27](https://ref.ly/Gen49:27) \+ 2 t.; cstr. [Je 5:6](https://ref.ly/Jer5:6); pl. זְאֵבִים Ez [22:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:27); cstr. זְאֵבֵי Hb [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hab1:8), [Zp 3:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:3); —*wolf*, never in narrative; in predictions of peace in Messian. age וְגָר זְאֵב עִם כֶּבֶש [Is 11:6](https://ref.ly/Isa11:6) זְאֵב וְטָלֶה יִרְעוּ כְּאֶחָד [Is 65:25](https://ref.ly/Isa65:25); in simile, of Benjamin's fierceness זְאֵב יִטְרָף ´ב [Gn 49:27](https://ref.ly/Gen49:27); of fierce horses of Chaldeans חַדּוּ מִזְּאֵבֵי עֶרֶב Hb [1:8](https://ref.ly/Hab1:8) (‖קַלּוּ מִנְּמֵרִים); of princes of Judah כִּזְאֵבִים טֹרְפֵי טָרֶף Ez [22:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:27); metaph. of enemies of Judah [Je 5:6](https://ref.ly/Jer5:6) זְאֵב עֲרָבוֺת (‖אַרְיֵה, נָמֵר); of corrupt and oppressive judges שֹׁפְטֶיהָ זְאֵבֵי עֶרֶב [Zp 3:3](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:3) (‖אֲרָיוֺת שֹׁאֲגִים).

> \[Str2062] † II. זְאֵב **n.pr.m.** (*wolf*)—a prince of Midian, Ju [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25), [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25), [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25), [8:3](https://ref.ly/Judg8:3), [Psalm 83:12](https://ref.ly/Ps83:12); also in **n.pr.loc.** רֶקֶב־זְאֵב Ju [7:25](https://ref.ly/Judg7:25) *wine\-vat of Zeeb*.

> \[Str2063] זאֹת **pron.f.** v. זֶה

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 5:6; Judges 7:25; Habakkuk 1:8; Isaiah 65:25; Genesis 49:27; Judges 8:3; Ezekiel 22:27; Psalms 83:12; Isaiah 11:6; Zephaniah 3:3
* **Associated Articles:** זֶה (ID: `BDB02544@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## זבב (id: BDB02518)

זבב (√ of foll.; cf. Ar. دَبٌّ *go hither and thither* (of a man); دَنْدَبَ *make to dangle*, or *move to and fro*, of a thing suspended in the air; but in this sense perhaps denom.)

> \[Str2070] † זְבוּב **n.m.**:[Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1) fly (as *moving to and fro* in the air? cf. Fl NHWBi. 438 b; NH *id.*, As. *zumbu*, DlS 63 f.; Ar. دُبَابٌ, Aram. דִּיבָבָא, ܕܶܒܳܐ, ܕܶܒܳܒܳܐ);—lit. only זְבוּבֵי מָוֶת [Ec 10:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl10:1) i.e. *dead flies;* so AV RV Hi Now al.; \> LXX De al. *death\-bringing, deadly flies;* זְבוּב metaph. of Egyptian army [Is 7:18](https://ref.ly/Isa7:18) (‖דְּבוֺרָה, *bee*, of Assyrian); on בַּעַל זְבוּב v. בַּעַל II. **4,** p. 127 supr.

> \[Str2079] † זַבַּי **n.pr.m.** (Palm. זבי VogNo. 28 (but this perh. \= *loricatus*, cf. زَبْء *lorica*); connex. with above √ uncertain)—Jew in Ezra's time [Ezr 10:28](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:28); LXX Ζαβου(θ); cf. also [Ne 3:20](https://ref.ly/Neh3:20) Kt (LXX Ζαβου etc., but Qr זַכַּי; v. זַכָּי [Ezr 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:9) \= [Ne 7:14](https://ref.ly/Neh7:14)).
> 
> 256:a. זבי: on Palm. זבי (prob. abbrev. from ... זבד), v. Lzb265 Cooke273, 291\.

* **Associated Passages:** Ecclesiastes 10:1; Nehemiah 7:14; Nehemiah 3:20; Ezra 2:9; Ezra 10:28; Isaiah 7:18
* **Associated Articles:** בָּעַל (ID: `BDB01339@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## זָבַד (id: BDB02521)

\[Str2064] † \[זָבַד] **vb.** bestow upon, endow with (Ar. زَبَدَ; Aram. זְבַד, ܙܒܰܕ † [Gn 30:20](https://ref.ly/Gen30:20), ܙܶܒܕܳܐ *dowry;* in Sab. n. pr., v. sub זָבָד infr.; also in Palm. n.pr. נבוזבד Vog73 and many others (זבד, זבדא, זבידא, זבדעתא, זבדבל \= Ζαβδιβηλος, etc.): see EutingSechs Inschr. aus Idalien p. 15\), זְבָדַנִי אלהים אֹתִי זֶבֶד טוֺב [Gn 30:20](https://ref.ly/Gen30:20) a (E), appar. in expl. of name Zebulun, cf. Thes Di; another expl. in vb, v. sub זבל.

> \[Str2065] † זֶ֫בֶד **n.m.** endowment, gift, [Gn 30:20](https://ref.ly/Gen30:20) a cf. foregoing.

> \[Str2066] † זָבָד **n.pr.m.** (*he hath given*, or, *a gift;* cf. Sab. n.pr. זבדם Hal168 DHMZMG 1883, 15, זביד DHMEpigr. Denkm. 50 \= زُبَيْد; also NH n.pr. זביד)—**1\.** a descendant of Judah [1 Ch 2:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:36), [2:37](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:37). **2\.** an Ephraimite [1 Ch 7:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:21). **3\.** one of David's valiant men [1 Ch 11:41](https://ref.ly/1Chr11:41). **4\.** one of the murderers of Joash of Judah [2 Ch 24:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:26) (LXX Ζαβελ, Ζαβεθ, Ζαβαθ), called יוֺזָכָר (q. v. sub הוה, יהוה) [2 K 12:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:22). **5\.** name of three returned exiles who had taken strange wives: **a.** [Ezr 10:27](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:27); **b.** v:[33](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:33); **c.** v:[43](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:43).

> \[Str2071] † זָבוּד **n.pr.m.** (*bestowed*) an officer of Solomon [1 K 4:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs4:5).

> \[Str2072] † זבוד **n.pr.m.** a returned exile [Ezr 8:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:14) Kt; Qr זַכּוּר, q. v. (B om.; A Ζαβουδ, i.e. זָבוּד; LXXL Ζακχουρ).

> \[Str2080] † זבידה Kt, זְבוּדָּה Qr, **n.pr.f.** mother of Jehoiakim [2 K 23:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:36) זְביּדָּ֯ה בַת־פְּדָיָה מִן־רוּמָה; (‖ [2 Ch 36:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:5) om. MT); quite otherwise LXXB Ἰελλα, daughter of Ἐδειλ; in A the names are Εἰελδαφ \& Εἰεδδιλα; LXX [2 Ch 36:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr36:5) Ζε(κ)χωρα \& Νηρειου; LXXL (K \& Ch) Ἀμιταλ \& Ἰερεμιου (confusion with Zedekiah's mother [24:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:18)).

> \[Str2067] † זַבְדִּי **n.pr.m.** (cf. Ar. زَبْدٌ *gift; ? gift of* ´י, vid. DrStud. Bib. i. 5 \& reff.; perh. *my gift, gift to me:* cf. זַבְדִּיאֵל; in NH n.pr. זבדי (זַבְדִּי or זַבְדַּי?); NT Ζεβεδαῖος, e.g. [Mk 1:19](https://ref.ly/Mark1:19), SyrVer ܙܰܒܕܰܝ, *Zebedee*)—**1\.** grandfather of Achan, of Judah (LXX Ζαμβρ(ε)ι etc., LXXL Ζαβδ(ε)ι) [Jos 7:1](https://ref.ly/Josh7:1) (app. \= זִמְרִי [1 Ch 2:6](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:6), LXX Ζαμβρ(ε)ι) v:[17](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:17), v:[18](https://ref.ly/1Chr2:18). **2\.** a Benjamite [1 Ch 8:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:19). **3\.** an officer of David [1 Ch 27:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:27). **4\.** a Levite [Ne 11:17](https://ref.ly/Neh11:17) (om. B; A Ζεχρι, LXXL Ζεχρει) prob. rd. זִכְרִי, as ‖ [1 Ch 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:15).

> \[Str2068] † זַבְדִיאֵל **n.pr.m** (*my gift is God*)—**1\.** father of one of David's officers [1 Ch 27:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:2). **2\.** a prominent Hebrew in Nehemiah's time [Ne 11:14](https://ref.ly/Neh11:14).

> \[Str2069] זְבַדְיָהוּ, זְבַדְיָה **n.pr.m.** (´י *hath bestowed,* cf. יְהוֺזָבָד, \& reff., also זַבְדִּיאֵל supr.)—זְבַדְיָהוּ **1\.** Levites, **a.** [1 Ch 26:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:2); **b.** [2 Ch 17:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:8). **2\.** an officer of Jehoshaphat [2 Ch 19:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:11). זְבַדְיָה **3\.** Benjamites, **a.** [1 Ch 8:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:15); **b.** [1 Ch 8:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:17). **4\.** one of David's men [1 Ch 12:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr12:8). **5\.** an officer of David [1 Ch 27:7](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:7). **6\.** a returning exile [Ezr 8:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:8). **7\.** one of priestly house [Ezr 10:20](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:20).

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 2:17; Genesis 30:20; Ezra 8:8; Joshua 7:1; 2 Kings 23:36; 1 Chronicles 27:27; Ezra 10:43; 1 Chronicles 27:2; 1 Kings 4:5; 1 Chronicles 26:2; Nehemiah 11:17; 1 Chronicles 27:7; 1 Chronicles 9:15; Ezra 8:14; Ezra 10:33; 1 Chronicles 8:15; 1 Chronicles 12:8; Mark 1:19; 1 Chronicles 2:36; Ezra 10:20; 1 Chronicles 8:17; 2 Chronicles 19:11; 1 Chronicles 11:41; 2 Chronicles 24:26; Ezra 10:27; 2 Chronicles 24:18; 1 Chronicles 2:37; 1 Chronicles 2:6; 2 Kings 12:22; Nehemiah 11:14; 2 Chronicles 17:8; 1 Chronicles 2:18; 2 Chronicles 36:5; 1 Chronicles 8:19; 1 Chronicles 7:21


## זָבַח (id: BDB02530)

\[Str2076] זָבַח:134 **vb.** slaughter for sacrifice (NH *id.*, Ph. זבח; As. *Zibû* DlPr 174 cf. LotzTP 174; Ar. ذَبَحَ, Sab. דַבח CISiv. 1, 2, No. 74 l. 11\. 12; Aram. דְּבַח, ܕܒܰܚ, Eth. ) **Qal**:112 *Pf.* ´ז [1 K 8:63](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:63), [13:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:2), זָבַחְתָּ [Ex 20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24) \+ 3 t., זָבַחְתִּי Ez [39:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:19), זָֽבְחוּ [Ex 34:15](https://ref.ly/Exod34:15) [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5), זְבַחְתֶּם [Ex 8:24](https://ref.ly/Exod8:24), זָבַחְנוּ [Ex 8:23](https://ref.ly/Exod8:23); *Impf.* וַיִּזְבַּח [Gn 31:54](https://ref.ly/Gen31:54) \+ 14 t.; 2 ms. sf. תִּזְבָּחֶנּוּ [Dt 15:21](https://ref.ly/Deut15:21), 2 fs. sf. וַתִּזְבָּחִים Ez [16:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:20), 1 s. אֶזְבַּח [Psalm 116:17](https://ref.ly/Ps116:17), אֶזְבְּחָה [Psalm 27:6](https://ref.ly/Ps27:6), [54:8](https://ref.ly/Ps54:8), [Jon 2:10](https://ref.ly/Jonah2:10); 2 mpl. תִּזְבְּחוּ [Lv 19:5](https://ref.ly/Lev19:5) \+ 2 t., תִּזְבָּחוּ [Lv 22:29](https://ref.ly/Lev22:29) \+ 2 t.; 1 pl. נִזְבְּחָה [Ex 3:18](https://ref.ly/Exod3:18) \+, etc. (61 t. Impf.); *Imv.* זְבַח [Psalm 50:14](https://ref.ly/Ps50:14), זִבְחוּ [Ex 8:21](https://ref.ly/Exod8:21), [Psalm 4:6](https://ref.ly/Ps4:6); *Inf. cstr.* זְבֹחַ [1 S 15:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:15) \+ 14 t., לִזְבּוֺחַ [2 Ch 11:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:16), בְּזָבְחוֺ [2 S 15:12](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:12); *Pt. act.* זֹבֵחַ [Ex 13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15) \+ 8 t., זוֺבֵחַ [Is 66:3](https://ref.ly/Isa66:3), pl. זֹבְחִים [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5) \+ (20 t. Pt.)—**I.** *slaughter for sacrifice* **1\.** c. acc. of **a.** kind of sacrifice זֶבַח ´ז [Gn 31:54](https://ref.ly/Gen31:54) \+, † פֶּסַח [Dt 16:2](https://ref.ly/Deut16:2), [16:5](https://ref.ly/Deut16:5), [16:6](https://ref.ly/Deut16:6), † שְׁלָמְים [Dt 27:7](https://ref.ly/Deut27:7), [Jos 8:31](https://ref.ly/Josh8:31), † תּוֺדָה [Psalm 50:14](https://ref.ly/Ps50:14), [50:23](https://ref.ly/Ps50:23), † עֹלוֺת ושׁלמים [Ex 20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24) (E). **b.** victims, (*a*) animals † בָּקָר וָצאֹן [1 K 8:63](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:63), [Nu 22:40](https://ref.ly/Num22:40), † צאן ובקר [2 Ch 18:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:2), † הַבְּרִיאָה Ez [34:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:3), † כָּל־פֶּטֶר רֶחֶם [Ex 13:15](https://ref.ly/Exod13:15) (J); † פָּרִים וְאֵילִים [1 Ch 15:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr15:26), שׁוֺר וָשֶה [Dt 17:1](https://ref.ly/Deut17:1); (β) mankind † בָּנִים וּבָנוֺת [Psalm 106:37](https://ref.ly/Ps106:37), Ez [16:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:20), † זֹבְחֵי אָדָם *sacrificers of mankind* [Ho 13:2](https://ref.ly/Hos13:2) (so Ra AE Hi Wü MV; *men that sacrifice* AV RV after Ki, so Pu Ew Ke Now Che SS). **c.** † תּוֺעֲבַת מִצְרַיִם [Ex 8:22](https://ref.ly/Exod8:22), [8:22](https://ref.ly/Exod8:22) (E), † מָשְׁחָת [Mal 1:14](https://ref.ly/Mal1:14). **2\.** absolute [1 S 1:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:5) \+ oft. **3\.** c. לְ of deity, † לְדָגוֺן Ju [16:23](https://ref.ly/Judg16:23), † לַשְּעִירִם [Lv 17:7](https://ref.ly/Lev17:7) (H), † לַשֵּׁדִים [Dt 32:17](https://ref.ly/Deut32:17), [Psalm 106:37](https://ref.ly/Ps106:37); *other gods* [Ex 34:15](https://ref.ly/Exod34:15) (JE) \+; but usually ליהוה [Ex 3:18](https://ref.ly/Exod3:18) \+ 9 t. JE, [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5), [19:5](https://ref.ly/Lev19:5), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Lev22:29) (all H, not elsewh. in P), [Dt 15:21](https://ref.ly/Deut15:21), [16:2](https://ref.ly/Deut16:2), [17:1](https://ref.ly/Deut17:1), [1 S 1:3](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:3) \+; † לֵאלֽהֵי אָבִיו [Gn 46:1](https://ref.ly/Gen46:1) (E), לאלהים [Psalm 50:14](https://ref.ly/Ps50:14), † לאדני [Mal 1:14](https://ref.ly/Mal1:14). **4\.** with local prep. בְּ [Gn 31:54](https://ref.ly/Gen31:54) (E) \+, עַל [Ex 20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24) (E) \+; לפני [Lv 9:4](https://ref.ly/Lev9:4) (P) \+, על פני השדה [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5) (H). **II.** *slaughter for eating* (connected also with sacrifice, as all eating of flesh among ancient Hebrews was sacrificial, RS Sem. 219\) [1 S 28:24](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:24) (fat calf for Saul), Ez [34:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:3) (fat sheep for shepherds), [2 Ch 18:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:2) (sheep and oxen for Jehoshaphat, c. לְ of person); cf. [Dt 12:15](https://ref.ly/Deut12:15), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Deut12:21) (abs.), [1 K 19:21](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:21). **III.** *slaughter in divine judgment* Ez [39:17–19](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:17-Ezek39:19) c. acc. זִבְחִי (Gog and Magog as feast for vultures), [1 K 13:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:2), [2 K 23:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:20) (priests of high\-places on their own altars). † **Pi.**:22 *Pf.* זִבַּח [2 Ch 33:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:22), זִבְּחוּ [Psalm 106:38](https://ref.ly/Ps106:38), זִבֵָּחוּ [Ho 12:12](https://ref.ly/Hos12:12); *Impf.* יְזַבֵּחַ Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16), יְזַבֵָּחוּ [Ho 4:13](https://ref.ly/Hos4:13), [4:14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Hos11:2) (7 t. Impf.); *Inf. cstr.* לְֹזַבֵּחַ [1 K 12:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:32); *Pt.* מְזַבֵּחַ [1 K 3:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:3), pl. מְזַבְּחִים [2 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:6) \+ 8 t., מְזַבְּחוֺת [1 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:8); —*sacrifice*, **1\.** of the abundant sacrifices made to Yahweh by Solomon [1 K 8:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:5) \= [2 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:6), and Hezekiah [2 Ch 30:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:22), and prob. intensive; but **2\.** elsewhere of sacrifice to other deities, possibly iterative in some cases, but certainly not in all: לַבְּעָלִים [Ho 11:2](https://ref.ly/Hos11:2), לֵאלֽהֵיהֶן [1 K 11:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:8), לַעֲגָלִים [12:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:32), לְחֶרְמוֺ Hb [1:16](https://ref.ly/Hab1:16), לַעֲצַבֵּי כְָ\+נעַן [Psalm 106:38](https://ref.ly/Ps106:38), לֵאלֽתֵי דַּרְמֶשֶק [2 Ch 28:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:23), לְכָל־הַפְּסִילִים [33:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:22); or in unlawful places עַל־רָאשֵׁי הֶהָרִים [Ho 4:13](https://ref.ly/Hos4:13) cf. v:[14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14), בַּגִּלְגָּל [12:12](https://ref.ly/Hos12:12), בַּבָּמוֺת [1 K 3:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:2), [3:3](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:3), [22:44](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:44), [2 K 12:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:4), [14:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs14:4), [15:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:4), [15:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs15:35), [16:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:4) \= [2 Ch 28:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:4). It is used without direct obj. [Ho 4:14](https://ref.ly/Hos4:14) and oft.; also c. acc. of victim שְׁוָרִים [Ho 12:12](https://ref.ly/Hos12:12) צאֹן וּבָקָר [1 K 8:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:5) \= [2 Ch 5:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr5:6); sons and daughters [Psalm 106:38](https://ref.ly/Ps106:38), זִבְחֵי שְׁלָמִים [2 Ch 30:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:22).

> \[Str2077] I. זֶ֫בַח:162 **n.m.**:Ju [16:23](https://ref.ly/Judg16:23) sacrifice, ´ז abs. [Gn 31:54](https://ref.ly/Gen31:54) \+; cstr. [Ex 34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25) \+; ָ\+זבַח [Lv 17:8](https://ref.ly/Lev17:8) \+ 16 t.; sf. זִבְחוֺ [Lv 7:16](https://ref.ly/Lev7:16), Ez [34:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek34:15), זִבְחֲכֶם [Lv 19:6](https://ref.ly/Lev19:6); pl. זְבָחִים [Gn 46:1](https://ref.ly/Gen46:1) \+; cstr. זִבְחֵי [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5) \+; sf. 3 mpl. זִבְחֵיהֶם [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5), זְבָחֵימוֺ [Dt 32:38](https://ref.ly/Deut32:38), זִבְחֹתָם † [Ho 4:19](https://ref.ly/Hos4:19) etc.;—the common and most ancient sacrifice, whose essential rite was eating the flesh of the victim at a feast in which the god of the clan shared by receiving the blood and fat pieces. In the older literature it is distinguished from מִנְחָה and עֹלָה, in the later literature from חַטָּאת and אָשָׁם. † **I.** general name for all sacrifices eaten at feasts:—**1\.** of the God of Israel [Gn 46:1](https://ref.ly/Gen46:1), [Ex 10:25](https://ref.ly/Exod10:25), [18:12](https://ref.ly/Exod18:12) (E) [Ho 3:4](https://ref.ly/Hos3:4), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Hos6:6), [Pr 15:8](https://ref.ly/Prov15:8), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Prov21:3), [21:27](https://ref.ly/Prov21:27), [Is 43:23](https://ref.ly/Isa43:23), [43:24](https://ref.ly/Isa43:24), [56:7](https://ref.ly/Isa56:7), [Je 6:20](https://ref.ly/Jer6:20), [7:21](https://ref.ly/Jer7:21), [7:22](https://ref.ly/Jer7:22), [17:26](https://ref.ly/Jer17:26), [33:18](https://ref.ly/Jer33:18), Ez [40:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:42), [44:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:11), [1 Ch 29:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:21), [29:21](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:21), [2 Ch 7:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:1), [7:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:4), [7:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:5), [7:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:12), [Ne 12:43](https://ref.ly/Neh12:43), [Dn 9:27](https://ref.ly/Dan9:27), [Ec 4:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:17). They should be זִבְחֵי צֶדֶק *sacrifices of righteousness* (offered in righteousness by the righteous) [Dt 33:19](https://ref.ly/Deut33:19), [Psalm 4:6](https://ref.ly/Ps4:6), [51:21](https://ref.ly/Ps51:19); זִבְחֵי הַבְהָבַי *sacrifices of gifts to me* (´י) [Ho 8:13](https://ref.ly/Hos8:13); the temple is בֵּית זָבַָח [2 Ch 7:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:12); they should not be רִיב ´ז *sacrifices of strife*, where strife prevails [Pr 17:1](https://ref.ly/Prov17:1). **2\.** of other deities [Ex 34:15](https://ref.ly/Exod34:15), [Nu 25:2](https://ref.ly/Num25:2), [Dt 32:38](https://ref.ly/Deut32:38) (JE) Ju [16:23](https://ref.ly/Judg16:23), [2 K 10:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:19), [10:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:24), [Ho 4:19](https://ref.ly/Hos4:19), [Is 57:7](https://ref.ly/Isa57:7), Ez [20:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:28); these are זִבְחֵי מֵתִים *sacrifices to dead things* (lifeless gods, opp. to ´י as living), [Psalm 106:28](https://ref.ly/Ps106:28). **II.** there are several kinds of זְבָחִים which are gradually distinguished: † **1\.** *the covenant sacrifice*, between Jacob and Laban [Gn 31:54](https://ref.ly/Gen31:54) (E), with God [Psalm 50:5](https://ref.ly/Ps50:5) (cf. [Ex 24:5](https://ref.ly/Exod24:5), [1 S 11:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:15)). † **2\.** *the passover* זֶבַח חַג הַפֶּסַח [Ex 34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25) (JE), זבח פסח [Ex 12:27](https://ref.ly/Exod12:27) (J); and prob. also זבחי [Ex 23:18](https://ref.ly/Exod23:18) (JE) \= [34:25](https://ref.ly/Exod34:25) (*id.*) † **3\.** *annual sacrifice* זֶבַח הַיָּמִים [1 S 1:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:21), [2:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:19), [20:6](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:6), cf. מִשְׁפָּחָה ´ז sacrifice of the clan [1 S 20:29](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:29). † **4\.** *thank offering* תּוֺדָה(הַ) ´ז [Lv 7:12](https://ref.ly/Lev7:12), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Lev22:29), [Psalm 107:22](https://ref.ly/Ps107:22), [116:17](https://ref.ly/Ps116:17), which is given as קָרְבָּנוֺ ´ז, זִבְחוֺ, הַזָּבַח [Lv 7:16](https://ref.ly/Lev7:16), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Lev7:17); and as a variety of שְׁלָמִים [Lv 7:13](https://ref.ly/Lev7:13), [7:15](https://ref.ly/Lev7:15); it is implied in זִבְחֵי תְרוּעָה [Psalm 27:6](https://ref.ly/Ps27:6); yet זֶבַח is generic with תּוֺדָה [2 Ch 29:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:31), [29:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:31). **5\.** in ritual of H \& P זבחים are defined by שׁלמים (q. v.) [Lv 3:1](https://ref.ly/Lev3:1) \+ 39 t. Lv Nu, [1 S 10:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:8), [1 K 8:63](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:63), [2 Ch 30:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:22), [33:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:16), [Pr 7:14](https://ref.ly/Prov7:14), but sometimes (rarely) ´ז alone is used in this sense, with or without suffix, where the meaning is plain from the context: [Lv 17:5](https://ref.ly/Lev17:5), [17:7](https://ref.ly/Lev17:7), [17:8](https://ref.ly/Lev17:8), [19:6](https://ref.ly/Lev19:6), [23:37](https://ref.ly/Lev23:37) (H) [Nu 15:3](https://ref.ly/Num15:3), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Num15:5), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Num15:8), [Jos 22:26](https://ref.ly/Josh22:26), [22:29](https://ref.ly/Josh22:29) (all P). These ritual offerings are of three varieties תּוֺדָה, נֶדֶר and נְדָבָה [Lv 7:12](https://ref.ly/Lev7:12), [7:16](https://ref.ly/Lev7:16) (P); in the several lists זבחים are distinguished from תודות [Am 4:4](https://ref.ly/Amos4:4) f. [2 Ch 29:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:31), from נדרים [Nu 15:8](https://ref.ly/Num15:8) (P) [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6), [12:11](https://ref.ly/Deut12:11), [Jon 1:16](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:16), from נדבות [Am 4:4](https://ref.ly/Amos4:4) f., [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6) and from the more comprehensive שׁלמים [Nu 15:8](https://ref.ly/Num15:8), [Jos 22:27](https://ref.ly/Josh22:27) (P). So also the sacrifice at the institution of the covenant at Horeb [Ex 24:5](https://ref.ly/Exod24:5) (J E) and the sacrifice at the installation of Saul [1 S 11:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:5) are defined as † זבחים שׁלמים. Thus ´ז seems not only to be used for all these special forms but also to include other festal sacrifices not defined in the codes of law. The ritual was the same for the entire class. They were all sacrifices for feasts in which the flesh of the victim was eaten by the offerers, except so far as the officiating priests had certain choice pieces and the blood and fat pieces went to the altar for God. The sacrifice at the institution of the covenant at Horeb, the Passover victim, and the ram of consecration, were special, in that there was an application of a portion of the blood to the persons and things which were to be consecrated, [Ex 12:22](https://ref.ly/Exod12:22), [24:8](https://ref.ly/Exod24:8), [Lv 8:23](https://ref.ly/Lev8:23). † **6\.** the slaughter of hostile nations is also a זֶבַח offered by God Himself, in which the vultures devour the flesh of the victims [Is 34:6](https://ref.ly/Isa34:6), [Je 46:10](https://ref.ly/Jer46:10), Ez [39:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:17), [39:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:17), [39:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek39:19), [Zp 1:7](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:7), [1:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph1:8). **7\.** the verb used with זֶבַח is ordinarily זָבַח but others also are found e.g. † עָשָה [Nu 6:17](https://ref.ly/Num6:17), [15:3](https://ref.ly/Num15:3), [15:5](https://ref.ly/Num15:5), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Num15:8), [Jos 22:23](https://ref.ly/Josh22:23) (P) [1 K 12:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:27), [2 K 5:17](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:17), [10:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs10:24), [Je 33:18](https://ref.ly/Jer33:18) (cf. Sab. עסי דַבח Hal148 DHMZMG 1888, 374\); † הֵבִיא [Am 4:4](https://ref.ly/Amos4:4), [Dt 12:6](https://ref.ly/Deut12:6), [12:11](https://ref.ly/Deut12:11), [Je 17:26](https://ref.ly/Jer17:26), † הִגִּישׁ [Am 5:25](https://ref.ly/Amos5:25), † הִקְרִיב [Lv 7:11](https://ref.ly/Lev7:11), [7:16](https://ref.ly/Lev7:16), [22:21](https://ref.ly/Lev22:21) (all P), † עָבַד [Is 19:21](https://ref.ly/Isa19:21), † נָתַן [Ec 4:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:17), יַעֲלָה עֹלָה אוֺ זֶבַח [Lv 17:8](https://ref.ly/Lev17:8) (H), ´בִּשֵּׁל ז Ez [46:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek46:24), שָׁחַט אֶת־הָעוֺלָה וְהַזָּבַָח Ez [40:42](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:42) cf. [44:11](https://ref.ly/Ezek44:11).

> \[Str2078] † II. זֶ֫בַח **n.pr.m.** a Midianite king Ju [8:5](https://ref.ly/Judg8:5), [8:6](https://ref.ly/Judg8:6), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Judg8:7), [8:10](https://ref.ly/Judg8:10), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Judg8:12), [8:12](https://ref.ly/Judg8:12), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Judg8:15), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Judg8:15), [8:18](https://ref.ly/Judg8:18), [8:21](https://ref.ly/Judg8:21), [8:21](https://ref.ly/Judg8:21), [Psalm 83:12](https://ref.ly/Ps83:12), LXX Ζεβεε.

> \[Str4096] מִזְבֵּחַ:401 **n.m.**:[Jos 22:10](https://ref.ly/Josh22:10) altar (Ar. مَذْبَحٌ, *place of slaughter, trench made by torrent*, Sab. מדַבח DHMEpigr. Denkm. 24\); ´מ abs. [2 K 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:22) \+ 223 t., הַמִּזְבֵָּחָה [Ex 29:13](https://ref.ly/Exod29:13) \+ 31 t.; cstr. מִזְבַּח [Ex 20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24) \+ 76 t., sf. מִזְבְּחִי [Ex 20:26](https://ref.ly/Exod20:26) \+ 7 t., מִזְבַּחֲךָ [1 K 8:31](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:31) \+ 3 t., מִזְבְּחֶָךָ [Dt 33:10](https://ref.ly/Deut33:10); מִזְבְּחוֺ Ju [6:31](https://ref.ly/Judg6:31) \+ 2 t.; pl. מִזְבְּחֹת [Nu 23:1](https://ref.ly/Num23:1) \+ 5 t., מִזְבְּחוֺת [2 K 21:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:3) \+ 23 t., sf. מִזְבְּחוֺתֶיךָ [Psalm 84:4](https://ref.ly/Ps84:4), other sfs. 21 t.;—**1\.** JE narrate that altars were built by Noah after leaving the ark [Gn 8:20](https://ref.ly/Gen8:20); by Abraham at Shechem [12:7](https://ref.ly/Gen12:7), Bethel [12:8](https://ref.ly/Gen12:8), Hebron [13:18](https://ref.ly/Gen13:18), mt. in land Moriah [22:9](https://ref.ly/Gen22:9); by Issac at Beersheba [26:25](https://ref.ly/Gen26:25); by Jacob at Shechem [33:20](https://ref.ly/Gen33:20) (yet this perh. mistake for מַצֵּבָה, being obj. of וַיַּצֶּב־שָׁם, not elsewhere with מזבח, cf. also Di), at Bethel [35:7](https://ref.ly/Gen35:7), by Moses at Rephidim [Ex 17:15](https://ref.ly/Exod17:15), Horeb [24:4](https://ref.ly/Exod24:4), by Balak at Bamoth Baal, Pisgah, \& Peor [Nu 23:1](https://ref.ly/Num23:1), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Num23:14), [23:29](https://ref.ly/Num23:29); by Joshua on Mt. Ebal [Jos 8:30](https://ref.ly/Josh8:30): the prophetic histories narrate that altars were built by Gideon at Ophra Ju [6:24](https://ref.ly/Judg6:24); by a man of God at Bethel Ju [21:4](https://ref.ly/Judg21:4); by Samuel at Ramah [1 S 7:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:17); by Saul after Michmash [1 S 14:35](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:35); by David on the threshing floor of Ornan [2 S 24:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:25) \= [1 Ch 21:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:18), [1 Ch 22:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr22:1); that Solomon sacrificed on the altar at Gibeon [1 K 3:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:4) and built altars in the temple at Jerusalem [1 K 6:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:20), [8:64](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:64); that Jeroboam built an altar at Bethel [1 K 12:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:32) (which was destroyed by Josiah [2 K 23:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15)); and that Elijah repaired an ancient altar on Carmel [1 K 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:30). An altar in Egypt is predicted [Is 19:19](https://ref.ly/Isa19:19). All this accords with the law of the Covenant code [Ex 20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24)–26 which recognises a plurality of altars and prescribes that they shall be built of אֲדָמָה, or of unhewn stones, and without steps. So אֲבָנִים [Dt 27:6](https://ref.ly/Deut27:6) (JE), אֲבָנִים שְׁלֵמוֺת [Jos 8:31](https://ref.ly/Josh8:31), and twelve stones [1 K 18:30](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:30), [18:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:32); cf. [Is 27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9). The altar was also a place of refuge [Ex 21:24](https://ref.ly/Exod21:24) (JE) [1 K 1:50](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:50), [1:51](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:51), [2:28](https://ref.ly/1Kgs2:28). **2\.** D prescribes one central altar [Dt 12:5](https://ref.ly/Deut12:5) f. 27; but no attempt to enforce this principle appears before Hezekiah ([2 K 18:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:4), [18:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:22)), and Josiah, whose reform is more effectual, [2 K 23:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:8)–[20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:20). **3\.** P limits sacrifices to the altars of the tabernacle. A great altar was built E. of the Jordan, but it was according to P only as an עֵד after the pattern of the altar before the Tabernacle [Jos 22:10](https://ref.ly/Josh22:10)–34\. P describes two altars: **a.** the מִזְבַּח הָעֹלָה [Ex 30:28](https://ref.ly/Exod30:28), [31:9](https://ref.ly/Exod31:9), [35:16](https://ref.ly/Exod35:16), [38:1](https://ref.ly/Exod38:1), [40:6](https://ref.ly/Exod40:6), [40:10](https://ref.ly/Exod40:10), [40:29](https://ref.ly/Exod40:29), [Lv 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lev4:7), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Lev4:10), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Lev4:25), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Lev4:25), [4:30](https://ref.ly/Lev4:30), [4:34](https://ref.ly/Lev4:34) \= הַנְּחשֶׁת ´מ [Ex 38:30](https://ref.ly/Exod38:30), [39:39](https://ref.ly/Exod39:39), made of acacia wood plated with brass, 5\*5\*3 cubits in size, with four horns and a network of brass, on which all sacrifices by fire were made [Ex 27:1](https://ref.ly/Exod27:1)– [8](https://ref.ly/Exod27:8) [38:1](https://ref.ly/Exod38:1)–[7](https://ref.ly/Exod38:7); **b.** מִקְטַר קְטֹרֶת ´מ made of acacia wood plated with gold, 1\*1\*2 cubits in size, with four horns and a crown of gold [Ex 30:1](https://ref.ly/Exod30:1)–[6](https://ref.ly/Exod30:6) \= הקטרת ´מ [Ex 30:27](https://ref.ly/Exod30:27), [31:8](https://ref.ly/Exod31:8), [35:15](https://ref.ly/Exod35:15), [37:25](https://ref.ly/Exod37:25) \= הזהב ´מ [Ex 39:38](https://ref.ly/Exod39:38), [40:5](https://ref.ly/Exod40:5), [40:26](https://ref.ly/Exod40:26), [Nu 4:11](https://ref.ly/Num4:11) \= קטרת הַסַּמִּים ´מ [Lv 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lev4:7); these altars are named elsewhere only in Chr. [1 Ch 6:34](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:34), [16:40](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:40), [21:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:29), [2 Ch 1:5](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:5), [1:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:6). **4\.** Solomon made two altars for the temple at Jerusalem: **a.** הַנְּחשֶׁת ´מ for the court [1 K 8:64](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:64) (which was laid aside by Ahaz [2 K 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:14), [16:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:15), who made an altar after the model of one he saw in Damascus [2 K 16:11](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:11)); in v:[14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:14) ´הַמִּזְבַּח הַנּ (cstr. form c. art.) is erron., rd. prob. מִזְבַּח cf. Ges§ 127\. 4 a ad fin.; this altar, according to Chr, measured 20\*20\*10 cubits [2 Ch 4:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:1); this מזבח נחשׁת(ה) also [7:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:7) \& Ez [9:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:2); \= העולה ´מ [2 Ch 29:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:18), repaired by Asa [2 Ch 15:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr15:8); **b.** an altar of cedar, plated with gold, in the temple before the דְּבִיר [1 K 6:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:20), [6:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:22), [7:48](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:48); \= הזהב ´מ [2 Ch 4:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:19); \= הַקְּטֹרֶת ´מ [1 Ch 28:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:18), [2 Ch 26:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:16). **5\.** Ezekiel plans two altars for the new temple: **a.** a table of wood 3\*2 cubits Ez [41:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek41:22); **b.** an altar for the court, prob. same dimensions as **4 a**, with stairs Ez [43:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:13)–[17](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:17). **6\.** after the return Jeshua built an altar on the ancient site in the court of the temple [Ezr 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:2). **7\.** besides these altars used in the worship of Yahweh, Ahab built an altar to Baal in Samaria [1 K 16:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:32); Ahaz made an altar on the roof of the upper chamber, and other kings of Judah made altars in the two courts, all of which were destroyed by Josiah [2 K 23:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:12). **8\.** the ancient and most common term for making altars was בָּנָה [Gn 35:7](https://ref.ly/Gen35:7) (E) [8:20](https://ref.ly/Gen8:20) (J) [Dt 27:5](https://ref.ly/Deut27:5), [27:6](https://ref.ly/Deut27:6), [Ex 32:5](https://ref.ly/Exod32:5) (JE) \+, implying building material; other vbs. are הֵקִים [2 S 24:18](https://ref.ly/2Sam24:18), [1 K 16:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:32), [2 K 21:3](https://ref.ly/2Kgs21:3), [1 Ch 21:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr21:18), [2 Ch 33:3](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:3); הֵכִין [Ezr 3:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:3) (sq. עַל־מְכוֺנֹתָו֯); שִים [Je 11:13](https://ref.ly/Jer11:13); וַיַּצֶּ־ב [Gn 33:20](https://ref.ly/Gen33:20) (but cf. **1\.**); [Nu 23:4](https://ref.ly/Num23:4) (E) has עָרַח *arrange*, *prepare;* עָשָה [Gn 35:1](https://ref.ly/Gen35:1), [35:3](https://ref.ly/Gen35:3), [Ex 20:24](https://ref.ly/Exod20:24) (E) [Gn 13:4](https://ref.ly/Gen13:4) (J) Ju 22:28, [1 K 12:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:33), [18:26](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:26); this becomes usual in P, [Ex 27:1](https://ref.ly/Exod27:1), [30:1](https://ref.ly/Exod30:1), [37:25](https://ref.ly/Exod37:25), [38:1](https://ref.ly/Exod38:1), [38:30](https://ref.ly/Exod38:30), so [2 Ch 4:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:1), [4:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:19), [7:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:7), [28:24](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:24). **9\.** the dedication of the altar was חֲנֻכַּת המובהּ [Nu 7:10](https://ref.ly/Num7:10), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Num7:11), [7:84](https://ref.ly/Num7:84), [7:88](https://ref.ly/Num7:88), [2 Ch 7:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr7:9); it was anointed with oil מָשַׁח [Ex 40:10](https://ref.ly/Exod40:10), [Lv 8:11](https://ref.ly/Lev8:11), [Nu 7:10](https://ref.ly/Num7:10) (all P). **10\.** removal of unlawful altars is expr. by נָתַץ [Ex 34:13](https://ref.ly/Exod34:13) (JE) [Dt 7:5](https://ref.ly/Deut7:5), Ju [2:2](https://ref.ly/Judg2:2), [6:30](https://ref.ly/Judg6:30), [6:31](https://ref.ly/Judg6:31), [6:32](https://ref.ly/Judg6:32), [2 K 11:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs11:18), [23:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:12), [23:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:15); נִתַּץ (Pi) [Dt 12:3](https://ref.ly/Deut12:3), [2 Ch 31:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:1), [34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4), [34:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:7); נֻתַּץ Ju [6:28](https://ref.ly/Judg6:28); הָרַס Ju [6:25](https://ref.ly/Judg6:25), [1 K 19:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:10), [19:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:14); שִׁבַּר [2 Ch 23:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:17); הֵסִיר [2 K 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:22) \= [Is 36:7](https://ref.ly/Isa36:7), [2 Ch 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr14:2), [30:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:14), [32:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:12), [33:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr33:15). **11\.** ministry at the altar was ´מִשְׁמֶרֶת הַמּ [Nu 18:5](https://ref.ly/Num18:5), Ez [40:46](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:46), cf. [Nu 3:31](https://ref.ly/Num3:31) (P), ´מְשָֽׁרְתֵי מ [Jo 1:13](https://ref.ly/Joel1:13); עָמַד עַל is said of officiating priest [1 K 13:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:1), עָלָה עַל [Ex 20:26](https://ref.ly/Exod20:26), 1 S [2:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:28), [1 K 12:33](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:33) \+ (cf. יָרַד [Lv 9:22](https://ref.ly/Lev9:22)); sacrifices were usually offered עַל־הַמִּזְבֵּחַ [Gn 22:9](https://ref.ly/Gen22:9), [Dt 12:27](https://ref.ly/Deut12:27) \+; v. especially the phrases with זָרַק [Ex 29:16](https://ref.ly/Exod29:16), [29:20](https://ref.ly/Exod29:20) \+ (v. זרק); הִקְטִיר [Lv 4:10](https://ref.ly/Lev4:10) (v. קטר **Hiph.**); הֶעֱלָה [Lv 6:3](https://ref.ly/Lev6:3) \+ (v. עלה **Hiph.**) לְבַעֵר [Ne 10:35](https://ref.ly/Neh10:35); but בַּמִּזְבֵּחַ [Gn 8:20](https://ref.ly/Gen8:20) (J) [Nu 23:2](https://ref.ly/Num23:2), [23:4](https://ref.ly/Num23:4), [23:14](https://ref.ly/Num23:14), [23:30](https://ref.ly/Num23:30) (E); one touching the altar is ´הַנֹּגֵעַ בַּמּ [Ex 29:37](https://ref.ly/Exod29:37) (P); ´לפני המ of placing, or standing *before* altar [Dt 26:4](https://ref.ly/Deut26:4), [1 K 8:22](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:22); as **acc. loc.** הַמִּזְבֵּ֫חָה in phrases esp. c. הִקְטִיר [Ex 29:13](https://ref.ly/Exod29:13) \+, and הֶעֱלָה [Lv 14:20](https://ref.ly/Lev14:20). **12\.** the horns of the altar were especially for the application of the blood of the sin\-offering in the ritual; sin is represented as graven לְקַרְנוֺת מִזְבְּחוֺתֵיכֶם [Je 17:1](https://ref.ly/Jer17:1); therefore of blood for cleansing it was said † ´נָתַן על קרנות המ [Ex 29:12](https://ref.ly/Exod29:12), [Lv 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lev4:7), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Lev4:18), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Lev4:25), [4:30](https://ref.ly/Lev4:30), [4:34](https://ref.ly/Lev4:34), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Lev8:15), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Lev9:9), [16:18](https://ref.ly/Lev16:18) (all P). An ancient custom is referred to, [Psalm 118:27](https://ref.ly/Ps118:27) אִסְרו־חַג בַּעֲבֹתִָים עַד־קרנות המזבח *bind the festal victim with cords, unto the horns of the altar:* Ainsworth paraphrases: ‘ that is, all the court over, untill you come even to the hornes of the altar, intending hereby many sacrifices, ’ so De; but Che ‘ bind the procession with branches, (step on) to the altar\-horns, ’ that is in sacred procession round the altar. **13\.** the יְסוֺד (q.v.) is also referred to in the ritual of the sin\-offerings in the phrases יָצַק אל יסוד [Lv 8:15](https://ref.ly/Lev8:15), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Lev9:9) \& שָׁפַךְ אל יסוד [Ex 29:12](https://ref.ly/Exod29:12), [Lv 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lev4:7), [4:18](https://ref.ly/Lev4:18), [4:25](https://ref.ly/Lev4:25), [4:30](https://ref.ly/Lev4:30), [4:34](https://ref.ly/Lev4:34) (all P) יִמָּחֵה אל יסוד [Lv 5:9](https://ref.ly/Lev5:9). **14\.** the altar was קֹדֶשׁ קָדָשִׁים [Ex 29:37](https://ref.ly/Exod29:37), [40:10](https://ref.ly/Exod40:10) (P); but repeated sin\-offerings were necessary to keep it pure and cleanse it from the pollution of the people in whose midst it was situated. This is expressed by חִטֵּא [Lv 8:15](https://ref.ly/Lev8:15) (P) Ez [43:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:22); כִּפֶּר [Lv 16:20](https://ref.ly/Lev16:20), [16:33](https://ref.ly/Lev16:33) (P) Ez [43:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:26); קִדַּשׁ [Ex 29:44](https://ref.ly/Exod29:44), [40:10](https://ref.ly/Exod40:10), [Lv 8:15](https://ref.ly/Lev8:15), [Nu 7:1](https://ref.ly/Num7:1) (P); טִהַר [2 Ch 29:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:18).

> \[Str2079] זַבַּי v. sub זבב. p. 256

## זָבַל (id: BDB02536)

\[Str2082] † \[זָבַל] **vb.** prob. exalt, honour (As. *zabâlu* COTGloss and id. on [Gn 30:20](https://ref.ly/Gen30:20), [1 K 8:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:13), cf. DlPr 62 f. so De; Ph. n.pr.f. בעלאזבל (prob. \= *Baal has exalted*) cf. אִיזֶבֶל p. 33 supr.; cf. Ar. زَبَلَ *take up and carry;* \> NöZMG 1886, 729, who connects these vbs. with Syr. ܣܒܰܠ *bear, carry*, cf. Heb. סָבַל)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִזְבְּלֵנִי אִישִׁי [Gn 30:20](https://ref.ly/Gen30:20) b (J), *my husband will honour me* (in expl. of name Zebulun (v. infr.); on another appar. expl. in va cf. זבד); \> AV al. *dwell with me*, cf. Thes RobGes who derive *dwell with* fr. an assumed mng. *be round* (cf. דור); and others (cf. MV8. 9\), who derive *dwell* as denom. from זְבֻל in sense *dwelling*.

> \[Str2073] † I. זְבֻל **n.\[m.]** elevation, height, lofty abode (NH זְבוּל *temple*);—בָּנוֺ בָּנִיתִי בֵּית זְבֻל לָךְ [1 K 8:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:13) \= [2 Ch 6:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:2) (poet.; prob. from book of Jashar, cf. LXX WeComp. 271 DrIntr.. 182; on בֵּית־זְבֻל \= As. *bît zabal, lofty house* cf. COT [1 K 8:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:13) DlPr 62 f.); לְבַלּוֺת שְׁאוֺל מִזְּבֻל לוֺ [Psalm 49:15](https://ref.ly/Ps49:15) text dub.; Ew Hi Ri Now rd. מַזְבּוּל (or מִזְבּוֺל, Ew) as n. \= *dwelling;* Che prop. for מִזְּבֻל, ´ם ז\[לְעוֺלָ] Checrit. n.; זְבֻל קָדְשְׁךָ [Is 63:15](https://ref.ly/Isa63:15) (‖שָׁמַיִם) *the high abode of thy holiness* (cf. Checrit. n,); of the lofty abode of sun and moon: שֶׁמֶשׁ יָרֵחַ עָמַד זְבָֻלָה Hb [3:11](https://ref.ly/Hab3:11).

## זוג (id: BDB02541)

זוג, זיג (appar. √ of foll.; Targum NH זוג, זִיג is *be clear, bright transparent* (Ch WB Jastr391 a); others (so Thes) derive foll. from √ זגג with same meaning, v. infr. \& F1 NHWBi. 500 b).

> \[Str2085] † זָג **n.\[m.]** name of some comparatively insignif. product of vine (גֶּפֶן הַיַּיִן), the eating of which was included in prohibition to Nazi\-rite; skin of grape, so Thes (after Onk Mishna Maaser. 5:§ 4, Orla 1:§ 8, cf. Sifre *ad loc.*): מֵחַרְצַנִּים וְעַד־זָג֗ לאֹ יאֹכֵָל [Nu 6:4](https://ref.ly/Num6:4) (NH זג, זוג; so called as *transparent ?* cf. Aram. חְגוּגִיתָא ܙܰܓܽܘܓܺܝܬܳܐ *glass*, NH זַגָּג *glazier* (Ar. زُجَاجٌ *glass*, is a loan\-wd. acc. to Frä64); cf. theory of √ in LagGes. Ath. 41, Fl1. c.: but in Heb. *glass* is זְכוֺכִית).

> \[Str2086–88] זֶד, זָדוֺן v. sub זיד. p. 267–68

* **Associated Passages:** Numbers 6:4


## זֶה (id: BDB02544)

\[Str2063] זֶה **demonstr. pron.** and **adv.;** fem. זאֹת, once זאֹתָה, [Jer 26:6](https://ref.ly/Jer26:6) Kt (also זֹה and זוֺ, q.v.); comm. זוּ (q.v.): this, here (the element ז \= د \= ܕ is widely diffused in the Semitic languages, as a demonstr. particle, often acquiring, like Engl. *that*, Germ. *der, die, das*, the force of a relative. Thus **a.** Ph. זֶ *this* (e.g. קבר ז *this grave*), also sts. זן, f. זא, comm. אז (v. CIS I. i. 1:4, 1:5, 1:6, 1:12, 44:1, 88:2 etc.); Aram. of Nineveh, Babylon, Têma, Egypt, זי as mark of the genit. \[lit. *that of*] (CIS II. i. 1, 2, 3, 4, etc., 65, 69–71, 113, 114, 141, 142, etc.), זנה *this* (*ib.* 113:22 145 C2), f. זא 113:15 145 B5; Aram. of Zinjirli ז, זן, זנה *this*, זי *which* (DHMSendsch. 56\); Eth. ዝ፡ *ze, this*, f. ዝኣ፡ *zā*, ዘ፡ *za, who* (masc.), also (like זִי, and דִּי, דְּ \[v. infr.]) in common use as a mark of the genitive; **b.** Arab. ܕ\-ܐ *this*, fem. دِي, دُو (gen. سِي, acc. دَاء, fem. ذَاتُ), *possessor of* (lit. *that of…*, i.e. one who owns), or in the Ṭayyite dialect, *who, which:* from د َاء, with هَا *lo!* prefixed, هَادَاء *this*, f. هَدِهِ, with the pron. element *\-k* affixed, ذَاكَ \= *this:* with *’al* (\= the art.) \+ a demonstr. element *la* prefixed, أَلَّدِي *who, which* (in origin \= Heb. הַלָּזֶה *this*, q.v.); Sab. דַן *this*, f. דַת, דַ *who, which*, f. דַת (PräZMG. 1872, 419, DHMZMG. 1883, 338 f., alsoEpigr. Denkm. 65\); **c.** B Aram. דִּי *which*, דֵּךְ, דִּכֵּן, דְּנָה (q.v.) *this*, f. דָּא (\= זאֹת), דָּךְ; Palm. and Nab. די *which*, דנה *this;* Targum דְּ *which*, דֵּין, דֵּיכֵּי *this*, f. דָּא; with הא *lo!* prefixed, הָדֵין, הָדָא (Syr. ܗܳܢ, ܗܳܢܳܐ, contr. from ܗܳܕܶܐ), هدا; Syr. ܕ *who, which;* Sam. ࠃ *who, which*, ࠃࠍ *this*, f. ࠃࠄ Mand. ד *who, which*, דהַ *this* (rare), more commonly האדין, האזין (NöMand § 80, 81\). Alike in Heb. and the other dialects, the corresp. plur. is derived from a different source: Heb. אֵלָּה, Ph. אל, Eth. *’ellū, ’ellā, these, ’ella, who*, Arab. أُولَى, *these*, أُولُوا, أُولَى *owners of*, Sab. אלן *these*, אל, אלי *who, which*, Aram. אִלֵּין, with הא *lo!* prefixed, ܗܳܠܶܝܢ, Sam. ࠀࠋࠉࠍ, Mand. עלין. Only the Arab. أَلَّدِي forms its own pl. اَلَّذِينَ. V. further WSG 107 ff.)—**1\.** standing alone: **a.** *this one*, sts. contemptuously (esp. with אֵת), [Gn 2:23](https://ref.ly/Gen2:23) לָזאֹת יִקָּרֵא אִשָּׁה כִּי מֵאִישׁ לֻקֳּחָה־זּאֹת, [5:29](https://ref.ly/Gen5:29) …זֶה יְנַחֲמֵנוּ, [12:12](https://ref.ly/Gen12:12), [38:28](https://ref.ly/Gen38:28), [Ex 10:7](https://ref.ly/Exod10:7) עַד מָתַי יִהְיֶה זֶה לָנוּ לְמוֺקֵשׁ, [1 S 10:27](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:27) מַה יּוֺשִׁיעֵנוּ זֶה how shall *this man* save us ? [16:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:8), [16:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam16:9), [21:16](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:15) that ye have brought אֶת־זֶה *this fellow* to play the madman against me ? [25:21](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:21) in vain have I kept אֶת־כָּל־אֲשֶׁר לָזֶה all that *this fellow* hath etc. [2 S 13:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:17) אֶת־זאֹת, [1 K 22:27](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:27) אֶת־זֶה, [Mi 5:4](https://ref.ly/Mic5:4), [Is 66:2](https://ref.ly/Isa66:2); as a genit., [Gn 29:27](https://ref.ly/Gen29:27), [29:28](https://ref.ly/Gen29:28), [1 K 21:2](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:2); with a *collective* force [Lv 11:4](https://ref.ly/Lev11:4); [11:9](https://ref.ly/Lev11:9) (\= [Dt 14:7](https://ref.ly/Deut14:7), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Deut14:9)), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Lev11:21), v:[29](https://ref.ly/Lev11:29), Ju [20:16](https://ref.ly/Judg20:16), [20:17](https://ref.ly/Judg20:17) (כָּל־זֶה), [Jb 19:19](https://ref.ly/Job19:19) (with a *plural* verb). In a purely *neuter* sense (of an act, event, announcement, etc.) זאת is most common, as with עשה to do *this* [Gn 3:14](https://ref.ly/Gen3:14), [20:5](https://ref.ly/Gen20:5), [20:6](https://ref.ly/Gen20:6), [45:19](https://ref.ly/Gen45:19) and oft., שׁמעו זאת [Am 8:4](https://ref.ly/Amos8:4), [Is 47:8](https://ref.ly/Isa47:8), [48:1](https://ref.ly/Isa48:1), [48:16](https://ref.ly/Isa48:16), [51:21](https://ref.ly/Isa51:21) al.; [Gn 41:39](https://ref.ly/Gen41:39), [Ex 17:14](https://ref.ly/Exod17:14), [Dt 32:6](https://ref.ly/Deut32:6), Ju [7:14](https://ref.ly/Judg7:14), [21:3](https://ref.ly/Judg21:3) הָֽיְתָה זאֹת…לָמָה (cf. [Jb 1:13](https://ref.ly/Job1:13), [Psalm 118:23](https://ref.ly/Ps118:23)) [1 K 11:39](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:39), [2 S 7:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:19), [2 K 3:18](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:18) \+; אֵין זאֹת [1 S 20:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:2) b cf. [Am 2:11](https://ref.ly/Amos2:11) rarely so with אֶ־ת † [Jos 22:24](https://ref.ly/Josh22:24), [Je 9:11](https://ref.ly/Jer9:11); [Psalm 92:7](https://ref.ly/Ps92:7); as an accus. of limit., *as regards this, in this*, Ez [20:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:27), [36:37](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:37), [Jb 19:26](https://ref.ly/Job19:26) (si vera l.), [33:12](https://ref.ly/Job33:12) *in this* thou art not just; (late) אַחֲרֵי זאֹת † [Jb 42:16](https://ref.ly/Job42:16), [Ezr 9:10](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:10), כל־זאת ´א † [2 Ch 21:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr21:18), [35:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:20); pointing forward to a foll. clause, [Gn 42:18](https://ref.ly/Gen42:18), [43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11), [Nu 8:24](https://ref.ly/Num8:24), [14:35](https://ref.ly/Num14:35), [Is 56:2](https://ref.ly/Isa56:2), [Jb 10:13](https://ref.ly/Job10:13) etc.; [Ex 9:16](https://ref.ly/Exod9:16), [1 S 25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31), [Is 1:12](https://ref.ly/Isa1:12) מִי דִקֵּשׁ זאת מִיֶּדְכֶם :רְמֹס חֲצֵרָֽי; to one introduced by כִּי [2 S 19:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:22), [Psalm 102:19](https://ref.ly/Ps102:19), [119:50](https://ref.ly/Ps119:50), [119:56](https://ref.ly/Ps119:56) (see also בְּזאֹת **6 b** β): but זֶה also sts. occurs similarly [Ex 13:8](https://ref.ly/Exod13:8), [Pr 24:12](https://ref.ly/Prov24:12), [Jb 15:17](https://ref.ly/Job15:17), [Ne 2:2](https://ref.ly/Neh2:2), [Ec 1:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:17), [2:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:15) גם זה הבל (so v:[19](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:19), v:[21](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:21), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:23) \+), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:18), [7:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:18); of a concrete obj., [Ex 30:31](https://ref.ly/Exod30:31), [2 K 4:43](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:43) מָה אֶתֵּן זֶה לִפְנֵי מֵאָה אִישׁ: in late Heb., [Ne 13:6](https://ref.ly/Neh13:6) בְּכָל־זֶה during all this, [2 Ch 32:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:9) אַחַר זֶה; (See also below, **6 g.**) **b.** repeated זֶה…זֶה, *this…that, the one…the other* or (if indefinite) *another*, [Gn 29:27](https://ref.ly/Gen29:27) (זאֹת), [Ex 14:20](https://ref.ly/Exod14:20) וְלאֹ קָרַב זֶה אֶל זֶה, [1 K 3:23](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:23), [22:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:20), [Is 6:3](https://ref.ly/Isa6:3), [44:5](https://ref.ly/Isa44:5) (3 t.), [Psalm 75:8](https://ref.ly/Ps75:8) זֶה יַשְׁפִּיל וְזֶה יָרִים, [Jb 1:16–18](https://ref.ly/Job1:16-Job1:18), [21:23](https://ref.ly/Job21:23), [21:25](https://ref.ly/Job21:25) \+.

 **2\.** In appos. to a subst.: **a.** preceding it (rare) [Ex 32:1](https://ref.ly/Exod32:1), [32:23](https://ref.ly/Exod32:23) זֶה משֶׁה, Ju [5:5](https://ref.ly/Judg5:5) (\= [Psalm 68:9](https://ref.ly/Ps68:9)) זֶה סִינַי *this* Sinai, [Jos 9:12](https://ref.ly/Josh9:12) זֶה לַחְמֵנוּ *this* our bread, [1 K 14:14](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:14), [2 K 6:33](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:33), [Is 23:13](https://ref.ly/Isa23:13) זֶה הָעָם, [Psalm 34:7](https://ref.ly/Ps34:7), [49:14](https://ref.ly/Ps49:14), [104:25](https://ref.ly/Ps104:25) ´זה הים גדול וג *this* sea *there* is great and broad, [118:20](https://ref.ly/Ps118:20), Ct [7:8](https://ref.ly/Song7:8), [Ezr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:12). (Cf. זוּ Hb [1:11](https://ref.ly/Hab1:11).) **b.** following it:—*(a)* when the subst. is determined by a pron. affix, [Gn 24:8](https://ref.ly/Gen24:8) שְׁבוּעָתִי זאֹת, *this* my oath, [Dt 5:16](https://ref.ly/Deut5:16) O that לְבָבָם זֶה *this their* *heart* (their present temper) might continue always! [21:20](https://ref.ly/Deut21:20), [Jos 2:14](https://ref.ly/Josh2:14), [2:20](https://ref.ly/Josh2:20) דְּבָרֵנוּ זֶה, Ju [6:14](https://ref.ly/Judg6:14) go בְּכֹחֲךָ זֶה in *this* thy strength, [Dn 10:17](https://ref.ly/Dan10:17), [2 Ch 24:18](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:18). (Cf. אֵלֶּה, **a.**) Rarely when it is undetermined, [2 K 1:2](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:2) חֳלִי זֶה (so [8:8](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:8), [8:9](https://ref.ly/2Kgs8:9)), [Psalm 80:15](https://ref.ly/Ps80:15) (β) with the art., הַזֶּה, הַזּאֹת, after a subst. determined also by the art., [Gn 7:1](https://ref.ly/Gen7:1) בַּדּוֺר הַזֶּה, v:[13](https://ref.ly/Gen7:13) הַיּוֺם הַזֶּה, [12:7](https://ref.ly/Gen12:7) הָאָרֶץ הַזּאֹת, [15:7](https://ref.ly/Gen15:7), [17:21](https://ref.ly/Gen17:21), [17:23](https://ref.ly/Gen17:23), [Dt 4:6](https://ref.ly/Deut4:6) הגוי הגדול הזה, and continually. Not however after a n.pr., except such as are construed with the art., as הַיַּרְדֵּן הַזֶּה [Gn 32:11](https://ref.ly/Gen32:11), [Dt 3:27](https://ref.ly/Deut3:27), [31:2](https://ref.ly/Deut31:2), [Jos 1:2](https://ref.ly/Josh1:2), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Josh1:11), [4:22](https://ref.ly/Josh4:22), הַלְּבָנוֺן הַזֶּה [Jos 1:4](https://ref.ly/Josh1:4), cf. [Nu 27:12](https://ref.ly/Num27:12) \= [Dt 32:49](https://ref.ly/Deut32:49) ([2 K 5:20](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:20) הַזֶּה belongs to הָאֲרַמִּי): and hardly ever ([2 Ch 1:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr1:10) after a noun with a pron. affix ([Jos 2:17](https://ref.ly/Josh2:17) מִשְּׁבֻעָתֵךְ הַזֶּה the gender of הזה shews that the text is in error: v. Dr§ 209 *Obs.*). **3\.** More oft. as pred., as [1 S 24:17](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:17) הֲקוֺלְךָ זֶה, [2 K 3:23](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:23) דָּם זֶה *this* is blood, [9:37](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:37) :זאֹת אִיזָֽבֶל, Ez [5:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:5) etc.; הֲזֶה [Is 14:16](https://ref.ly/Isa14:16), הֲזאֹת [23:7](https://ref.ly/Isa23:7), [La 2:15](https://ref.ly/Lam2:15); oft. at the beginning or close of enumerations (esp. P), descriptions, injunctions, etc., as [Gn 5:1](https://ref.ly/Gen5:1) זה ספר *this* is the book…, [9:12](https://ref.ly/Gen9:12) זאֹת אוֺת הַבְּרִית, [17:10](https://ref.ly/Gen17:10), [20:13](https://ref.ly/Gen20:13) זֶה חַסְדֵּךְ אֲשֶׁר תַּעֲשִי עִמָּדִי, [Lv 6:2](https://ref.ly/Lev6:2), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Lev6:13), [6:18](https://ref.ly/Lev6:18), [7:1](https://ref.ly/Lev7:1), [7:11](https://ref.ly/Lev7:11), [Dt 6:1](https://ref.ly/Deut6:1), [33:1](https://ref.ly/Deut33:1), [Jos 13:2](https://ref.ly/Josh13:2), [Jb 27:13](https://ref.ly/Job27:13), [Is 58:6](https://ref.ly/Isa58:6) etc.; …זה הדבר אשׁר [Ex 16:16](https://ref.ly/Exod16:16), [35:4](https://ref.ly/Exod35:4), [Jos 5:4](https://ref.ly/Josh5:4), Ju [20:9](https://ref.ly/Judg20:9), [21:11](https://ref.ly/Judg21:11), [Is 27:22](https://ref.ly/Isa27:22), [Je 38:21](https://ref.ly/Jer38:21) \+, cf. …זה אשׁר *this* is what (*or* how)…[Gn 6:15](https://ref.ly/Gen6:15), [Ex 29:38](https://ref.ly/Exod29:38), [Je 33:16](https://ref.ly/Jer33:16); זֶה לְךָ הָאוֺת [Ex 3:12](https://ref.ly/Exod3:12), [1 S 2:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:34) \+; at the end, [Lv 7:37](https://ref.ly/Lev7:37), [11:46](https://ref.ly/Lev11:46), [13:50](https://ref.ly/Lev13:50), [Nu 5:21](https://ref.ly/Num5:21), [7:17](https://ref.ly/Num7:17), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Num7:23) etc., [Jos 19:8](https://ref.ly/Josh19:8), [19:16](https://ref.ly/Josh19:16), [19:23](https://ref.ly/Josh19:23) etc., [Is 14:26](https://ref.ly/Isa14:26), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Isa16:13) (…זֶה הַדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר), [17:14](https://ref.ly/Isa17:14), [54:17](https://ref.ly/Isa54:17), [Je 13:25](https://ref.ly/Jer13:25), [Psalm 109:20](https://ref.ly/Ps109:20), Ct [5:16](https://ref.ly/Song5:16), [5:16](https://ref.ly/Song5:16), [Jb 5:27](https://ref.ly/Job5:27), in the latter case sts. with the force of *such* (i.e. such as has been described), [18:21](https://ref.ly/Job18:21), [20:29](https://ref.ly/Job20:29) (cf. אֵלֶּה [Psalm 73:12](https://ref.ly/Ps73:12)), [Psalm 24:6](https://ref.ly/Ps24:6), [48:15](https://ref.ly/Ps48:14) *such* (\= such a one) is God, our God, for ever (Hi De) [Zp 2:15](https://ref.ly/Zeph2:15); cf. [Jb 14:3](https://ref.ly/Job14:3) אַף־עַל־זֶה upon one *such as this* (v:[1](https://ref.ly/Job14:1), v:[2](https://ref.ly/Job14:2)) dost thou open thy eyes? **4\.** It is attached enclitically, almost as an adv., to certain words, esp. interrog. pronouns, to impart, in a manner often not reproducible in Engl. idiom, directness and force, bringing the question or statement made into close relation with the speaker. (Contrast from this point of view מי זה and מי הוא: v. הוא **4 b.** Thus **a.** אֵי־זֶה:15 where, *then ?* [Jb 28:12](https://ref.ly/Job28:12) אֵי־זֶה מְקוֺם בִּינָה, v:[20](https://ref.ly/Job28:20), [38:19](https://ref.ly/Job38:19) b (v. other exx. sub אַי **1 b,** p. 32\). **b.** מִי־זֶה, † [1 S 17:55](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:55), [17:56](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:56) בֶּן־מִי־זֶה֭ :הָעָֽלֶם the son of *whom, here*, is the lad ? [Je 49:19](https://ref.ly/Jer49:19) (\= [50:44](https://ref.ly/Jer50:44)) ´וּמִי זֶה רֹעֶה אֲשֶׁר וג and *who* (emph.) is the shepherd that etc., [La 3:37](https://ref.ly/Lam3:37), [Psalm 24:8](https://ref.ly/Ps24:8) *Who* is the king of glory ? (for which in v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ps24:10) the stronger מִי הוּא זֶה is said: so [Je 30:21](https://ref.ly/Jer30:21), [Est 7:5](https://ref.ly/Esth7:5)), [25:12](https://ref.ly/Ps25:12). Elsewhere, the rend. *Who* is *this…* is admissible: [Is 63:1](https://ref.ly/Isa63:1), [Je 46:7](https://ref.ly/Jer46:7), [Jb 38:2](https://ref.ly/Job38:2), [42:3](https://ref.ly/Job42:3); cf. מִי־זאֹת Ct [3:6](https://ref.ly/Song3:6), [6:10](https://ref.ly/Song6:10), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5). **c.** מַה־זֶה *how, now ?* [Gn 27:20](https://ref.ly/Gen27:20), Ju [18:24](https://ref.ly/Judg18:24) וּמַה־זֶּה תּאֹמְרוּ אֵלַי and *how, now*, do ye say to me…? [1 K 21:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:5), [2 K 1:5](https://ref.ly/2Kgs1:5); *what, now ?* (τίποτε;) [1 S 10:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam10:11) מַה־זֶּה הָיָה לְבֶן־קִישׁ; עַל־מַהאזֶּה *for what ?* [Ne 2:4](https://ref.ly/Neh2:4): contracted into מַזֶּה [Ex 4:2](https://ref.ly/Exod4:2). (Cf. Aram. מָדֵין *why ?* ܡܳܕܶܝܢ used as aconj. *therefore*, e.g. [2 S 18:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam18:22) SyrVer, PS2013: both formed similarly.) **d.** מַה־זּאֹת in the phrase (עֲשִיתֶם, עָשִית) מַה־זּאֹת עָשִיתָ [Gn 3:13](https://ref.ly/Gen3:13), [12:18](https://ref.ly/Gen12:18), [26:10](https://ref.ly/Gen26:10), [29:25](https://ref.ly/Gen29:25), [42:28](https://ref.ly/Gen42:28), [Ex 14:11](https://ref.ly/Exod14:11), Ju [2:2](https://ref.ly/Judg2:2), [15:11](https://ref.ly/Judg15:11) \+. Either *What, now*, hast thou (have ye) done ? (Fl De) or *what* is *this* (that) thou hast (ye have) done ? (The Arab. grammarians dispute on the precise construction of the corresp. phrase in Arabic مَا ذَا صَنَعْتَ; De:[Gen. 3:13](https://ref.ly/Gen3:13) (ed. 4\), and esp. FlKl Schr. i. 356 Lanep. 948\.) **e.** לָמָּֽה־זֶּה *wherefore, now ?* [Gn 18:13](https://ref.ly/Gen18:13), [25:22](https://ref.ly/Gen25:22), [32:30](https://ref.ly/Gen32:30), [Ex 5:22](https://ref.ly/Exod5:22), [Nu 11:20](https://ref.ly/Num11:20) \+ oft. Cf. Arab. لِمَاء دَْ **f.** הַאַתָּה זֶה † [2 S 2:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam2:20) האתה זה עשהאל Art thou Asahel ? [1 K 18:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:7), [18:17](https://ref.ly/1Kgs18:17), without הֲ [Gn 27:24](https://ref.ly/Gen27:24); in an indirect question [Gn 27:21](https://ref.ly/Gen27:21). **g.** הִנֵּה זֶה *behold, here…* † [1 K 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs19:5) (in narrative), [Is 21:9](https://ref.ly/Isa21:9), Ct [2:8](https://ref.ly/Song2:8), [2:9](https://ref.ly/Song2:9). Cf. [Nu 13:17](https://ref.ly/Num13:17) (unusual) עֲלוּ זֶה בַּנֶּגֶבּ go up *here* in the South. **h.** עַתָּה זֶה, † [1 K 17:24](https://ref.ly/1Kgs17:24) עַתָּה זֶה יָדַעְתִּי *now* I know that…, [2 K 5:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:22) *just now.* Also **i.** prefixed to expressions denoting a period of time: [Gn 27:36](https://ref.ly/Gen27:36) he hath supplanted me זֶה פַעֲמַיִם *now* two times (so [43:10](https://ref.ly/Gen43:10)), [31:38](https://ref.ly/Gen31:38) *now, already* twenty years, v:[41](https://ref.ly/Gen31:41) (…זֶה־לִּי, [45:6](https://ref.ly/Gen45:6), [Nu 14:22](https://ref.ly/Num14:22) *now* ten times, [22:28](https://ref.ly/Num22:28), [22:32](https://ref.ly/Num22:32) זֶה שָׁלשׁ רְגָלִים, [Dt 8:2](https://ref.ly/Deut8:2), [8:4](https://ref.ly/Deut8:4), [Jos 22:3](https://ref.ly/Josh22:3) זֶה יָמִים רַבִּים, [2 S 14:2](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:2) \+; [Zc 7:3](https://ref.ly/Zech7:3) זֶה כַּמָּה שָׁנִים *already* how many years! cf. [Ru 2:7](https://ref.ly/Ruth2:7). **5\.** In poetry, as a *relative* pron. (rare: but see also זוֺ, זוּ): Psalm [74:2](https://ref.ly/Ps74:2) the hill of Zion זה שׁכנת בּוֺ *wherein* thou dwellest, [78:54](https://ref.ly/Ps78:54), [104:8](https://ref.ly/Ps104:8) אל־מְקום זה to the place (*st. c.* Ges§ 130\. 3\) *which…* v:[26](https://ref.ly/Ps104:26), [Pr 23:22](https://ref.ly/Prov23:22), [Is 25:9](https://ref.ly/Isa25:9); \= *that which* [Jb 15:17](https://ref.ly/Job15:17), *those who* [19:19](https://ref.ly/Job19:19) (so once, [Ex 13:8](https://ref.ly/Exod13:8), even in prose). In some of the passages cited the punctuators, by coupling זה with the preceding subst., and separating it from what follows by a disj. accent (as הַר־זֶ֝ה), appear not to have recognised its relative sense, but to have construed, *‘this* mountain, (which) thy right hand,’ etc. **6\.** With prefixes (in special senses):—**a.** בָּזֶה:15 *in this* (place), *here*, [Gn 38:21](https://ref.ly/Gen38:21), [38:22](https://ref.ly/Gen38:22), [Ex 24:14](https://ref.ly/Exod24:14), [Nu 23:1](https://ref.ly/Num23:1), [23:1](https://ref.ly/Num23:1), [1 S 1:26](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:26), [9:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam9:11) \+; of time, *then*, † [Est 2:13](https://ref.ly/Esth2:13). Once בַּזֶה [1 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:10). **b.** בְּזאֹת *(a) with this \= on these conditions*, [Gn 34:15](https://ref.ly/Gen34:15), [34:22](https://ref.ly/Gen34:22), [1 S 11:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam11:2), [Is 27:9](https://ref.ly/Isa27:9); \= *herewith, thus provided* [Lv 16:3](https://ref.ly/Lev16:3). (β) *by* or *through this* (esp. with יָדַע), [Gn 42:15](https://ref.ly/Gen42:15), [42:33](https://ref.ly/Gen42:33), [Ex 7:17](https://ref.ly/Exod7:17), [Nu 16:28](https://ref.ly/Num16:28), [Jos 3:10](https://ref.ly/Josh3:10), [Psalm 41:12](https://ref.ly/Ps41:12); so בָּזאֹת [Mal 3:10](https://ref.ly/Mal3:10). (γ) *in spite of this*, [Lv 26:27](https://ref.ly/Lev26:27), [Psalm 27:3](https://ref.ly/Ps27:3), for which the fuller בְּכָל־זאֹת occurs, [Is 5:25](https://ref.ly/Isa5:25), [9:11](https://ref.ly/Isa9:11), [9:16](https://ref.ly/Isa9:16), [9:20](https://ref.ly/Isa9:20), [10:4](https://ref.ly/Isa10:4), [Psalm 78:32](https://ref.ly/Ps78:32), [Je 3:10](https://ref.ly/Jer3:10), [Ho 7:10](https://ref.ly/Hos7:10). (δ) בָּזאֹת *for this cause* (late style) † [1 Ch 27:24](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:24), [2 Ch 19:2](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:2); *in this matter* [20:17](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:17); **c.** כָּזֶה (*a*) † [Gn 41:38](https://ref.ly/Gen41:38) …הֲנִמְצָא כָזֶה אִישׁ אשׁר, [Is 56:12](https://ref.ly/Isa56:12), [58:5](https://ref.ly/Isa58:5), [Je 5:9](https://ref.ly/Jer5:9) גּוֺי אֲשֶׁר כָּזֶה (so v:[29](https://ref.ly/Jer5:29), [9:8](https://ref.ly/Jer9:8)). (β) כָּזֹה וְכָזֶה *thus and thus*, † Ju [18:4](https://ref.ly/Judg18:4), [2 S 11:25](https://ref.ly/2Sam11:25), [1 K 14:5](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:5). **d.** כְּזאֹת *the like of this* \= *as follows*, † [Gn 45:23](https://ref.ly/Gen45:23); כָּזאֹת *the like of this* \= *things such as these* Ju [13:23](https://ref.ly/Judg13:23) (c. השׁמיע), [15:7](https://ref.ly/Judg15:7), [19:30](https://ref.ly/Judg19:30) לא נראתה כָּזאֹת, [1 S 4:7](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:7) (cf. [Je 2:10](https://ref.ly/Jer2:10), [2 Ch 30:26](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:26)), [2 S 14:13](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:13) (c. חשׁב), [Is 66:8](https://ref.ly/Isa66:8) (c. שׁמע), [Ezr 7:27](https://ref.ly/Ezra7:27); \= *accordingly, to that effect* (c. דִּבֶּר) [2 Ch 34:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:22); \= *in like manner* Ju [8:8](https://ref.ly/Judg8:8); \= *thus* (as has been described) [1 K 7:37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:37), [1 Ch 29:14](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:14), [2 Ch 31:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:20), [32:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:15): כָּזאֹת וְכָזאֹת *thus and thus* † [Jos 7:20](https://ref.ly/Josh7:20), [2 S 17:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:15), [17:15](https://ref.ly/2Sam17:15), [2 K 5:4](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:4), [9:12](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:12). **e.** מִזֶּה *from here, hence* [Gn 37:17](https://ref.ly/Gen37:17), [42:15](https://ref.ly/Gen42:15), [50:25](https://ref.ly/Gen50:25), [Ex 11:1](https://ref.ly/Exod11:1), [11:1](https://ref.ly/Exod11:1), [13:3](https://ref.ly/Exod13:3), [Dt 9:12](https://ref.ly/Deut9:12) \+ oft.: מִזֶּה…מִזֶּה *on one side…on the other side* [Ex 17:12](https://ref.ly/Exod17:12) מזה אחד ומזה אחד, [25:19](https://ref.ly/Exod25:19), [26:13](https://ref.ly/Exod26:13), [32:15](https://ref.ly/Exod32:15) מזה ומזה הם כתובים, [Nu 22:24](https://ref.ly/Num22:24), [1 S 14:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:4), [1 K 10:19](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:19), [10:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs10:20), [Zc 5:3](https://ref.ly/Zech5:3) \+; מִוֶּה וּמִזֶּה לְ *on one side and on the other side of…* [Ex 38:15](https://ref.ly/Exod38:15), [Jos 8:33](https://ref.ly/Josh8:33), Ez [45:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek45:7), [48:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek48:21). **f.** עַל זֶה *on this account* (rare), [La 5:17](https://ref.ly/Lam5:17) (pointing forwards), [Est 6:3](https://ref.ly/Esth6:3). So עַל זאֹת [Am 8:8](https://ref.ly/Amos8:8), [Mi 1:8](https://ref.ly/Mic1:8), [Je 2:12](https://ref.ly/Jer2:12) ([Jb 17:8](https://ref.ly/Job17:8)) [4:8](https://ref.ly/Jer4:8), [4:28](https://ref.ly/Jer4:28), [Psalm 32:6](https://ref.ly/Ps32:6); in late prose [Ezr 8:23](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:23), [9:15](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:15), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:2), [Ne 13:14](https://ref.ly/Neh13:14) 2 Ch [16:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:9), [16:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:10), [29:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:9), [32:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:20). **g.** עִם זֶה *in spite of this* † [Ne 5:18](https://ref.ly/Neh5:18).—On אֵי זֶה *which ?* אֵי מִזֶּה *whence ?* אֵי לָזאֹת *how ?* v. sub אַי **2**, p. 32; and on הַלָּז, הַלָּזֶה, הַלֵּזוּ, v. p. 229\. *Note.*—זֶה in [1 S 17:34](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:34) (in many edd.) is a typographical error (not a Kt) of Jacob b. Chayim's Rabbin. Bible of 1525 for שה *sheep*, which has been perpetuated hence in other subseq. edd. The reading of MSS. and of the best ancient edd. is שֶה (cf. de RossiVar. ## זֹה (id: BDB02545)

\[Str2090] † זֹה *this* (f.) \= זאֹת (cf. זוֺ, in Mish. the regular fem. of זֶה)—[2 K 6:19](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:19), Ez [40:45](https://ref.ly/Ezek40:45), [Ec 2:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:2), [2:24](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:24), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:15), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:18), [7:23](https://ref.ly/Eccl7:23), [9:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:13) (זאת does not occur in Ec); also in the phr. כָּזֹה וְכָזֶה (v. זה **6 c** β).

* **Associated Passages:** Ecclesiastes 5:18; Ecclesiastes 7:23; Ezekiel 40:45; Ecclesiastes 9:13; Ecclesiastes 2:2; 2 Kings 6:19; Ecclesiastes 5:15; Ecclesiastes 2:24


## זוּ (id: BDB02546)

\[Str2097] † זוּ i.q. זֹה (q.v.): only [Ho 7:16](https://ref.ly/Hos7:16) b זוֺ לַעָגָּם *this* (i.e. the falling by the sword, va) is their derision; and (as, relat.) [Psalm 132:12](https://ref.ly/Ps132:12) וְעֵדֹ֫תִ֭י זֹוֺ and my testimony *which…* (neglecting the accentuation: v. De, and cf. the remark under זֶה **5**).

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 132:12; Hosea 7:16


## זוּ (id: BDB02547)

\[Str2098] † זוּ **pron.** (poet.), indeclinable. **1\.** a **demonstr.** Hb [1:11](https://ref.ly/Hab1:11) זוּ כֹחוֺ *this* his strength, [Psalm 12:8](https://ref.ly/Ps12:8) מִן־הַדּוֺר זוּ (unusual, for הַזֶּה), [62:12](https://ref.ly/Ps62:12) שְׁתַּיִם־זוּ שָׁמַעָתִּי *these* two things \&c. (but: better (WickesPost. Acc. 64\) שְׁ֭תַּיִם ז֣וּ two things (are there) *which* \&c.: v. **2**). **2** a **relative**, [Ex 15:13](https://ref.ly/Exod15:13) עַם־זוּ גָאָלְתָּ the people *which* thou hast redeemed, v:[16](https://ref.ly/Exod15:16), [Is 42:24](https://ref.ly/Isa42:24) זוּ חָטָאנוּ לוֺ *against whom* we sinned, [43:21](https://ref.ly/Isa43:21), [Psalm 9:16](https://ref.ly/Ps9:16) (fem.) רֶשֶׁת־שזוּ טָ֝פָמנוּ so [31:5](https://ref.ly/Ps31:5), [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ps10:2), [17:9](https://ref.ly/Ps17:9) (with a *plural* antecedent) מִפְּנֵי רְשָׁעִים זוּ שַׁדּוּנִי, [32:8](https://ref.ly/Ps32:8), [68:29](https://ref.ly/Ps68:29) (thou) *who* hast wrought for us ! [142:4](https://ref.ly/Ps142:4), [143:8](https://ref.ly/Ps143:8). To most of these passages the remark made under זה **5** respecting the intention of the punctuators also applies: comp. De on [Psalm 9:16](https://ref.ly/Ps9:16). (Cf. دُو, used as an indeclin. relat. pron. \= أَلّدِي in the Ṭayyite dialect: thus أتى عليهم ذو أتى there has come upon them *that which* has come: ذحن ذو فعلنا كذا it is we *who* have done this. See SchuHariri Cons. ii. 75, Thes406, WAG. i. 347, e, Lanep. 986, FlKl. Schr. i. p. 359\.)

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 42:24; Psalms 10:2; Psalms 68:29; Psalms 62:12; Psalms 143:8; Habakkuk 1:11; Psalms 142:4; Psalms 12:8; Psalms 31:5; Exodus 15:16; Psalms 9:16; Psalms 32:8; Exodus 15:13; Psalms 17:9; Isaiah 43:21

## זָהַם (id: BDB02550)

\[Str2092] † \[זָהַם] **vb.** be foul, loathsome, but only **Pi.** causat. (NH *id., be foul*, Pi. *make foul;* Aram. זְהִים *foul*, زَهِمَ *make foul, fetid;* Ar. زَهِمَ *stink, be greasy*)—**Pi.** *Pf.* 3 fs. sf. 3 ms. וְזִהֲמַתּוּ חַיָּתוֺ לָחֶם (consec.) [Jb 33:20](https://ref.ly/Job33:20) *his life maketh it, bread, loathsome* to him (‖ונפשׁו מַאֲכַל תַּאֲוָה) (On sf. v. Ges§ 131, 2, R. 4; Dr:[1 S 21:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:14): acc. to Siegf the word is hopelessly corrupt.)

> \[Str2093] † ָ\+זהַם **n.pr.m.** (fr. above √ ?) a son of Rehoboam, [2 Ch 11:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:19).

* **Associated Passages:** 2 Chronicles 11:19; 1 Samuel 21:14; Job 33:20


## I זָהַר (id: BDB02552)

\[Str2094] † I. \[זָהַר] **vb.** be light, shining (Ar. زَهَرَ, زَهُرَ *shine*, adj. اَزْهَرُ *bright* (cf. LagBN 120\); Aram. זְהַר, ܙܗܰܪ; NH in deriv.; v. also sub II. זהר)—**Hiph.** intrans. (late) *send out light, shine*, fig. of everlasting glory of teachers of righteousness: וְהַמַּשְכִּלִים יַוְהִרוּ כְּזֹהַר הָרָקִיעַ [Dn 12:3](https://ref.ly/Dan12:3) *and they that make wise shall shine like the shining of the firmament* (‖וּמַצְדִּיקֵי הָרַבִּים כַּכּוֺכָבִים לְעוֺלָם וָעֶד).

> \[Str2096] † ז֫הַֹר **n.\[m.] shining, brightness** (Ar. زُهْرَةٌ, NH זְהוֺר, cf. Aram. זִיהֲרָא);—abs. Ez [8:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:2) ´כְּמַרְאֵה ז *like an appearance of shining* (explained, vb, as resembling amber, חַשְׁמַל cf. [1:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek1:27)); also cstr. [Dn 12:3](https://ref.ly/Dan12:3) v. foregoing.

* **Associated Passages:** Ezekiel 1:27; Ezekiel 8:2; Daniel 12:3


## II זָהַר (id: BDB02554)

\[Str2094] † II. \[זָהַר] **vb.** only **Hiph., Niph.** (NH Hiph., *admonish*, cf. Aram. זְהַר (Aph. Pa. etc.), ܙܗܰܪ (Ar. زَهَرَ VIII. *take care of, be mindful of* is perh. Aram. loan\-word, so TA cf. Lane).—Most identify with I. זהר \= *give light, enlighten, instruct, admonish;* this possible, but not certain, mng. *shine* is late in Heb.; usual sense as given below is not *enlighten, illumine* mentally, but *warn;* v. also construction with two accusatives [Ex 18:20](https://ref.ly/Exod18:20));—**Hiph.** *Pf.* וְהִזְהִיר consec. Ez [33:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:3); sf. וְהִזְהִירהֹ [2 K 6:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:10); 2 ms. הִזְהַרְתָּ Ez [3:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:19), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:9), וְהִזְהַרְתָּ֫ Ez [3:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:17), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:7), וְהִזְהַרְתָּ֫ה [Ex 18:20](https://ref.ly/Exod18:20), sf. הִזְהַרְתּוֺ Ez [3:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:18), [3:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:20), [3:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:21), וְהִזְהַרְתָּ consec. Ez [3:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:17), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:7); וְהִזְהַרְתֶּם consec. [2 Ch 19:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:10); *Inf. cstr.* לְהַזְהִיר Ez [3:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:18), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:8); —*instruct, teach, warn:* [Ex 18:20](https://ref.ly/Exod18:20) (E) *teach them ordinances* (2 acc.); [2 K 6:10](https://ref.ly/2Kgs6:10) the place *which* (אֲשֶׁר) *the man of God told him of* (אָמַר־לוֺ) and *warned him* (sf.) *of;* elsewh. only Ez Ch; *warn, give a warning to*, alw. sq. acc. Pers. only: Ez [3:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:18), [3:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:19), [3:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:20), [33:3](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:3); *give them* (acc.) *a warning from me* (מִפֶּנִּי, ´י speaks) Ez [3:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:17), [33:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:7); *warn the wicked* (רָשָׁע) *from* (away from, מִן) *his way* Ez [3:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:18), [33:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:8), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:9); *warn the righteous* (צַדִּיק) Ez [3:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:21) sq. Inf. *in order that the righteous sin not*, cf. [2 Ch 19:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr19:10) sq. acc. Pers. \+ final cl. c. וְ. **Niph.** *Pf.* 3 ms. נִזְהָ֔ר Ez [3:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:21), [33:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:4), [33:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:5), [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6); also נִזְהָ֭ר v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:5) (rd. perh. הִזְהִיר, so We Sm Co); *Imv.* הִזָּהֵר [Ec 12:12](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:12); *Inf. cstr.* לְהִזָּהֵר [Ec 4:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:13); Pt. נִזְהָר [Psalm 19:12](https://ref.ly/Ps19:12); —*be instructed, admonished, warned* (pass. of Hiph.):—גַּם־עַבְדְּךָ נִזְהָ֣ר בָּהֶם [Psalm 19:12](https://ref.ly/Ps19:12) *yea thy servant, he is instructed* (or *warned*) *by them* (i.e. lets himself be instructed or warned by the ´מִשְׁפְּטֵי י v:[10](https://ref.ly/Ps19:10)); in Ez distinctly *take warning*, abs. Ez [3:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek3:21) (cf. **Hiph.**) [33:4](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:4), [33:5a](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:5); so [Ec 12:2](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:2) and, more generally, אֲשֶׁר לאֹ יָדַע לְהִזָּהֵר [Ec 4:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:13) *who knoweth not how to take admonition; be warned, receive warning* Ez [33:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:6) (of people, c. לאֹ, i.e. if no warning be given them), also MT v:[5b](https://ref.ly/Ezek33:5), but rd. perh. Hiph. (v. supr.) *but he* (i.e. the watchman) *gave warning, he hath delivered his* (*own*) *soul.*

April\-May, named as month of beginning the temple building; בְּחֹדֶשׁ זִו הוּא הַחֹדֶשׁ הַשֵּׁנִי [1 K 6:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:1) \= בְּיֶרַח ִ\+זו v:[37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:37); LXX B v:[1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:1) has simply ἐν μηνὶ τῷ δευτέρῳ, v:[37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:37) (in LXX v4) ἐν μηνὶ Νεισῷ καὶ τῷ δευτέρῳ μηνί̀ LXX codd. ἐν μηνὶ Ζιου μηνὶ τῷ δευτέρῳ; LXXL (only v:[37](https://ref.ly/1Kgs6:37)) ἐν δευτέρῳ μηνί, ἐν μηνὶ Ζιου;—(si vera l. comp. perh. Aram. זִיו ܙܝܺܘܳܐ *brightness, freshness*, so BAram. [Dn 2:31](https://ref.ly/Dan2:31) \+; cf. Targum יֶרַח זִיו נִיצָנַיָּא *month of brightness of flowers;* As. *zîmu* (*zîvu*), DlPr 152 f. cf. NöZMG 1886, 732; Thes al. der. זִו, זִיו ( \= זְהִיו) fr. זהה in sense *be bright, splendid*, etc., cf. Ar. زَهَا *increase, thrive* (of seed\-produce), زَهْوٌ *a beautiful and bright*, or *fresh*, plant; Aram. זְהָא, adj. זָהוֺהָא *haughty, proud;* ܙܗܳܐ *glory, boast, be magnificent;* yet cf. Ol:§ 174 ad fin.)

## זוּב (id: BDB02557)

\[Str2100] † \[זוּב] **vb. flow, gush** (NH *id.*, Aram. דּוּב, דִּיב, ܕܳܒ, all used oft. in sense **4** infr.; Ar. ذَابَ (med. و) *melt, dissolve, flow*)—only **Qal** *Impf.* יָזוּב [Lv 15:25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25) etc.; *Pt.* זָב [Je 49:9](https://ref.ly/Jer49:9) f. זָבָ֫ה [Lv 15:19](https://ref.ly/Lev15:19) etc.;—**1\.** *flow, gush* (poet.), of water from the smitten rock [Psalm 78:20](https://ref.ly/Ps78:20) (‖שָׁטַף), [105:41](https://ref.ly/Ps105:41) (‖הָלַח), [Is 48:21](https://ref.ly/Isa48:21) (‖נָזַל). **2\.** *Pt.* freq. in זָבַת חָלָב וּדְבַשׁ (אֶרֶץ), always of promised land, [Ex 3:8](https://ref.ly/Exod3:8), [3:17](https://ref.ly/Exod3:17), [13:5](https://ref.ly/Exod13:5) (all J) [33:3](https://ref.ly/Exod33:3), [Nu 13:27](https://ref.ly/Num13:27), [14:8](https://ref.ly/Num14:8), [16:13](https://ref.ly/Num16:13), [16:14](https://ref.ly/Num16:14) (all JE) [Dt 6:3](https://ref.ly/Deut6:3), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Deut11:9), [26:9](https://ref.ly/Deut26:9), [26:15](https://ref.ly/Deut26:15), [27:3](https://ref.ly/Deut27:3), [31:20](https://ref.ly/Deut31:20), [Jos 5:6](https://ref.ly/Josh5:6) (D) [Lv 20:24](https://ref.ly/Lev20:24) (H) [Je 11:5](https://ref.ly/Jer11:5), [32:22](https://ref.ly/Jer32:22), Ez [20:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:6), [20:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:15) (in last two ins. ארץ LXX Co); זָב עִמְקֵח [Je 49:4](https://ref.ly/Jer49:4) (fertile valley of Ammon). **3\.** fig. *flow away* \= *pine away* (from hunger) [La 4:9](https://ref.ly/Lam4:9). **4\.** *flow*, of issue from woman (all P), [Lv 15:25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25) יָזוּב זוֺב דָּמָהּ (v. ווֺב); esp. *have an issue* (*flux, gleet*), of woman [Lv 15:25](https://ref.ly/Lev15:25) and [15:19](https://ref.ly/Lev15:19) (תִּהְיֶה זָבָה); usu. of man, [2 S 3:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam3:29) (‖מְצֹרָע); elsewhere P, [Lv 15:2](https://ref.ly/Lev15:2) זָב מִבִּשָרוֺ; with cogn. acc. [15:33](https://ref.ly/Lev15:33); ordinarily *Pt.* זָב *one that has an issue* [Lv 15:4](https://ref.ly/Lev15:4), [15:6](https://ref.ly/Lev15:6), [15:7](https://ref.ly/Lev15:7), [15:8](https://ref.ly/Lev15:8), [15:9](https://ref.ly/Lev15:9), [15:11](https://ref.ly/Lev15:11), [15:12](https://ref.ly/Lev15:12), [15:13](https://ref.ly/Lev15:13), [15:32](https://ref.ly/Lev15:32) (all P), [22:4](https://ref.ly/Lev22:4) (H; ‖צָרוּעַ); also [Nu 5:2](https://ref.ly/Num5:2) (P; ‖ *id.*)
