## II זוז (id: BDB02570)

II. זוז (*be abundant*, cf. esp. As. *zâzu, abundance*, DlPr. 67 f. ZimBP 94, 97; only as √ of foll.; perh. related to I. זוז (cf. Thes), but this very dubious).

> \[Str2123] II. זִיז **n.\[m.]** abundance, fulness: only מִזִּיז כְּבוֺדָהּ [Is 66:11](https://ref.ly/Isa66:11) that ye may suck out and be delighted, *from the abundance of her glory* (i.e. of Jerusalem) (so Di al.; \> Checrit. n., and OP 472 f. Brd (cf. Ew *‘Euter’*), who comp. vulg. Ar. *zîza, udder* (breast, teat); this suits vb. תָּמֹצּוּ and ‖תִּינְקוּ וּשְבַעְתֶּם מִשֹּׁד, but is dub. in Heb., perh. not Shemit., v. Dozyi. 619 a and reff.; also LagProph. Chald. who proposes, plausibly, מִבִּיזִ, cf. Aram. ܒܶܙܳܐ PS502, ביזא; Ar. بزّ *pap*).

> \[Str2104] † זוּזִים **n.pr.gent.** an ancient trans\-Jordanic people, dwelling in הָם [Gn 14:5](https://ref.ly/Gen14:5); otherwise unknown; abode appar. in (later) land of Ammon, between Bashan and Moab; commonly supposed \= זַמְזֻמִּים q.v., in that case perh. txt. error in one case or the other, cf. NöUntersuchungen z. Kritik d. A. T. 162\.—(LXX ἔθνη ἰσχυρά).—On n.pr.loc. Ζιζα, زيزا, between Boṣra and Lejûn, cf. Kn Di ad loc., TristrMoab 182 ff.

> \[Str2105] † זוֺחֵת **n.pr.m.** a descendant of Judah (etym. and mng. unknown) [1 Ch 4:29](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:29) (v. also בֶּן־זוֺחֵת), LXX Ζωαν, Ζωαβ, A Ζωχαθ, LXXL Ζαωθ.

> \[Str2106] זָוִית v. sub זוה. above

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 4:29; Isaiah 66:11; Genesis 14:5


## I זול (id: BDB02575)

I. זול (prob. √ of foll., Ar. زَالَ *remove, depart*).

> \[Str2108] † \[זוּלָה] **n.\[f.]** prop removal, only found in the *st. c.* as **prep.** and **conj.** except, only, save that—cstr. זוּלַת [2 K 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:14) and (with the obsolete case\-ending י—ִ) זוּלָתִי [Dt 1:36](https://ref.ly/Deut1:36), [4:12](https://ref.ly/Deut4:12), [Jos 11:13](https://ref.ly/Josh11:13), [1 K 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:18), [12:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:20), [Psalm 18:32](https://ref.ly/Ps18:32); with sf. זוּלָתִי [Ho 13:4](https://ref.ly/Hos13:4), [Is 45:5](https://ref.ly/Isa45:5), [45:21](https://ref.ly/Isa45:21), זוּלָֽתְךָ, זוּלָתֶָךְ [2 S 7:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:22) \= [1 Ch 17:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr17:20), [Is 26:13](https://ref.ly/Isa26:13), [64:3](https://ref.ly/Isa64:3), [Ru 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:4), זוּלָתָהּ [1 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:10); —**1\. prep.** *except, besides*, lit. *with removal of…* (syn. מִבַּלְעֲדֵי [Psalm 18:32](https://ref.ly/Ps18:32), [Is 45:21](https://ref.ly/Isa45:21); בִּלְתִּי [Ho 13:4](https://ref.ly/Hos13:4); and cf. [Dt 1:36](https://ref.ly/Deut1:36) with [Nu 32:12](https://ref.ly/Num32:12)), [Dt 1:36](https://ref.ly/Deut1:36) זולתי כלב *with the exception of* Caleb, [1 S 21:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:10) אֵין אַחֶרֶת זוּלָתָהּ there is none other *except* it, [1 K 12:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:20), [2 K 24:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs24:14), [Ru 4:4](https://ref.ly/Ruth4:4): oft. in such phrases as, There is no God (or, Who is God?) *except* me (or thee), [2 S 7:22](https://ref.ly/2Sam7:22), [Psalm 18:32](https://ref.ly/Ps18:32), [Ho 13:4](https://ref.ly/Hos13:4), [Is 45:5](https://ref.ly/Isa45:5), [45:21](https://ref.ly/Isa45:21), [64:3](https://ref.ly/Isa64:3). Always after a neg. or qu., exc. [Is 26:13](https://ref.ly/Isa26:13) אדנים זולתך lords *other than* thou. Connected inexactly with what precedes, ´ז acquires the force of *only*, [Dt 4:12](https://ref.ly/Deut4:12) ye saw no form זוּלָתִי קוֺל *save* a voice \= (there was) *only* a voice, [1 K 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:18). **2\.** conj. [Jos 11:13](https://ref.ly/Josh11:13) *except that* he burnt Hazor alone.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 64:3; 1 Kings 12:20; 1 Kings 3:18; Hosea 13:4; Joshua 11:13; Ruth 4:4; 2 Samuel 7:22; Numbers 32:12; Isaiah 45:5; Deuteronomy 4:12; 1 Chronicles 17:20; 2 Kings 24:14; Deuteronomy 1:36; Isaiah 45:21; Isaiah 26:13; 1 Samuel 21:10; Psalms 18:32


## II זוּל (id: BDB02577)

\[Str2107] †II. זוּל **vb.** lavish (cf. Aram. זוּל, *be cheap, of little value, lightly esteem;* similarly זלל q.v.)—**Qal** *Pt.* הַזָּלִים זָהָב מִכִּיס [Is 46:6](https://ref.ly/Isa46:6) (‖כֶּסֶף בַּקָּנֶה יִשְׁקָֹלוּ).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 46:6


## זוּן (id: BDB02578)

\[Str2109] † זוּן **vb.** feed (NH. BAram. *id.*; Syr. ܙܳܢ; Sam. ࠆࠂࠍ, ࠆࠄࠍ);—**Hoph.** *Pt.* סוּסִים מוזנים [Je 5:8](https://ref.ly/Jer5:8) (Kt; i.e. מוּזָנִים; Qr מְיֻזָּנִים from יזן q.v.) *well\-fed horses*, fig. of adulterers; DlPr 73 f. comp. As. *zanânu, be full* (i.e. of sexual desire).

> \[Str4202] † מָזוֺן **n.m.** food, sustenance (so Talm. BAram. Syr.)—בָּר זָלָחֶם וּמָזוֺן [Gn 45:23](https://ref.ly/Gen45:23) (Di ‘´מ rather an Aram. word; perh. later gloss for orig. צֵדָה v:[21](https://ref.ly/Gen45:21), [42:25](https://ref.ly/Gen42:25)’); הַמָּזוֺן לָרֹב [2 Ch 11:23](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:23).

> \[Str2181] † זוֺנָה v. זנה

* **Associated Passages:** Jeremiah 5:8; 2 Chronicles 11:23; Genesis 42:25; Genesis 45:23; Genesis 45:21
* **Associated Articles:** זָנָה (ID: `BDB02675@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## זוּעַ (id: BDB02581)

\[Str2111] † זוּעַ **vb.** tremble, quake (Aram. (incl. BAram.) *id.*, ܙܳܥ; cf. also Ar. زَاع *impel*, زَعْزَعَ *move, agitate, shake* (on these v. NöZMG 1886, 725\), As. *zû, storm\-wind* (\= Aram. זוֺעָא) Zim BP 94—**Qal** (late) *Pf.* 3 ms. זָע [Est 5:9](https://ref.ly/Esth5:9); *Impf.* 3 mpl. c. rel. שֶׁיָּזֻעזּ [Ec 12:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:3); —*tremble, quake*, in presence of powerful superior [Est 5:9](https://ref.ly/Esth5:9) (sq. מִמֶּנּוּ); said of שֹׁמְרֵי הַבַּיִת in descr. of infirm old man [Ec 12:3](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:3) **Pilp.** *Pt.* sf. מְזַזְעֶיךָ Hb [2:7](https://ref.ly/Hab2:7); causat. and intens. they that shall *violently shake thee*, fig. of foes of Babylon, instruments of Yahweh's judgment.

> \[Str2113,2189] † זְוָעָה and, by transpos., זַעֲוָה (Di [Dt 28:25](https://ref.ly/Deut28:25) cf. Sta§ 192 a) **n.f.** a trembling, i.e. object of trembling, terror: זְוָעָה [Is 28:19](https://ref.ly/Isa28:19), also Kt [Je 15:4](https://ref.ly/Jer15:4) \+ 4 t.; זַעֲוָה [Dt 28:25](https://ref.ly/Deut28:25), Ez [23:46](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:46) (also Qr [Je 15:4](https://ref.ly/Jer15:4) \+ 4 t.);—*object of trembling, terror, fright;* זְהָיִיתָ לְזַעֲוָה [Dt 28:25](https://ref.ly/Deut28:25) *and thou shalt become a terror* to al1 (לְכֹל) the kingdoms of the earth; ´וגו ´וּנְתַתִּים לְז) [Je 15:4](https://ref.ly/Jer15:4), [29:18](https://ref.ly/Jer29:18), [34:17](https://ref.ly/Jer34:17) (‖וְלִשְׁרֵקָה וּלְחֶרְפָּה לְאָלָה וּלְשַׁמָּה) also ´וגו ´וּנְתַתִּים לְז [24:9](https://ref.ly/Jer24:9) (‖וּלְמָשָׁל לִשְׁנִינָה וְלִקְלָלָה לְחֶרְפָּה), Ez [23:46](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:46), [2 Ch 29:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:8) .

> \[Str2127] † זִיעַ **n.pr.m.** a Gadite, [1 Ch 5:13](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:13), LXX Ζουε, Ζεα.

> \[Str2190] † זַעֲוָן **n.pr.m.** a Horite [Gn 36:27](https://ref.ly/Gen36:27) \= [1 Ch 1:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 28:19; 1 Chronicles 1:42; Ezekiel 23:46; Ecclesiastes 12:3; Esther 5:9; Genesis 36:27; Habakkuk 2:7; Jeremiah 24:9; Jeremiah 29:18; 1 Chronicles 5:13; Jeremiah 15:4; 2 Chronicles 29:8; Jeremiah 34:17; Deuteronomy 28:25


## I זוּר (id: BDB02585)

\[Str2114] †I. \[זוּר] **vb.** be a stranger (Ar. زَارَ (med. و) *incline toward, repair to, visit;* II. *honour as visitor or guest;* IX. *decline, turn aside;* زَايءِرٌ visitor; Aram. זוּר, oft. \= Heb. סוּר *turn aside, turn aside to visit*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. זֹר֫וּ [Psalm 58:4](https://ref.ly/Ps58:4) (Köi. 445\), זָ֫רוּ [Psalm 78:30](https://ref.ly/Ps78:30), [Jb 19:13](https://ref.ly/Job19:13); *Pt.* זָר [Ex 30:33](https://ref.ly/Exod30:33) \+ 27 t.; f. זָרָה [Ex 30:9](https://ref.ly/Exod30:9) \+ 7 t.; mpl. זָרִים [Ho 5:7](https://ref.ly/Hos5:7) \+ 30 t.; fpl. זָרוֺת [Pr 22:14](https://ref.ly/Prov22:14), [23:33](https://ref.ly/Prov23:33); \= sf. זָרָיִח [Is 29:5](https://ref.ly/Isa29:5); **1\.** Pf. *become estranged* [Psalm 58:4](https://ref.ly/Ps58:4), c. מן *from* [Jb 19:13](https://ref.ly/Job19:13), [Psalm 78:30](https://ref.ly/Ps78:30). **2\.** **Pt.** as adj. *strange*, or as noun *stranger:* **a.** to the family, of another household, אִישׁ זָר [Dt 25:5](https://ref.ly/Deut25:5), elsewhere זָר [1 K 3:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs3:18), [Jb 19:15](https://ref.ly/Job19:15), [Pr 6:1](https://ref.ly/Prov6:1), [11:15](https://ref.ly/Prov11:15), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Prov20:16), [27:13](https://ref.ly/Prov27:13); זָרִים [Psalm 109:11](https://ref.ly/Ps109:11), [Pr 5:10](https://ref.ly/Prov5:10), [5:17](https://ref.ly/Prov5:17); בנים זרים, children of another household than God's [Ho 5:7](https://ref.ly/Hos5:7); especially of another family than priests' אִישׁ זָר [Lv 22:12](https://ref.ly/Lev22:12) (H) [Nu 17:5](https://ref.ly/Num17:5) (P); זָר [Lv 22:10](https://ref.ly/Lev22:10), [22:13](https://ref.ly/Lev22:13) (H) [Ex 29:33](https://ref.ly/Exod29:33), [30:33](https://ref.ly/Exod30:33), [Nu 3:10](https://ref.ly/Num3:10), [3:38](https://ref.ly/Num3:38), [18:7](https://ref.ly/Num18:7) (P); not belonging to the tribe of Levi, [Nu 1:51](https://ref.ly/Num1:51), [18:4](https://ref.ly/Num18:4) (P). **b.** to the person, *another* [Jb 19:27](https://ref.ly/Job19:27), [Pr 14:10](https://ref.ly/Prov14:10), [27:2](https://ref.ly/Prov27:2); זָרָה (אִשָּׁה) *strange woman, harlot* [Pr 2:16](https://ref.ly/Prov2:16), [5:3](https://ref.ly/Prov5:3), [5:20](https://ref.ly/Prov5:20), [7:5](https://ref.ly/Prov7:5), [22:14](https://ref.ly/Prov22:14), [23:33](https://ref.ly/Prov23:33). **c.** to the land, *foreign*, זָרִים *foreigners* (as such usu. enemies) [Ho 7:9](https://ref.ly/Hos7:9), [8:7](https://ref.ly/Hos8:7), [Is 1:7](https://ref.ly/Isa1:7), [1:7](https://ref.ly/Isa1:7), [25:2](https://ref.ly/Isa25:2), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Isa25:5), [29:5](https://ref.ly/Isa29:5), [61:5](https://ref.ly/Isa61:5), [Je 5:19](https://ref.ly/Jer5:19), [30:8](https://ref.ly/Jer30:8), [51:2](https://ref.ly/Jer51:2), [51:51](https://ref.ly/Jer51:51), [La 5:2](https://ref.ly/Lam5:2), Ez [7:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:2), [11:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:9), [16:32](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:32), [28:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:7), [28:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek28:10), [30:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:12), [31:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:12), [Jo 4:17](https://ref.ly/Joel4:17), Ob:[11](https://ref.ly/Obad1:11), [Jb 15:19](https://ref.ly/Job15:19), [Psalm 54:5](https://ref.ly/Ps54:5); אֵל זָר *foreign god* [Psalm 44:21](https://ref.ly/Ps44:21), [81:10](https://ref.ly/Ps81:10) \= זָר [Is 43:12](https://ref.ly/Isa43:12); so זָרִים [Dt 32:16](https://ref.ly/Deut32:16) and (in fig.) [Je 2:25](https://ref.ly/Jer2:25), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Jer3:13); מַיִם זָרִים *foreign waters* [2 K 19:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:24), [Je 18:14](https://ref.ly/Jer18:14); זְמֹרַת זָר [Is 17:10](https://ref.ly/Isa17:10) *vine\-slip of a stranger;* כְּמוֺ־זָר נֶחְשָׁבוּ [Ho 8:12](https://ref.ly/Hos8:12) *they are regarded as foreign;* מַעֲשֵהוּ זָר [Is 28:21](https://ref.ly/Isa28:21) *his work is foreign* (as if dealing with enemies). **d.** *strange* to the law; קְטֹרֶת זָרָה *strange incense* [Ex 30:9](https://ref.ly/Exod30:9) (P); אֵשׁ זָרָה *strange fire* [Lv 10:1](https://ref.ly/Lev10:1), [Nu 3:4](https://ref.ly/Num3:4), [26:61](https://ref.ly/Num26:61)(P). **Niph.** *Pf.* נָז֫רֹוּ [Is 1:4](https://ref.ly/Isa1:4), Ez [14:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:5); *be estranged* [Is 1:4](https://ref.ly/Isa1:4), pregn., sq. אָחוֺר (cf. RV); מֵעַל Ez [14:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek14:5). **Hoph.** *Pt.* מוּזָר *estranged* [Psalm 69:9](https://ref.ly/Ps69:9).

## II זוּר (id: BDB02586)

\[Str2114] †II. \[זוּר, זִיר] **vb.** be loathsome, Bö§ 1142 (Ar. ذَارَ *fastidivit, abhorruit;* As. *zâru, resist*, *Impf. izîru*, DlPr 65 SchrCOT Gloss);—only 3 fs. זָ֫רָה [Jb 19:17](https://ref.ly/Job19:17) רוּחִי זָ֫רָה לְאִשְׁתִּי *my breath is loathsome to my wife* (וְחַנֹּתִי לְ in ‖ cl.); \> most, who derive fr. I. זוּר, *become strange* and so *repugnant*.

> \[Str2114] † זָרָא **n.\[f.]** loathsome thing, עַד אֲשֶׁר יֵצֵא מֵאַפְּכֶם וְהָיָה לָכֶם לְזָרָא [Nu 11:20](https://ref.ly/Num11:20) (JE) *and it* (the flesh) *become to you a loathsome thing* (LXX εἰς χολέραν, Vulgate *in nauseam*), prob. err. for זָרָה (Sam.)

* **Associated Passages:** Job 19:17; Numbers 11:20


## III זוּר (id: BDB02588)

\[Str2115,2116] †III. \[זוּר] **vb.** press down and out (Aram. זִיר *press together*, Syr. ܙܘܰܪ, ܙܳܪ *compress;* cf. Ar. زَيَّرَ *twist* the lip of a beast)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 pl. זֹ֫רוּ [Is 1:6](https://ref.ly/Isa1:6) (pass.; \> √זרר, Ar. زَرَّ *draw forcibly together* Ol:§ 233 c, 245 l Köi. 333\); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיָּ֫זַר Ju [6:38](https://ref.ly/Judg6:38), 3 fs. sf. תְּזוּרֶהָ [Jb 39:15](https://ref.ly/Job39:15); *Pt. pass* f. זוּרֶה ( \= זוּרָה Ges§ 80, 2, d) [Is 59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5); —*press* (twist or wring) *out* a fleece Ju [6:38](https://ref.ly/Judg6:38); *not pressed out*, of sore (fig. of continued disaster) [Is 1:6](https://ref.ly/Isa1:6); *press under foot* an egg, [Jb 39:15](https://ref.ly/Job39:15) (‖דּוּשׁ), [Is 59:5](https://ref.ly/Isa59:5) (pass.)

> \[Str2213] † זֵר **n.m.** circlet, border (orig. *that which presses, binds*, cf. also Aram. ܙܺܝܪܳܐ *bracelet*, זִירָא *wreath, crown*, NH זִיר *id.*, זִירָה *ring*, *wrestler's ring;* others fr. a √ זרר)—זֵר זֵהָב [Ex 25:11](https://ref.ly/Exod25:11), [25:24](https://ref.ly/Exod25:24), [25:25](https://ref.ly/Exod25:25), [30:3](https://ref.ly/Exod30:3), [37:2](https://ref.ly/Exod37:2), [37:11](https://ref.ly/Exod37:11), [37:12](https://ref.ly/Exod37:12), [37:26](https://ref.ly/Exod37:26); זֵרוֺ [Ex 30:4](https://ref.ly/Exod30:4), [37:27](https://ref.ly/Exod37:27) (all P).

> \[Str2223] † זַרְזִיר **adj. girded, girt** (cf. BaNB 206\)—only cstr. [Pr 30:31](https://ref.ly/Prov30:31) זַרְזִיר מָתְנַיִם אוֺ־תָיִו *that which is girt in the loins*, etc., named with lion, he\-goat, and king, as stately in motion. Perh.\= *grey\-hound* Ew Bö De al., or *war\-horse* Bo Ges Hi al.; Vrss. *cock*, Talm. *raven*, v. De Now (NH זַרְזִיר *starling* (*war\-horse* only in interpr. of [Pr 30:31](https://ref.ly/Prov30:31)), Aram. ܙܰܪܙܺܝܪܳܐ; Lexx. also ܙܰܪܙܽܘܪܳܐ *starling*, in Ar. زُرْزُورٌ, perh. loan\-wd. Others der. fr. a √זרר, v. supr.)

> \[Str4205] † מָזוֺר **n.\[m.]** wound (as needing to have its matter *pressed out*)—fig. of injury to, or sufferings of, Isr. and Judah: מָזוֺר [Ho 5:13](https://ref.ly/Hos5:13) also [Je 30:13](https://ref.ly/Jer30:13) (Gf Che RVm; but accents Ew Ges Gie AV RV *for pressing*, i.e. binding up); מְזֹרוֺ [Ho 5:13](https://ref.ly/Hos5:13) (cf. III. זור [Is 1:6](https://ref.ly/Isa1:6)).—מָזוֺר Ob:[7](https://ref.ly/Obad1:7) v. sub מזר.

> \[Str2117] † זָזָא v. sub זוז. p. 265

## זָחַח (id: BDB02593)

\[Str2118] † \[זָחַח] **vb.** remove, displace (Ar. زَجَّ *push, thrust away;* comp. also Aram. זוּחַ, ܙܳܚ, *move, move away* (intrans.))—**Niph.** *Impf.* וְלאֹ יִזַּח הַחשֶׁן מֵעַל הָאֵפוֺד [Ex 28:28](https://ref.ly/Exod28:28) *and that the breast\-piece be not displaced* etc., [39:21](https://ref.ly/Exod39:21) (both P).

* **Associated Passages:** Exodus 28:28; Exodus 39:21


## I זָחַל (id: BDB02594)

\[Str2119] †I. \[זָחַל] **vb. shrink back, crawl away** (Aram. זְחַל *crawl* (also *drop, drip*, of water: so NH זָחַל), זָחֵיל *worm;* Syr. ܙܳܚܠܴܐ *locust* (as *crawling*); Ar. زَحَلَ *withdraw, retire to a distance* (v. Lane, Wetzst in DeHiob 2, 428\), and Sab. זחַל *withdraw, humble oneself* DHMZMG 1875, 610\)—**Qal** only *Pt.* pl. cstr. זֹחֲלֵי [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24), [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17); —*crawl*, of reptiles (pt.) זֹחֲלֵי עָפָר [Dt 32:24](https://ref.ly/Deut32:24) i.e. serpents (as poisonous), instruments of Yahweh's judgment on Isr.; ארץ ´ז [Mi 7:17](https://ref.ly/Mic7:17) *id.* (as crawling into the earth to hide), sim. of nations in fear of ´י.

> \[Str2120] † זֹחֶ֫לֶת **n.f.** mng. dub.; perh. crawling thing, serpent (WeSkizzen iii. 171 cites Ar. *Zuḫal* \= *Saturn*, in connex. with [1 K 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:9); cf. Lane \& Wetzst in DeHiob 2, 428 on view that Zuḫal \= *he who withdraws*, because of planet Saturn's remoteness)—only c. art., in design. loc. עִם אֶבֶן הַזֹּחֶלָת [1 K 1:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:9) (cf. אֶבֶן ad fin.)

* **Associated Passages:** Micah 7:17; Deuteronomy 32:24; 1 Kings 1:9


## II זָחַל (id: BDB02596)

\[Str2119] † II. \[זָחַל] **vb. fear, be afraid** (Aram. דְּחַל, ܕܚܶܠ; cf. Ar. ذَحْلٌ *rancour*, *malevolence;* NöZMG. 1886, 741\)—only **Qal** *Pf.* 1 S. זָחַלְתִּי [Jb 32:6](https://ref.ly/Job32:6) *therefore I feared* (‖וָאִירָא); \> most, who derive fr. I. זָחַל \= *shrink away* in fear, *hold back* (RV), cf. Targum אִסְתְּפִּיתִי Fl Ch WBii. 571\.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 32:6


## זִיד (id: BDB02597)

\[Str2102] † \[זִיד, זוּד] **vb.** boil up, seethe, act proudly, presumptuously, rebelliously (NH Hiph. הזיד) as Heb.; Aram. of Zinjirli זד *presumptuous*, DHMSendsch. 56; Ar. زَادَ (med. ي) *increase, exceed, exaggerate*, e.g. in talk; also Sab. n.pr, זיד (زَيْد) DHMEpigr. Denkm. 44 זידאל Idib. 52 (on distinct. betw. זוד and זיד in Sab. v. Sab. Denkm.:64\); perh. also Sab. מזדודן *great one, prince*, e.g. Sat. Denkm.:89\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. זָ֫דָה [Je 50:29](https://ref.ly/Jer50:29); 3 pl. זָ֫דוּ [Ex 18:11](https://ref.ly/Exod18:11); —of Egyptians, בַּדָּבָר אֲשֶׁר זָדוּ עֲלֵיהֶם [Ex 18:11](https://ref.ly/Exod18:11)(E) *in the matter* as to *which they acted presumptuously against them;* of Babylon, זָדָה ´אֶל־י [Je 50:29](https://ref.ly/Jer50:29) *against* ´י *hath she acted presumptuously*. **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 pl. הֵזִידוּ [Ne 9:10](https://ref.ly/Neh9:10) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יָזִיד [Dt 18:20](https://ref.ly/Deut18:20), יָזִד [Ex 21:14](https://ref.ly/Exod21:14); וַיָּ֫זֶד [Gn 25:29](https://ref.ly/Gen25:29); 3 mpl. יְזִידוּן [Dt 17:13](https://ref.ly/Deut17:13); 2 mpl. וַתָּזִדוּ [Dt 1:43](https://ref.ly/Deut1:43); **1\.** *boil, seethe*, trans., וַיָּ֫זֶד יַעֲקֹב נָזִיד [Gn 25:29](https://ref.ly/Gen25:29) *and Jacob boiled pottage*. **2\.** *act presumptuously, insolently*, [Ex 21:14](https://ref.ly/Exod21:14) (JE) כִּי יָזִד אִישׁ עַל־רֵועהוּ לְגָרְגוֺ בְעָרְמָה *when a man acteth presumptuously against his neighbour to slay him;* usually against ´י; sq. vb. fin. [Dt 1:43](https://ref.ly/Deut1:43)) *and ye acted presumptuously and went up;* sq. inf. [18:20](https://ref.ly/Deut18:20) the prophet Who *shall presume to speak* a word in my name; also abs. [17:13](https://ref.ly/Deut17:13) elsewh. only Ne; of Egyptians [9:10](https://ref.ly/Deut9:10) sq. עַל (\= **Qal** [Ex 18:11](https://ref.ly/Exod18:11)); abs. v:[16](https://ref.ly/Deut9:16) (‖´וַיַּקְשׁוּ אֶת־רְפָּם וְלאֹ שָֽׁמְעוּ וגו); v:[29](https://ref.ly/Deut9:29) (‖וְלאֹ ´שָֽׁמְעוּ וגו).

> \[Str2086] † זֵד **adj.** insolent, presumptuous:—´ז [Pr 21:24](https://ref.ly/Prov21:24); pl. זֵדִים [Je 43:2](https://ref.ly/Jer43:2) \+ 11 t.;—alw. masc., and alw. of opposition to ´י, wickedness; with qualified noun expressed, only הָאֲנָשִׁים הַזֵּדִים [Je 43:2](https://ref.ly/Jer43:2), of opponents of Jerem.; elsewh. used substantively, as. term. techn. for godless, rebellious men; once sg. זֵד יָהִיר לֵץ שְׁמוֺ [Pr 21:24](https://ref.ly/Prov21:24) *a haughty insolent one, scorner* is *his name* (‖עוֺשֶה בְּעֶבְרַת זָדוֺן); usu. pl. [Is 13:11](https://ref.ly/Isa13:11)(´גְּאוֺן ז; ‖גַּאֲוַת עָרִיצִים); [Mal 3:13](https://ref.ly/Mal3:13) (‖עשֵֹׁי רִשְׁעָה), v:[19](https://ref.ly/Mal4:1) (‖רִשְׁעָה עשֵֹׁה); and in [Psalm 86:14](https://ref.ly/Ps86:14) (‖עָרִיצִים), [119:21](https://ref.ly/Ps119:21)(‖מִמִּצְוֺתֶיךָ הַשֹּׁגִים), v:[51](https://ref.ly/Ps119:51), v:[69](https://ref.ly/Ps119:69), v:[78](https://ref.ly/Ps119:78), v:[85](https://ref.ly/Ps119:85), v:[122](https://ref.ly/Ps119:122); so prob. also [Psalm 19:14](https://ref.ly/Ps19:14) גַּם מִזֵּדִים חֲשךְ עַבְדֶָּךָ *also from presumptuous men hold back thy servant* (Ew Ol Hup Che RVm); \> presumptuous sins Deal. (AVRV).

> \[Str2087] † זָדוֺן **n.m.**: [Pr 11:2](https://ref.ly/Prov11:2) insolence, presumptuousness (on format. cf. LagBN 204, Anm.), abs. ´ז [Dt 17:12](https://ref.ly/Deut17:12) \+ 7 t.; cstr. זְדוֺן [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16), Ob:[3](https://ref.ly/Obad1:3); sf. זְדֹנְךָ [1 S 17:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:28); —**1\.** *insolence, presumption* toward men; affirmed of David by Eliab [1 S 17:28](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:28) (‖רֹעַ לְבָבֶָךָ); usu. **2\.** of presumptuous godlessness (cf. זוּד, זֵד), exhibited in disobedience to priest or judge [Dt 17:12](https://ref.ly/Deut17:12); in false prediction [18:22](https://ref.ly/Deut18:22); in gen. [Pr 11:2](https://ref.ly/Prov11:2) (opp. צְנוּעִים), [13:10](https://ref.ly/Prov13:10); ´עֶבְרַת ז [Pr 21:24](https://ref.ly/Prov21:24) (cf. זֵד); לִבֶָּךָ ´ז) [Je 49:16](https://ref.ly/Jer49:16) ( \= Ob:[3](https://ref.ly/Obad1:3)) *presumption of thine heart;* of Babylon personif. under fig. of ´ז [Je 50:31](https://ref.ly/Jer50:31), [50:32](https://ref.ly/Jer50:32); dub. is ´פָּרַח הַזּ Ez [7:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek7:10) *insolence hath blossomed* (preceded by צָץ הַמַּטֶּה) i.e. either of Isr. as demanding punishment, or of Babylon as instr. of punishment.

> \[Str2121] † \[זֵידוֺן] **adj.** insolent, raging, only pl. and fig. הַמַּיִם הַזֵּידוֺנִים [Psalm 124:5](https://ref.ly/Ps124:5).

> \[Str5138] † נָזִיד **n.\[m.]** thing sodden or boiled, pottage; abs. ´נ [Gn 25:29](https://ref.ly/Gen25:29) \+ 4 t.; cstr. נְזִיד [Gn 25:34](https://ref.ly/Gen25:34); —a kind of boiled leguminous food, obj. of וַיָּ֫זֶד [Gn 25:29](https://ref.ly/Gen25:29) (v. זוּד **Hiph.**); defined v:[34](https://ref.ly/Gen25:34) as נְזִיד עֲדָשִׁים (v. עדשׁ); obj. of בַּשֵּׁל [2 K 4:38](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:38); boiled in a סִיר v:[39](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:39) cf. v:[40](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:40); mentioned, appar. as a common food, [Hg 2:12](https://ref.ly/Hag2:12).

LagM iii. 215 ff HomAufsätze u. Abh., 1892, 94, 99 ff.; √dub.; Thes and most der. fr. assumed זהה *be bright, fresh, luxuriant* (v. sub זִוּ) \+ ת afformat. which is. then treated as radical, cf. Sta§ 187 a; but this not certain, cf. Ol:§ 119 b, 142 a; acc. to LagArmen. Stud. § 1347 ´ז is Armen. loan\-word, v. also LagM l. c.; BN 219, Anm.);—abs. ´ז [Gn 8:11](https://ref.ly/Gen8:11), cstr. זֵית [Dt 8:8](https://ref.ly/Deut8:8); [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32); sf. זֵיתְךָ [Dt 24:20](https://ref.ly/Deut24:20), זֵיתֶָךָ [Ex 23:11](https://ref.ly/Exod23:11), [Dt 28:40](https://ref.ly/Deut28:40); pl. זֵיתִים [Dt 6:11](https://ref.ly/Deut6:11), etc.;—**1\.** *olive\-tree* Ju [9:8](https://ref.ly/Judg9:8), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Judg9:9) (personif., in Jotham's fable), [Ho 14:7](https://ref.ly/Hos14:7) (sim. of beauty), [Jb 15:33](https://ref.ly/Job15:33) \= (as casting off its flowers, sim. of wicked man); in [2 S 15:23](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:23) ins. אֲשֶׁר ´הַזּ (before הַמִּדְבָּר, read ´בַּמּ) LXXL Dr; also pl. of two *olive\-trees* [Zc 4:3](https://ref.ly/Zech4:3), [4:11](https://ref.ly/Zech4:11); רַעֲנָן ´ז [Psalm 52:10](https://ref.ly/Ps52:10) *fresh*, (thriving) *olive\-tree* (sim. of prosperity), so [Je 11:16](https://ref.ly/Jer11:16); ´עֲלֶה־ז [Gn 8:11](https://ref.ly/Gen8:11)(J) *olive\-branch, sprig*, cf. [Ne 8:15](https://ref.ly/Neh8:15) (here \+ עֲלֵי עֵץ־שֶׁמֶן, etc.); also pl. שְׁתִלֵי זֵיתִים [Psalm 128:3](https://ref.ly/Ps128:3) *olive\-shoots* (sim. of children); שִׁבֲּלֵי זֵיתִים [Zc 4:12](https://ref.ly/Zech4:12) *olive\-branches* (in Zech.'s vision); זַיִת in representative or coll. sense, \= *olive\-trees*, groups of growing olive\-trees, as property, source of wealth, *olive\-yards* [Ex 23:11](https://ref.ly/Exod23:11) (JE; \+ כֶּרֶם), Ju [15:5](https://ref.ly/Judg15:5) (\+ *id.*); עֵץ־הַזַּיִת [Hg 2:19](https://ref.ly/Hag2:19) (\+ הַגֶּפֶן וְהַתְּאֵנָה וְהָרִפּוֺן); also in phr. כִּחֵשׁ מַעֲשֵה־זַיִת Hb [3:17](https://ref.ly/Hab3:17) *the bearing, yield*, of *olive\-trees* (‖וּשְׁדֵמוֺר לאֹ־עָשָה אֹכֶל, cf. also תְּאֵנָה and יְבוּל גֶּפֶן va); specif. זֵית שֶׁמֶן, *oil\-yielding olive\-trees* [Dt 8:8](https://ref.ly/Deut8:8) cf. [2 K 18:32](https://ref.ly/2Kgs18:32); ´נֹקֶף ז *striking* or *beating of olive\-trees* [Is 17:6](https://ref.ly/Isa17:6), [24:13](https://ref.ly/Isa24:13) (sim. of desolation at judgment of ´י), cf. תַחְבֹּט ´כִּ זֵיתְךָ [Dt 24:20](https://ref.ly/Deut24:20); rather more often pl.; [Am 4:9](https://ref.ly/Amos4:9) (‖גַּנּוֺת, כְּרָמִים, תְּאֵנִים), [1 S 8:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:14) (‖שָדוֺת, כְּרָמִים), cf. also [Dt 6:11](https://ref.ly/Deut6:11), [28:40a](https://ref.ly/Deut28:40), [Jos 24:13](https://ref.ly/Josh24:13), [2 K 5:26](https://ref.ly/2Kgs5:26), [1 Ch 27:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:28), [Ne 5:11](https://ref.ly/Neh5:11), [9:25](https://ref.ly/Neh9:25). **2\.** of fruit of olive\-tree, *olives:* [Dt 28:40b](https://ref.ly/Deut28:40) (c. שֶׁמֶן); תִּדְרֹךְ זַיִת [Mi 6:15](https://ref.ly/Mic6:15) (‖שֶׁמֶן); also שֶׁמֶן זַיִת *olive\-oil* [Ex 27:20](https://ref.ly/Exod27:20), [30:24](https://ref.ly/Exod30:24) (P) [Lv 24:2](https://ref.ly/Lev24:2) (H). **3\.** in designations of place: מַעֲלֵה הַזֵּיתִים [2 S 15:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:30) *the ascent of the olive\-trees* (LXX ἐν τῇ ἀναβάσει τῶν ἐλαιῶν \= Mount of Olives); הַר־הַזֵּיתִים [Zc 14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4), [14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4) (LXX τὸ ὄρος τὠν ἐλαιῶν); the well\-known hill E. of Jerus. (NT usu. as [Zc 14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4) LXX, e.g. [Mk 13:3](https://ref.ly/Mark13:3); cf. also הָהָר [1 K 11:7](https://ref.ly/1Kgs11:7), Ez 11:33, [Ne 8:15](https://ref.ly/Neh8:15); Targum טזּר זֵיתַיָּא [2 K 23:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs23:13) cf. [2 S 15:30](https://ref.ly/2Sam15:30), [Zc 14:4](https://ref.ly/Zech14:4) etc.; Targum Ct [8:5](https://ref.ly/Song8:5) טוּר מִשְׁחָא, and so Talm. etc.)

> \[Str2241] † זֵתָם **n.pr.m.** (sub זית in Thes, \= foregoing)—a Levite (Gershonite) [1 Ch 23:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:8), appar. son of לַעְדָּן and brother of יְחִיאֵל and יוֺאֵל; [26:22](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:22) appar. grandson of לַעְדָּן, and son of יְחִיאֵלִי (expressly called brother of יוֺאֵל); LXX Ζεθομ, Ζοθομ, LXXL Ζηθαν.

## זָכָה (id: BDB02615)

\[Str2135] † \[זָכָה] **vb.** be clear, clean, pure, alw. in moral sense (As. *zakû* ZimBP; Aram. דְּכָא, ܕܟܳܐ, ܕܟܺܝ; cf. Ar. دَكَاء *be bright* (of a fire), *pungent* (of an odour); *be acute, quick of mind*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 ms. יִזְכֶּה [Jb 15:14](https://ref.ly/Job15:14), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Job25:4); 2 ms. תִּזְכֶּה [Psalm 51:6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:6); 1 s. interrog. הַאֶזְכֶּה [Mi 6:11](https://ref.ly/Mic6:11); —**1\.** *be clean, pure*, of man, in the sight of God, [Jb 15:14](https://ref.ly/Job15:14), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Job25:4) (in both ‖יצדק; cf. also זַכּוּ [15:15](https://ref.ly/Job15:15), [25:5](https://ref.ly/Job25:5)). **2\.** *be clear, be justified* \= be regarded as just, righteous, of God, [Psalm 51:6](https://ref.ly/Ps51:6) (‖תצדק), [Mi 6:11](https://ref.ly/Mic6:11). **Pi.** *Pf.* 1 s. זִכִּיתִי [Psalm 73:13](https://ref.ly/Ps73:13), [Pr 20:9](https://ref.ly/Prov20:9); *Impf.* יְזַכֶּ־ה [Psalm 119:9](https://ref.ly/Ps119:9):—*make* or *keep clean, pure*, obj. לְבָבִי [Psalm 73:13](https://ref.ly/Ps73:13) (‖אֶרְחַץ כַּפָּי), obj. לִבִּי [Pr 20:9](https://ref.ly/Prov20:9) (‖טָהַרְתִּי); obj. אֶת־אָרְחוֺ [Psalm 119:9](https://ref.ly/Ps119:9). **Hithp.** *Imv.* m.pl. הִזַּכּוּ [Is 1:16](https://ref.ly/Isa1:16) *make yourselves clean* (‖רַחֲצוּ);—on form cf. Ges§ 54, 2, b, Rem. KöLgb. i, 345, 534 Sta§ 129 d.

> \[Str2137] זְכוֺכִית v. sub זכך. below

* **Associated Passages:** Job 25:5; Micah 6:11; Proverbs 20:9; Psalms 73:13; Psalms 119:9; Job 25:4; Job 15:15; Psalms 51:6; Job 15:14; Isaiah 1:16


## זָכַךְ (id: BDB02617)

\[Str2141] † \[זָכַךְ] **vb.** be bright, clean, pure (kindred with foregoing. Only La Jb)—**Qal** *Pf.* only 3 mpl. זַכּ֫וּ;—**1\.** *be right, shining*, fig. of splendour of nobles נְזִירִים מִשֶּׁלֶג ´ז [La 4:7](https://ref.ly/Lam4:7) (‖מֵחָלָב צַחוּ). **2\.** *be clean, pure* in God's sight, of heavens [Jb 15:15](https://ref.ly/Job15:15), of stars [25:5](https://ref.ly/Job25:5) (‖יַאֲהִיל of moon; cf. also יִזְכֶּה [15:14](https://ref.ly/Job15:14) [25:4](https://ref.ly/Job25:4)). **Hiph.** *cleanse*, only *Pf.* 1 s. :וַהֲזִכּוֺתִי בְּבֹר כַּפָּֽי [Jb 9:30](https://ref.ly/Job9:30), fig. of making morally spotless (‖הִתְרָחַצְתִּי).

> \[Str2134] † זַךְ **adj.** pure, clean; abs. זַךְ [Jb 8:6](https://ref.ly/Job8:6) \+ 5 t.; זָךְֶ [Ex 27:20](https://ref.ly/Exod27:20), [Lv 24:2](https://ref.ly/Lev24:2); f. זַכָּה [Ex 30:34](https://ref.ly/Exod30:34) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** lit., *pure*, i.e. unmixed, free from foreign substances, of olive oil [Ex 27:20](https://ref.ly/Exod27:20), [Lv 24:2](https://ref.ly/Lev24:2), of frankincense [Ex 30:34](https://ref.ly/Exod30:34), [Lv 24:7](https://ref.ly/Lev24:7) (all P and H). **2\.** fig., *pure, clean, righteous* (only Jb Pr):—of man [Jb 8:6](https://ref.ly/Job8:6) (‖יָשָׁר), [33:9](https://ref.ly/Job33:9) (\+ בְּלִי פָשַׁע; ‖חַף); as subst. \= *the pure, righteous* וְזַךְ יָשָׁר פָּעֳלוֺ [Pr 21:8](https://ref.ly/Prov21:8) (opp. וָזָרָ q.v.); of mode of life כָּל־דַּרְכֵי־אִישׁ זַךְ בְּעֵינָיו [Pr 16:2](https://ref.ly/Prov16:2); of mode of action (פָּעֳלוֺ) [20:11](https://ref.ly/Prov20:11) (‖יָשָׁר); of doctrine (לִקְחִי) [Jb 11:4](https://ref.ly/Job11:4) (‖בַּר הייתי); of prayer [16:17](https://ref.ly/Job16:17) (‖לאֹ חָמָס בְּכַפָּי).

> \[Str2137] † זְכוֺכִית **n.\[f.]** glass (Targum זכוכיתא; but v. Frä64); as precious (‖זָהָב), yet of less value than wisdom [Jb 28:17](https://ref.ly/Job28:17). (Baer as above; זְכוּכִית van d. H, al.)

> \[Str2140] † זַכַּי **n.pr.m.** head of family of returned exiles, זַכָּ֔י [Ezr 2:9](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:9) (LXX Ζακχου, LXXL Ζακχαιου) \= [Ne 7:14](https://ref.ly/Neh7:14) (LXX Ζαθου, A Ζακχουρ, LXXL Ζακχαιου); perh. \= זַכַּי [Ne 3:20](https://ref.ly/Neh3:20) Qr (Kt זַבַּי q.v.; LXX Ζαβ(ρ)ου, LXXL Ραββαι).
> 
> 269:a. זַּכַיּ: Nes (privately) suggests that this might be abbrev. fr. זכריה.

* **Associated Passages:** Exodus 30:34; Nehemiah 7:14; Leviticus 24:2; Job 33:9; Job 9:30; Job 11:4; Proverbs 20:11; Ezra 2:9; Proverbs 21:8; Exodus 27:20; Leviticus 24:7; Job 25:5; Job 8:6; Job 16:17; Proverbs 16:2; Job 25:4; Lamentations 4:7; Job 15:15; Nehemiah 3:20; Job 28:17; Job 15:14


## זָכַר (id: BDB02621)

\[Str2142] † זָכַר **vb.** remember (As *zikâru, name, mention*, cf. n. *zikru*, Hpt in KAT2 Gloss. i LyonSarg.; Zinj. זכר, Ar. ذَكَرَ, Aram דְּכַר, ܕܟܰܪ, Palm. דרכנא *monument*, VogNo. 36 b, דכיר *memorial*, VogNo. 62, Sab. in n.pr. ידַכראל DHMEpigr. Denkm. 43, but Eth. ዘከረ፡)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. ´ז [Gn 40:23](https://ref.ly/Gen40:23) \+; 3 fs. זָֽכְרָה [La 1:7](https://ref.ly/Lam1:7), [1:9](https://ref.ly/Lam1:9); 2 ms. וְזָכַרְתָּ֫ [Dt 5:15](https://ref.ly/Deut5:15) \+; sf. זְכַרְתַּנִי [Gn 40:14](https://ref.ly/Gen40:14) cf. [1 S 1:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:11), זְכַרְתָּם [Psalm 88:6](https://ref.ly/Ps88:6); 2 fs. זָכַרְתְּ [Is 47:7](https://ref.ly/Isa47:7), [57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11) cf. [17:10](https://ref.ly/Isa17:10) \+ Ez [16:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:22); [16:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43) Qr (both Kt זכרתי), וְזָכַרְתְּ consec. Ez [16:61](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:61); 3 pl. זָֽכְרוּ Ju [8:34](https://ref.ly/Judg8:34) \+, etc.; *Impf.* 3 ms. יִזְכֹּר [Ho 8:13](https://ref.ly/Hos8:13) \+; יִזְכָּ־ר [2 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:11), [Pr 31:7](https://ref.ly/Prov31:7); וַיִּזְכֹּר [Gn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Gen8:1) \+; sf. וַיִּוְכְּרֶהָ [1 S 1:19](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:19); 2 fs. תִּזְכְּרִי [Is 54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4) \+ 2 t.; 1 s. אֶזְכֹּר [Lv 26:42](https://ref.ly/Lev26:42), [26:42](https://ref.ly/Lev26:42), [Is 43:25](https://ref.ly/Isa43:25) ([Psalm 77:12](https://ref.ly/Ps77:12) Qr v. infra), אֶזְכָּ־ר [Je 31:34](https://ref.ly/Jer31:34), וָאֶזְכֹּר [Ex 6:5](https://ref.ly/Exod6:5); sf. 2 ms. אֶזְכָּרְךָ [Psalm 42:7](https://ref.ly/Ps42:7), sf. 2 fs. אֶזְכְּרֵכִי [Psalm 137:6](https://ref.ly/Ps137:6) (v. Ges§ 58, 3, R, 1 Sta§§ 356, a, 2, 634 a, 2\), etc.; *Imv.* ms. זְכֹר [Ex 32:13](https://ref.ly/Exod32:13) \+; זְכָ־ר [Psalm 25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7) \+; זָכְרָה [Ne 6:14](https://ref.ly/Neh6:14) \+; sf. זָכְרֵנִי Ju [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28) \+ 2 t.; mpl. זִכְרוּ [Is 46:9](https://ref.ly/Isa46:9) \+, זְכָֹרוּ [Ne 4:8](https://ref.ly/Neh4:8); *Inf. abs.* זָכוֺר [Ex 13:3](https://ref.ly/Exod13:3) \+; זָבֹר [Je 31:30](https://ref.ly/Jer31:30) \+; *cstr.* לִזְכֹּר [Gn 9:16](https://ref.ly/Gen9:16), Ez [23:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:19), etc.; sf. בְּזָכְרֵנִי [Psalm 137:1](https://ref.ly/Ps137:1); *Pt.* act. pl. cstr. וּלְזֹכְרֵי [Psalm 103:18](https://ref.ly/Ps103:18); זָכוּר [Psalm 103:14](https://ref.ly/Ps103:14) v. infr.—**I.** human subj. **1\.** *remember, recall, call to mind*, usu. as affecting present feeling, thought, or action: **a.** *remember* past experiences (acc.) [Gn 42:9](https://ref.ly/Gen42:9) (E), [2 K 9:25](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:25) (acc. Pers. agent); sq. cl. with מִי [Jb 4:7](https://ref.ly/Job4:7); things formerly known (acc.) [Je 17:2](https://ref.ly/Jer17:2); sq. cl. with כִּי Ju [9:2](https://ref.ly/Judg9:2); with implied regret, longing [Nu 11:5](https://ref.ly/Num11:5) (JE), [Psalm 42:5](https://ref.ly/Ps42:5), [137:1](https://ref.ly/Ps137:1), [La 1:7](https://ref.ly/Lam1:7) cf. [Psalm 77:7](https://ref.ly/Ps77:7), neg. [137:6](https://ref.ly/Ps137:6) (‖שׁכח v:[5](https://ref.ly/Ps137:5)); so (sq. בְּ) [Je 3:16](https://ref.ly/Jer3:16) (‖עלה על לב and פקד). **b.** *recall* past distress, etc.; obj. not expressed, [La 3:20](https://ref.ly/Lam3:20) (זכור זכר), [Jb 11:16](https://ref.ly/Job11:16) (שׁכח in ‖ cl.); neg., sq. acc. [Is 54:4](https://ref.ly/Isa54:4) (‖שׁכח), [Pr 31:7](https://ref.ly/Prov31:7) (‖ *id.*). **c.** *remember* sins, (1\) to repent of them [Dt 9:7](https://ref.ly/Deut9:7) (\+ אל־תשׁכח, sq. cl. with את־אשׁר), elsewh. only Ezek., usu. c. acc. Ez [16:61](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:61) (\+ וְנִכְלַמְתְּ), v:[63](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:63) (obj. not expressed), [20:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:43), [36:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:21); (2\) to renew and repeat them Ez [23:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:19), neg. v:[27](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:27). **d.** esp. *remember* the dealings of ´י, expressed in great variety of terms in acc., [Dt 7:18](https://ref.ly/Deut7:18), [8:2](https://ref.ly/Deut8:2), [24:9](https://ref.ly/Deut24:9), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Deut32:7) (‖בין), [Is 46:9](https://ref.ly/Isa46:9), [63:11](https://ref.ly/Isa63:11), [Psalm 77:12](https://ref.ly/Ps77:12) Qr (Kt Hiph. q.v.) v:[12](https://ref.ly/Ps77:12), [105:5](https://ref.ly/Ps105:5), [143:5](https://ref.ly/Ps143:5) (‖הגיתי, אֲשוֺחֵחַ) [1 Ch 16:12](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:12); negatively, [Is 43:18](https://ref.ly/Isa43:18); (‖אַל תִּתְבָֹּ\+ננוּ), Ez [16:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:22), [16:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43), [Psalm 78:42](https://ref.ly/Ps78:42), [106:7](https://ref.ly/Ps106:7), [Ne 9:17](https://ref.ly/Neh9:17) (‖וַיְמָאֲנוּ לִשְׁמֹעַ); obj. cl. with בִּי esp. D, [Dt 5:15](https://ref.ly/Deut5:15), [15:15](https://ref.ly/Deut15:15), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Deut16:12), [24:18](https://ref.ly/Deut24:18), [24:22](https://ref.ly/Deut24:22), [Psalm 78:35](https://ref.ly/Ps78:35). **2\.** *remember* persons (human subj.), **a.** to their advantage:—sq. acc. [Gn 40:14](https://ref.ly/Gen40:14) (E), v:[23](https://ref.ly/Gen40:23) (E; neg., ‖שׁכח), [1 S 25:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam25:31). **b.** to make use of them (acc.) [Na 2:6](https://ref.ly/Nah2:6). **c.** their acts (acc.) to their advantage [2 Ch 24:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:22), to their disadvantage [2 S 19:20](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:20) (neg.); to make vengeance [Dt 25:17](https://ref.ly/Deut25:17). **d.** *remember* human obligations, acc. rei: בְּרִית אַחִים [Am 1:9](https://ref.ly/Amos1:9); cf. perh. [Est 2:1](https://ref.ly/Esth2:1) (acc. Pers. and also of deed); neg. [Ec 9:15](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:15) (acc. Pers.), [Psalm 109:16](https://ref.ly/Ps109:16) (sq. חסד עשות). **3\.** *remember* ´י: **a.** *call* him *to mind* [2 S 14:11](https://ref.ly/2Sam14:11) (i.e. recall a specific command of his). **b.** *recall*, and (esp.) *keep* ´י *in mind* [Dt 8:18](https://ref.ly/Deut8:18), [Je 51:50](https://ref.ly/Jer51:50), [Is 64:4](https://ref.ly/Isa64:4) (‖עשה צדק), Ez [6:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:9), [Zc 10:9](https://ref.ly/Zech10:9), [Jo 2:8](https://ref.ly/Joel2:8), [Psalm 42:7](https://ref.ly/Ps42:7), [63:7](https://ref.ly/Ps63:7) (‖אהגה), [77:4](https://ref.ly/Ps77:4), [Ne 4:8](https://ref.ly/Neh4:8); negatively Ju [8:34](https://ref.ly/Judg8:34), [Is 17:10](https://ref.ly/Isa17:10) (opp. שׁכח), [57:11](https://ref.ly/Isa57:11) (‖לאֹ שַמְתְּ עַל־לִבֵּךְ); cf. also אֶתאבּוֺרְאֶָךָ ´ז [Ec 12:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl12:1), and (no obj. expressed) [Psalm 22:28](https://ref.ly/Ps22:28) (\+ ´וישׁובו אל י); remember ´י's name [Psalm 119:55](https://ref.ly/Ps119:55). **4\.** *remember:* **a.** words of Moses [Jos 1:13](https://ref.ly/Josh1:13) (D) [Mal 3:22](https://ref.ly/Mal3:22); ´י's instructions through prophet [Is 44:21](https://ref.ly/Isa44:21), 46:48 (‖השׁיבו על לב), also [Mi 6:5](https://ref.ly/Mic6:5) (sq. cl. with מה). **b.** commandments of ´י (so as to do them) [Nu 15:39](https://ref.ly/Num15:39) (P, or H), v:[40](https://ref.ly/Num15:40) (P; \+ ועשיתם), [Psalm 103:18](https://ref.ly/Ps103:18) (\+ לעשותם), [119:52](https://ref.ly/Ps119:52) (משׁפטים); his covenant [1 Ch 16:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr16:15). **5\.** *think of* or *on, call to mind* something present or future:—sq. acc., issue of conduct [Is 47:7](https://ref.ly/Isa47:7), [La 1:9](https://ref.ly/Lam1:9) (end of Jerusalem); fight with crocodile [Jb 40:32](https://ref.ly/Job40:32); obj. a general truth, prosperity of wicked [Jb 21:6](https://ref.ly/Job21:6) (obj. not expr.); (fleeting) days of life [Ec 5:19](https://ref.ly/Eccl5:19); (coming) days of darkness, [11:8](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:8); a duty [Jb 36:24](https://ref.ly/Job36:24) (sq. cl. with כִּי). **6\.** *remember* a day, to observe, commemorate it:—day of Exodus [Ex 13:3](https://ref.ly/Exod13:3) (J) [Dt 16:3](https://ref.ly/Deut16:3); sabbath [Ex 20:8](https://ref.ly/Exod20:8) (E; ‖שמר [Dt 5:12](https://ref.ly/Deut5:12)). **7\.** *remember*, with implied *mention of,* obj. ´י [Je 20:9](https://ref.ly/Jer20:9) (‖דִּבֶּר בִּשְׁמוֺ); ´מַשָּא י [23:36](https://ref.ly/Jer23:36).

 **II.** Subj. ´י(אלהים). **1\.** *remember* persons: **a.** individuals, with kindness, granting requests, protecting, delivering etc., c. acc. Pers., [Gn 30:22](https://ref.ly/Gen30:22) (E), [1 S 1:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:11) (opp. שׁכח), v:[19](https://ref.ly/1Sam1:19), Ju [16:28](https://ref.ly/Judg16:28) (sq. וְחַזְּקֵנִי); [Je 15:15](https://ref.ly/Jer15:15) (‖פָּקְדֵנִי), [Psalm 106:4](https://ref.ly/Ps106:4) (‖ *id.*), [Jb 14:13](https://ref.ly/Job14:13) (\+ תשׁית לי חק), also [Gn 8:1](https://ref.ly/Gen8:1) (P), [19:29](https://ref.ly/Gen19:29) (P); neg. [Psalm 88:6](https://ref.ly/Ps88:6) (‖מידך נִגְזָרָוּ); sq. לְ Pers. [Psalm 25:7](https://ref.ly/Ps25:7) (yet cf. Che); sq. acc. rei \+ לְ Pers. (dat. commod.) [Ne 5:19](https://ref.ly/Neh5:19), [13:14](https://ref.ly/Neh13:14), [13:22](https://ref.ly/Neh13:22), [13:31](https://ref.ly/Neh13:31). **b.** individuals, to punish, sq. לְ Pers. [Ne 6:14](https://ref.ly/Neh6:14), [13:29](https://ref.ly/Neh13:29). **c.** his servants, people, the afflicted, (graciously) sq. לְ [Ex 32:13](https://ref.ly/Exod32:13) (JE), [Dt 9:27](https://ref.ly/Deut9:27), [Psalm 136:23](https://ref.ly/Ps136:23); sq. acc. [Psalm 9:13](https://ref.ly/Ps9:13) (opp. שׁכת), [74:2](https://ref.ly/Ps74:2), [115:12](https://ref.ly/Ps115:12) (sq. יְבָרֵךְ); cf. [Je 31:20](https://ref.ly/Jer31:20) (זכור זכר). **d.** his land [Lv 26:42](https://ref.ly/Lev26:42) (H), and neg. VB).

> \[Str2138] † \[זָכוּר] **n.m.** male, i. q. זָכָר, but only c. sf.;—coll. כָּל־זְכוּרְךָ [Ex 23:17](https://ref.ly/Exod23:17) \= [34:23](https://ref.ly/Exod34:23) (both JE), \= [Dt 16:16](https://ref.ly/Deut16:16), of attendance at feasts; כָּל־זְכוּרָהּ (of city refusing terms of peace) [Dt 20:13](https://ref.ly/Deut20:13) commanded to be slain (opp. הַנָּשִׁים, הַטַּף, etc., v:[14](https://ref.ly/Deut20:14)).

> \[Str2147] † זִכְרִי **n.pr.m.** LXX Ζεχρει, Ζαχρι, etc., exc. as below:—**1\.** a Reubenite [1 Ch 27:16](https://ref.ly/1Chr27:16). **2\.** a Judahite [2 Ch 17:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr17:16). **3\.** Levites: **a.** [Ex 6:21](https://ref.ly/Exod6:21) (P). **b.** [1 Ch 26:25](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:25). **c.** an Asaphite [1 Ch 9:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:15) ‖ [Ne 11:17](https://ref.ly/Neh11:17) where rd. זכרי (for זַבְדִּי, q. v.); cf. also *infr.* זַכּוּר **3 b. 4\.** a priest [Ne 12:17](https://ref.ly/Neh12:17) (LXXL Ζαχαριας). **5\.** Benjamites: **a.** [1 Ch 8:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:19). **b.** v:[23](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:23). **c.** v:[27](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:27). **d.** [Ne 11:9](https://ref.ly/Neh11:9). **6\.** father of a captain in Jehoiada's time [2 Ch 23:1](https://ref.ly/2Chr23:1) (LXX Ζαχαρια, LXXL Ζεχρι). **7\.** mighty man of Ephraim [2 Ch 28:7](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:7) (LXX Ἐζεχρει, LXXL Ζαχαριας).

> \[Str2139] † זַכּוּר **n.pr.m.** LXX Ζακχυρ, Ζακχουρ, etc.:—**1\.** a Reubenite [Nu 13:4](https://ref.ly/Num13:4) (P). **2\.** a Simeonite [1 Ch 4:26](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:26). **3\.** Levites: **a.** [1 Ch 24:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:27). **b.** an Asaphite [1 Ch 25:2](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:2) (Σακχους), v:[10](https://ref.ly/1Chr25:10) (Ζαχχουθ), [Ne 12:35](https://ref.ly/Neh12:35) (Ζακχουρ), cf. זִכְרִי supr. **3 c. c.** [Ne 10:13](https://ref.ly/Neh10:13) cf. [13:13](https://ref.ly/Neh13:13) **4\.** companion of Ezra, [Ezr 8:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra8:14) Qr (Kt זבוד, cf. supr. p. 256 a). **5\.** contemp. of Nehemiah, [Ne 3:2](https://ref.ly/Neh3:2).

> \[Str2146] † זִכָּרוֺן, זִכָּרֹן **n.m.**:[Ec 1:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:11) memorial, remembrance (cf. LgBN 199, 200\)—abs. זִכָּרוֺן [Jos 4:7](https://ref.ly/Josh4:7) \+; זִכָּרֹן [Ex 28:12](https://ref.ly/Exod28:12), [28:12](https://ref.ly/Exod28:12), [28:29](https://ref.ly/Exod28:29); cstr. זִכְרוֺן [Ec 1:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:11) \+ 2 t.; sf. זִכְרוֺנֵח [Is 57:8](https://ref.ly/Isa57:8); pl. הַזִּכְרֹנוֺת [Est 6:1](https://ref.ly/Esth6:1); sf. זִכְרֹנֵיכֶם [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12); —**1\.** *memorial, reminder:* **a.** memorial\-day [Ex 12:14](https://ref.ly/Exod12:14) (P). **b.** memorial\-usage [Ex 13:9](https://ref.ly/Exod13:9) (JE). **c.** memorial\-objects, altar\-plates [Nu 17:5](https://ref.ly/Num17:5) (P); stones in Jordan [Jos 4:7](https://ref.ly/Josh4:7) (JE); crowns in temple [Zc 6:14](https://ref.ly/Zech6:14); ´ז in [Is 57:8](https://ref.ly/Isa57:8) is symbol of strange god (Di), or perh. phallus\-image, as sign of harlot (Che), cf. \> Du (who prop. זְכָרוֺן, from זָכָר). **d.** memorial\-record; in a book [Ex 17:14](https://ref.ly/Exod17:14) (E); cf. סֵפֶר זִכָּרוֺן Mal [3:16](https://ref.ly/Mal3:16), הַזִּכְרֹנות דברי הימים ´ס [Est 6:1](https://ref.ly/Esth6:1); memorial, as proof of citizenship [Ne 2:20](https://ref.ly/Neh2:20) (‖חֵלֶק, צְדָקָה); reminder of Israel, ´לִפְנֵי י, of כֶּסֶף הַכִּפֻּרִים [Ex 30:16](https://ref.ly/Exod30:16); spoils of war [Nu 31:54](https://ref.ly/Num31:54); inscribed stones of ephod [Ex 28:12](https://ref.ly/Exod28:12), [28:29](https://ref.ly/Exod28:29), called ´אַבְנֵי ז v:[12](https://ref.ly/Exod28:12) \= [39:7](https://ref.ly/Exod39:7); blowing of trumpets [Nu 10:10](https://ref.ly/Num10:10) (foregoing all P), cf. זִכְרוֺן תְּרוּעַה [Lv 23:24](https://ref.ly/Lev23:24) (H; where, however, no ´לפני י, v. Di); זכרון(ה)מִנְחַת [Nu 5:15](https://ref.ly/Num5:15), [5:18](https://ref.ly/Num5:18) (P); *memorial\-sentence, apophthegm* [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12). **2\.** *remembrance* [Ec 1:11](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:11) (cstr. before prep. Ges§ 130, 1\), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:11) [2:16](https://ref.ly/Eccl2:16).
> 
> 272:a. זִכָּרוֺן: cite also (sub **1**) זִכְרֹנֵיכֶם מִשְׁלֵ־י אֵפֶר [Jb 13:12](https://ref.ly/Job13:12) *your memorial words are ashen sayings* (i.e. worthless), cf. Bu Du; Margolis (privately) suggests that LXX seems to have read יִמָּשֵׁל לְאֵפֶר \[? \< רָנְּכֶם] רָנְיְכֶם *your exultation shall be like ashes.*

## זלג (id: BDB02635)

זלג (Ar. زَلَجَ *glide, slip;* of arrow, *skim, slide along;* مِزْلَاجٌ a kind of *latch, sliding bolt;* Aram. זְלַג is *pour forth* (tears), *flow down*).

> \[Str4207] † מַזְלֵג **n.m.** appar. a sacrificial implement, three\-pronged fork שְׁלשׁ הַשִּׁנַּיִם ´הַמּ [1 S 2:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:13), cf. v:[14](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:14) (v. DrSm. pp. 23, 291\).

> \[Str4207] † \[מִזְלָגָה] **n.\[f.]** id., only pl. הַמִּזְלָגֹת [Ex 38:3](https://ref.ly/Exod38:3), [Nu 4:14](https://ref.ly/Num4:14); הַמִּזְלָגוֺת [1 Ch 28:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:17), [2 Ch 4:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:16); sf. מִזְלְגֹתָיו [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3); —a sacrificial implement, belonging to altar in tabernacle, [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3), [38:3](https://ref.ly/Exod38:3), [Nu 4:14](https://ref.ly/Num4:14) (all P); belonging to temple [1 Ch 28:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:17), [2 Ch 4:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:16).

* **Associated Passages:** Exodus 27:3; 2 Chronicles 4:16; 1 Chronicles 28:17; Numbers 4:14; 1 Samuel 2:13; Exodus 38:3; 1 Samuel 2:14


## I זָלַל (id: BDB02638)

\[Str2151] † I. \[זָלַל] **vb.** shake (Ar. زَلَّ *slip,* زَلْزَلَ *agitate, shake;* تَزَلْزَلَ *be agitated, quake*, esp. of earth\-quake)—**Niph.** *Pf.* נָזֹ֫לּוּ *shake, quake* [Is 63:19](https://ref.ly/Isa63:19) of mts. at ´י's presence (Ges De Che Di Du; LXX Vulgate as if fr. נזל *flow down*); so [64:2](https://ref.ly/Isa64:2) (but here prob. not original Che Di Du); read נָזֹלּוּ likewise Ju [5:5](https://ref.ly/Judg5:5) (LXX Thes Stu Be Bla MV SS; MT נָֽזְלוּ, fr. נזל).

> \[Str2150] † \[זַלְזַל] **n.\[m.]** (quivering) tendrils;—only pl. *tendrils* of vine הַזַּלְזַלִּים [Is 18:5](https://ref.ly/Isa18:5) (‖הַנְּטִישׁוֺת), in fig. of Yahweh's destroying the Assyrians.

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 63:19; Judges 5:5; Isaiah 18:5; Isaiah 64:2


## II זָלַל (id: BDB02640)

\[Str2151] † II. \[זָלַל] **vb. 1\.** be light, worthless, **2\.** make light of (As. *zalâlu, be in ruins*, COTGloss (AsrnStandard Inscr. 15\), Ar. دَلَّ \= Aram. ܕܰܠ (rare), ܕܠܻܝܠܴܐ *easy;* but זְלַל, ܙܰܠ, are more common (all intrans.))—**Qal** only *Pt.* act. זוֺלֵל [Dt 21:20](https://ref.ly/Deut21:20) \+ 2 t.; זוֺלֵלָה [La 1:11](https://ref.ly/Lam1:11); pl. זוֺלְלִים [Pr 28:7](https://ref.ly/Prov28:7), cstr. זֹלְלֵי [Pr 23:20](https://ref.ly/Prov23:20)—**1\.** *be worthless, insignificant* [Je 15:19](https://ref.ly/Jer15:19) (opp. יָקָר) [La 1:11](https://ref.ly/Lam1:11) (of Jerusalem in distress). **2\.** trans. *make light of* \= *be lavish with, squander* (cf. II. זוּל), esp. of gluttony בשר ´ז [Pr 23:20](https://ref.ly/Prov23:20) (‖סֹבְאֵי יַיִן), abs. v:[21](https://ref.ly/Prov23:21), [Dt 21:20](https://ref.ly/Deut21:20) (both ‖סֹבֵא), [Pr 28:7](https://ref.ly/Prov28:7). **Hiph.** *Pf.* sf. הִזִּילוּהָ [La 1:8](https://ref.ly/Lam1:8) causat. of **Qal 1** *make light of, despise* (opp. כִּבֵּד); on form cf. Ges§ 67, R. 8 RobGes, MV SS (\> assigned to זוּל by Thes KöLgb. i. 471\).

> \[Str2149] † זֻלֻּת **n.f.** worthlessness, only כְּרֻם זֻלֻּת לִבְנֵי אָדָם \[van d. H. זֻלּוּת] [Psalm 12:9](https://ref.ly/Ps12:9) cf. De \& Checrit. n.

* **Associated Passages:** Proverbs 28:7; Proverbs 23:21; Lamentations 1:11; Lamentations 1:8; Psalms 12:9; Proverbs 23:20; Jeremiah 15:19; Deuteronomy 21:20

## זלף (id: BDB02644)

\[Str2153] זלף (√ of foll.; Thes comp. Aram. זְלַף Pa. *drop, drip;* cf. ܙܠܰܦ; also NH זִלּוּף *sprinkling,* ܙܠܳܦܳܐ *dropping, pouring,* زَلَفَةٌ a *full bowl*, etc.)

## זָמַם (id: BDB02646)

\[Str2161] † זָמַם **vb.** consider, purpose, devise (Aram. זְמַם in der. conjj.; cf. Ar. ܙܰܡ *speak, talk* (rare); Aram. ܙܡ is *sonuit, resonavit,* the n. ܙܡܳܡܳܐ is *tinnitus, strepitus*)—only **Qal** *Pf.* זָמַם [Dt 19:19](https://ref.ly/Deut19:19) \+ 2 t.; זָמָם [La 2:17](https://ref.ly/Lam2:17); זָֽמְמָה [Pr 31:16](https://ref.ly/Prov31:16); זַמּ֫וֺתָ [Pr 30:32](https://ref.ly/Prov30:32); זַמֹּ֫תִי [Je 4:28](https://ref.ly/Jer4:28) but also זָמַ֫מְתִּי [Zc 8:14](https://ref.ly/Zech8:14), [8:15](https://ref.ly/Zech8:15); זַמֹּתִי [Psalm 17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3) either belongs here with irreg. accent (v. De), or is Inf. after anal. of ה“ל e.g. חַנּוֺת cf. [77:10](https://ref.ly/Ps77:10) (Hi Ri); Checrit. n. rds. זִמָּתִי; זָמָמוּ [Psalm 31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14); *Impf.* יָֽזְמוּ [Gn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Gen11:6) (Ges§ 67, R. 11\); on poss. *Inf.* זמֹּתִ֫י [Psalm 17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3) v. supr.; *Pt.* זֹמֵם [Psalm 37:12](https://ref.ly/Ps37:12); —**1\.** *consider, fix thought upon*, c. acc. of concrete obj. שָדֶה [Pr 31:16](https://ref.ly/Prov31:16). **2\.** *purpose, devise:* **a.** esp. of Yahweh's purpose in punishment [Je 4:28](https://ref.ly/Jer4:28) (abs.), [51:12](https://ref.ly/Jer51:12) sq. עשה \+ acc. (‖מְזִמָּה), [La 2:17](https://ref.ly/Lam2:17) (sq. אשׁר) [Zc 1:6](https://ref.ly/Zech1:6) (c. כאשׁר sq. inf.), [8:14](https://ref.ly/Zech8:14) (*id.*); of blessing only כֵּן שַׁבְתִּי זָמַמְתִּי לְהֵיטִיב [Zc 8:15](https://ref.ly/Zech8:15) (opp. v:[14](https://ref.ly/Zech8:14)). **b.** of evil purpose of wicked men לעשות ´אשׁר יז [Gn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Gen11:6), [Dt 19:19](https://ref.ly/Deut19:19) (c. כאשׁר \+ inf.), [Psalm 31:14](https://ref.ly/Ps31:14) (sq. inf.); abs. [Pr 30:32](https://ref.ly/Prov30:32) (‖נבל), [Psalm 17:3](https://ref.ly/Ps17:3) *have I* (i.e. if I have) *devised evil* (so De); sq. לְ Pers. (\= *against*) [Psalm 37:12](https://ref.ly/Ps37:12).

273:a. זָמַם **ad.** fin.: the text of [Pr 30:32](https://ref.ly/Prov30:32) is questioned by Frankenberg Toy al.; v. on II. נבל, 614:b infr.

> \[Str2162] † \[זָמָם] **n.\[m.]** plan, device (bad sense), only זְמָמוֺ [Psalm 140:9](https://ref.ly/Ps140:9) (‖מַאֲוַיֵּי רָשָׁע).

> \[Str2154] † I. זִמָּה **n.f.** plan, device, wickedness—abs. ´ז [Lv 18:17](https://ref.ly/Lev18:17) \+ 17 t.; cstr. זִמַּת [Pr 24:9](https://ref.ly/Prov24:9) \+ 2 t.; sf. זִמָּתֵח Ez [16:58](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:58) \+ 3t.; זִמַּתְכֶ֫נַָה Ez [23:48](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:48), [23:49](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:49); pl. זמּוֺת [Is 32:7](https://ref.ly/Isa32:7), sf. זִמֹּתַי [Jb 17:11](https://ref.ly/Job17:11); —**1\.** *plan, purpose* [Jb 17:3](https://ref.ly/Job17:3); elsewhere always **2\.** in bad sense: **a.** *evil device* [Is 32:7](https://ref.ly/Isa32:7) (c. יעִץ, sq. לְחַבֵּל); זִמַּת אִוֶּלָת [Pr 24:9](https://ref.ly/Prov24:9); cf. [Psalm 26:10](https://ref.ly/Ps26:10) (‖שֹׁחַד), [119:150](https://ref.ly/Ps119:150). **b.** *wickedness* in act ´עשה ז [Ho 6:9](https://ref.ly/Hos6:9) (where murder in context), [Pr 10:23](https://ref.ly/Prov10:23), [21:27](https://ref.ly/Prov21:27) cf. Now. Esp. **c.** of unchastity: incest [Lv 18:17](https://ref.ly/Lev18:17), [20:14](https://ref.ly/Lev20:14), [20:14](https://ref.ly/Lev20:14), licentiousness [19:29](https://ref.ly/Lev19:29) (all H), Ju [20:6](https://ref.ly/Judg20:6) (‖נְבָלָה), adultery [Jb 31:11](https://ref.ly/Job31:11); freq. (mostly Ez) metaph. of idolatry of people under fig. of harlotry and adultery [Je 13:27](https://ref.ly/Jer13:27) (זִמַּת זְנוּתֵח), Ez [16:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:27) (דַּרְכֵח זִמָּה), v:[43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43), v:[58](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:58) (both ‖תּוֺעֵבָה), [22:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:9) (c. עשה), v:[11](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:11) (טִמֵּא בְּזִמָּה, ‖תועבה), [23:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:21), [23:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:27) (‖זְנוּתֵח), v:[29](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:29) (עֶרְוַת זְנוּנַיִח וְזִמָּתֵח וְתַזְנוּתָיִח), v:[35](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:35) (‖תַּזְנוּתָיִח), v:[44](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:44) (´אִשּׁוֺת הַזּ, Co ´לעשות ז), v:[48](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:48), v:[48](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:48), v:[49](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:49) (‖חֲטָאֵי גִלּוּלֵיכֶן), [24:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:13). \[In Ez [16:27](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:27), [16:43](https://ref.ly/Ezek16:43), [24:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek24:13) del. Co, chiefly on intern. grounds.]

> \[Str2155] † II. זִמָּה **n.pr.m.** of several Levites (Gershonites)—**1\.** [1 Ch 6:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:5). **2\.** [1 Ch 6:27](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:27). **3\.** [2 Ch 29:12](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:12).

> \[Str4209] † מְזִמָּה **n.f.** purpose, discretion, device (Je and WisdLt), מְזִמָּה [Jb 42:2](https://ref.ly/Job42:2) \+ 5 t.; הַמְּזִמָּ֫תָה [Je 11:15](https://ref.ly/Jer11:15) (but rd. for רַבִּים ´המ with LXX הַמְּזִמֹּת הַֽנְדָרִים); מְזִמָּתוֺ [Je 51:11](https://ref.ly/Jer51:11); pl. abs. מְזִמּוֺת [Psalm 37:7](https://ref.ly/Ps37:7) \+ 7 t.; cstr. id. [Je 23:20](https://ref.ly/Jer23:20), [30:24](https://ref.ly/Jer30:24); sf. מְזִמּוֺתָיו [Psalm 10:4](https://ref.ly/Ps10:4); —**1\.** of ´י's *purposes* [Jb 42:2](https://ref.ly/Job42:2); chiefly in punishment (cf. זָמַם, זִמָּה) [Je 23:20](https://ref.ly/Jer23:20), [30:24](https://ref.ly/Jer30:24), [51:11](https://ref.ly/Jer51:11)(‖זמם v:[12](https://ref.ly/Jer51:12)). **2\.** *power of devising, discretion* [Pr 1:4](https://ref.ly/Prov1:4) (‖דַּעַת), [2:11](https://ref.ly/Prov2:11) (‖תְּבוּנָה), [3:21](https://ref.ly/Prov3:21) (‖תֻּשִׁיָּה), [5:2](https://ref.ly/Prov5:2) (‖דַּעַת); דַּעַת מְזִמּוֺת [8:12](https://ref.ly/Prov8:12). **3\. a.** evil *thoughts* of men [Psalm 10:4](https://ref.ly/Ps10:4), evil *devices* of men [Jb 21:27](https://ref.ly/Job21:27) (‖מחשׁבות), [Psalm 10:2](https://ref.ly/Ps10:2), [21:12](https://ref.ly/Ps21:12) (both c. חָשַׁב). **b.** *wickedness* in act [Je 11:15](https://ref.ly/Jer11:15), [Psalm 37:7](https://ref.ly/Ps37:7) (both c. עָשָה), [139:20](https://ref.ly/Ps139:20) לִמְזִמָּה \= *wickedly;*—note phrases: מְזִמּוֺת אישׁ *a man of (evil) devices* (or *practices*) [Pr 12:2](https://ref.ly/Prov12:2) (opp. טוֺכ) [14:17](https://ref.ly/Prov14:17); בַּעַל־מְזִמּוֺת [Pr 24:8](https://ref.ly/Prov24:8) (‖מְחַשֵּׁב לְהָרֵעַ; v. בַּעַל).
> 
> 273:b. מְזִמָּה, 1\. 3: rd. in [Je 11:15](https://ref.ly/Jer11:15) altogether עָֽשְתָה מְזִמֹּת הַֽנְדָרִים וּבְשַר קֹדֶשׁ יִעֲבִרוּ מֵעָלַיִח תָעָתֵךְ.

## זָמַן (id: BDB02652)

\[Str2163] † \[זָמַן] **vb.** (Aram. and late) only **Pu.** *Pt.* be fixed, appointed, of time (Ar. زَمِنَ *continue*, Aram. Pa. זַמֵּן, ܙܰܡܶܢ *summon to fixed time* or *place, invite, appoint;* cf. זִמָן infr.)—עִתִּים מְזֻמָּנִים [Ezr 10:14](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:14) [Ne 10:35](https://ref.ly/Neh10:35); מְזֻמָּנוֺת ´ע [Ne 13:31](https://ref.ly/Neh13:31).

— זְמָן; Mand. זנאן, v. NöM 152; on OPers. origin v. BAram.

> \[Str2165] † זְמָן **n.m.** (late) appointed time, time (Ar. زَمَنٌ, Eth. ዘመን፡ BAram. Targum זְמַן, but Syr. ܙܰܒܢܳܐ, Mand. זיבנא, Sam. ࠆࠁࠍ, Palm. זבנא, cf. ReckendorfZMG 1888, 394\)—sg. זְמָן [Ne 2:6](https://ref.ly/Neh2:6), [Ec 3:1](https://ref.ly/Eccl3:1); sf. וְכִזְמַָ\+נּם [Est 9:27](https://ref.ly/Esth9:27), בִּזְמַנֵּיהֶם v:[31](https://ref.ly/Esth9:31), (on Dagh. cf. Ges§ 20\. 2, c).

## I זמר (id: BDB02654)

\[Str2167] † I. \[זמר] **vb.** only **Pi.** make music in praise of God (´י), only poet. (Ar. زَمَرَ *pipe, play* on a reed; Aram. ܙܡܪ, זַמֵּר and deriv.; Eth. ዘመረ፡ in der. conjj.; on an orig. mng. *hum, murmur*, cf. HupZKM iii, (1840\) 394 ff. iv, 139 ff. IdPsalm. ## II זָמַר (id: BDB02659)

\[Str2168] † II. \[זָמַר] **vb.** trim, prune (NH *id.;* relation to √ I. obscure)—**Qal** *Impf.* 2 ms. תִּזְמֹר [Lv 25:3](https://ref.ly/Lev25:3), [25:4](https://ref.ly/Lev25:4) of pruning a vineyard (כֶּרֶם; H). **Niph.** *Impf.* יִזָּמֵר [Is 5:6](https://ref.ly/Isa5:6) *be pruned*, subj. כֶּרֶם (‖יֵעָדֵר).

> \[Str2159] † II. זָמִיר **n.\[m.]** trimming, pruning (cf. LgBN 173\)—only עַת הַזָּמִיר Ct [2:12](https://ref.ly/Song2:12) (\> others *singing*).—I. זמיר v. sub I. זמר supr.

> \[Str2156] † זְמוֺרָה **n.\[f.]** branch, twig, shoot—abs. ´ז [Nu 13:23](https://ref.ly/Num13:23), ´הַזּ Ez [8:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:17), [15:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:2); cstr. זְמֹרַת [Is 17:10](https://ref.ly/Isa17:10); sf. זְמֹרֵיהֶם [Na 2:3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3); —*branch* of grape\-vine [Nu 13:23](https://ref.ly/Num13:23) (JE), Ez [15:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek15:2); *branch, twig* (Co *Reissigbündel*) used in idolatrous worship אֶל־אַפָּם ´הַזּ Ez [8:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek8:17) (v. Sm and most), but custom obscure (v. Da), and txt. dub.; זְמֹרַת זָר (cf. Ew§ 287 b) [Is 17:10](https://ref.ly/Isa17:10) *twigs* of a strange one (i.e. of a strange god) fig. of idolatrous cults adopted by Isr.; ´ז pl. in fig. of Israelites [Na 2:3](https://ref.ly/Nah2:3).

> \[Str4211] † \[מַזְמֵרָה] **n.f.** pruning\-knife, only pl. וְכָרַת הַזַּלְזַלִּים בַּמַּזְמֵרוֺת [Is 18:5](https://ref.ly/Isa18:5); לְמַזְמֵרוֺת [Is 2:4](https://ref.ly/Isa2:4), [Mi 4:3](https://ref.ly/Mic4:3) (both ‖לְאִתִּים); וּמַזְמֵרֹתֵיכֶם [Jo 4:1](https://ref.ly/Joel4:1) (‖אִתֵּיכֶם).

> \[Str4212] † \[מְזַמֶּרֶת] **n.f.** snuffers, as utensil of Sol.'s temple, only pl. abs. מְזַמְּרוֺת [2 K 12:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:14), הַמְזַמְּרוֺת [1 K 7:50](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:50) \= [2 Ch 4:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:22), [2 K 25:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:14) \= [Je 52:18](https://ref.ly/Jer52:18).

## III זמר (id: BDB02664)

III. זמר (√ of foll.; mng. dub.; DHM in MV10, 983 comp. Ar. ذِمَارٌ *thing to be protected, thing sacred, inviolable,* Lane978, Sab. דַמר *protect;* Aram. ܕܰܡܶܪ *wonder at, admire,* ܕܡܝܺܪܳܐ *mirus, admirabilis,* etc.; hence זמרת [Gn 43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11) ‘die Merkwürdigkeiten des Landes’).

275:a. III. זמר: Ar. ذمر is *urge with chiding, reproof*, دِّمَارٌ *thing involving blame* (if not protected, defended); this hardly supports meaning *choice products* for III. זִמְרָה.

> \[Str2173] † II. זִמְרָה **n.f.** of uncertain meaning, perh. choice products (cf. DHM supr.), of various fruits, etc. מִזִּמְרַת הארץ [Gn 43:11](https://ref.ly/Gen43:11); LXX οἱ καρποί, so MV Str.; \> *music,* or *praise of the land,* fig. for produce (√ I. זמר) Thes Tu Kn SS.—I. ´ז v. sub I. זמר
> 
>  Uncertain in deriv. are the five foll.: —

> \[Str2169] † \[זֶ֫מֶר] **n.\[m.]** a certain animal allowed as food, most prob. some kind of *mountain\-sheep or \-goat* (SyrVer ܐܪܢܐ) וָזָמֶָר [Dt 14:5](https://ref.ly/Deut14:5). (AV RV *chamois:* but see TristramDB 2\. s. v., who points out that this is not a native of any Bible land.)

> \[Str2174] † I. זִמְרִי **n.pr.m.** LXX Ζαμβρ(ε)ι—**1\.** a Simeonite [Nu 25:14](https://ref.ly/Num25:14) (P). **2\.** grandson of Judah [2 Ch 2:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr2:6) (app. \= זַבְדִּי [Jos 7:1](https://ref.ly/Josh7:1) q.v.) **3\.** king of Isr. before Omri [1 K 16:9](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:9), [16:10](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:10), [16:12](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:12), [16:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:15), [16:16](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:16), [16:18](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:18), [16:20](https://ref.ly/1Kgs16:20), [2 K 9:31](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:31). **4\.** a Benjamite [1 Ch 8:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:36), [8:36](https://ref.ly/1Chr8:36) \= [9:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:42), [9:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr9:42).

> \[Str2174] † II. זִמְרִי **n.pr.gent.** vel **part.,** only ´מַלְכֵי ז [Je 25:25](https://ref.ly/Jer25:25) (\+ מלכי עֵילָם and מלכי מָדָי); \= Σεμβρῖται in Ethiopia (Straboxvii, i. 786\) ? cf. Gf; om. LXX; Interpol. acc. to Kue Gie.

> \[Str2175] † זִמְרָן **n.pr.m.** son of Abraham \& Keturah, LXX Ζε(μ)βραν [Gn 25:2](https://ref.ly/Gen25:2) and [1 Ch 1:32](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:32) (LXXL here Ζεμραμ). On locality referred to v. Di.

> \[Str2160] † זְמִירָה **n.pr.m.** a Benjamite, [1 Ch 7:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr7:8), LXX Ἀμαριας; LXXA, LXXL Ζαμαρια(ς).

> \[Str2177] † זַן **n.\[m.]** (late) kind, sort (Aram. ܙܶܢ, זַן √ dub.; cf. De [Psalm 144:13](https://ref.ly/Ps144:13))—מִזַּן אֶל זַן [Psalm 144:13](https://ref.ly/Ps144:13) *from kind to kind*, i.e. *all sorts, kinds;* pl. וּזְנִים [2 Ch 16:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:14) (various) *sorts*.

[Jb 40:17](https://ref.ly/Job40:17); **b.** fig. of common people, opp. to rulers [Is 9:13](https://ref.ly/Isa9:13), [19:15](https://ref.ly/Isa19:15) (in both, ראֹשׁ וְזָנָב, ‖כִּפָּה וְאַגְמוֺן; [Is 9:14](https://ref.ly/Isa9:14) is incorrect gloss); of subject\-people (opp. ראֹשׁ) [Dt 28:13](https://ref.ly/Deut28:13), [28:44](https://ref.ly/Deut28:44). **2\.** *end, stump* (of firebrand, אוּד) in metaph. [Is 7:4](https://ref.ly/Isa7:4).

> \[Str2179] † \[זִנֵּב] **vb.denom. Pi.** cut off, or smite, the tail, only fig., of hostile attack in war; *Pf.* consec. 2 mpl. וְזִנַּבְתֶּם [Jos 10:19](https://ref.ly/Josh10:19) (sq. אֹתָם); *Impf.* 3 ms. וַיְזַנֵּב בְּךָ כָּל־הַנֶּחֱשָׁלִים [Dt 25:18](https://ref.ly/Deut25:18); in both \= *attack* or *smite in the rear*.

## I זָנַח (id: BDB02679)

\[Str2186] † I. זָנַח **vb.** reject, spurn (perh. cf. As. *zinû, be angry,* esp. of gods Gu§ 105 ZimBP 23 ff.)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ז [Ho 8:3](https://ref.ly/Hos8:3), [8:5](https://ref.ly/Hos8:5), [La 2:7](https://ref.ly/Lam2:7); זָנַחְתָּ [Psalm 44:10](https://ref.ly/Ps44:10) \+ 2 t.; sf. זְנַחְתָּנִי [Psalm 43:2](https://ref.ly/Ps43:2); זְנַחְתָּנוּ [Psalm 60:3](https://ref.ly/Ps60:3), [60:12](https://ref.ly/Ps60:12), [108:12](https://ref.ly/Ps108:12); זְנַחְתִּים [Zc 10:6](https://ref.ly/Zech10:6); *Impf.* יִזְנַח [Psalm 77:8](https://ref.ly/Ps77:8), [La 3:31](https://ref.ly/Lam3:31); תִּזְנַח [Psalm 44:24](https://ref.ly/Ps44:24) \+ 2 t.;—*reject*, Isr. rejects good [Ho 8:3](https://ref.ly/Hos8:3); Samaria's calf rejects her [Ho 8:5](https://ref.ly/Hos8:5) (others make ´י subj.; WeKl. Proph. rds. אזנת *I reject*), elsewh. God rejects people [Psalm 43:2](https://ref.ly/Ps43:2), [60:3](https://ref.ly/Ps60:3), [77:8](https://ref.ly/Ps77:8), [88:15](https://ref.ly/Ps88:15), [Zc 10:6](https://ref.ly/Zech10:6); לָנֶצַח [Psalm 44:24](https://ref.ly/Ps44:24), [74:1](https://ref.ly/Ps74:1); לעולם [La 3:31](https://ref.ly/Lam3:31); ´ולא תצא בצבאתינו ז [Psalm 44:10](https://ref.ly/Ps44:10), [60:12](https://ref.ly/Ps60:12), [108:12](https://ref.ly/Ps108:12); נַפְשִׁי מִשָּׁלוֺם [La 3:17](https://ref.ly/Lam3:17); king [Psalm 89:39](https://ref.ly/Ps89:39); altar [La 2:7](https://ref.ly/Lam2:7). **Hiph.** *Pf.* הִזְנִיחַ [2 Ch 29:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:19); sf. הִזְנִיחָם [2 Ch 11:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:14); 3 pl. הֶאֶזְנִיחוּ [Is 19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6), v. II. זנח; *Impf.* יַזְנִיחֲךָ [1 Ch 28:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:9); —(late) *reject* (\= earlier **Qal**), Jeroboam *rejects* the Levites ´מכהן לי [2 Ch 11:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr11:14); Ahaz the sacred vessels [2 Ch 29:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:19); ´י rejects Solomon [1 Ch 28:9](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:9).

* **Associated Passages:** Lamentations 3:31; Psalms 89:39; Psalms 108:12; Psalms 43:2; 2 Chronicles 29:19; Isaiah 19:6; Psalms 60:3; Lamentations 3:17; Psalms 60:12; Hosea 8:5; Zechariah 10:6; Psalms 88:15; 2 Chronicles 11:14; Psalms 44:10; 1 Chronicles 28:9; Psalms 77:8; Psalms 74:1; Psalms 44:24; Hosea 8:3; Lamentations 2:7
* **Associated Articles:** II זָנַח (ID: `BDB02680@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## II זָנַח (id: BDB02680)

\[Str2186] † II. \[זָנַח] stink, emit stench (cf. Ar. زَنَخَ *become rancid,* of oil, etc., Lane);—only **Hiph.** *Pf.* 3 mpl. הֶאֶזְנִיחוּ נְהָרוֺת *rivers stink* [Is 19:6](https://ref.ly/Isa19:6) (rd. הִזְנִיחוּ Ges§ 53, 6 O1:§ 255 b Sta§ 420 a Köi. 293; but Ew§ 123 b De Di derive from elative אַזְנָח or אֶזְנָח).

> \[Str2182] זָנוֺחַ **n.pr.loc. 1\.** *Zanu‘a*, SE. Ṣorea (RobBR ii. 61 BDPal 163\) [Jos 15:34](https://ref.ly/Josh15:34), [Ne 3:13](https://ref.ly/Neh3:13), [11:30](https://ref.ly/Neh11:30), [1 Ch 4:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:18). **2\.** a place in the mts. [Jos 15:56](https://ref.ly/Josh15:56), possibly *Zânûta*, SW. of Hebron, GuêrinJudêe iii. 200 Survey iii. 404; but Di thinks this is too far south.

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 4:18; Joshua 15:34; Nehemiah 3:13; Joshua 15:56; Nehemiah 11:30; Isaiah 19:6


## זָנַק (id: BDB02682)

\[Str2187] † \[זָנַק] **vb.** only **Pi.** leap (NH זִנֵּק *cause to spring, spurt;* Aram. ܙܢܰܩ *throw, shoot,* also *bind*)—*Impf.* 3 ms. יְזַנֵּק מִן־הַבָּשָׁן [Dt 33:22](https://ref.ly/Deut33:22) *he leapeth forth from Bashan* (of Dan under fig. of lion's whelp).

> \[Str2188] † זֵעָה v. יָזַע

> \[Str2189] זְעָוָה v. זְוָעָה sub זוּעַ

> \[Str2190] † זַעֲוָן **n.pr.m.** a descendant (branch or tribe) of Seir (LXX Ζουκαμ, but Ζαυαν LXXL 1 Ch [1:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42); Sam. זוען)—[Gn 36:27](https://ref.ly/Gen36:27) \= [1 Ch 1:42](https://ref.ly/1Chr1:42). p. 266

* **Associated Passages:** 1 Chronicles 1:42; Genesis 36:27; Deuteronomy 33:22
* **Associated Articles:** יזע (ID: `BDB03884@BDBHebrewLexicon`); זוּעַ (ID: `BDB02581@BDBHebrewLexicon`)


## זָעַךְ (id: BDB02686)

\[Str2193] † \[זָעַךְ] **vb.** extinguish, only **Niph.** *be extinguished* יָמַי נִזְעָכוּ [Jb 17:1](https://ref.ly/Job17:1) (‖רוּחִי חֻבָּלָה). Elsewhere always דעך (q.v.), and so in cogn. languages. Prob. error for נדעכו.

* **Associated Passages:** Job 17:1


## זָעַם (id: BDB02687)

\[Str2194] † זָעַם **vb.** be indignant (cf. Aram. (rare) ܙܥܰܡ *blame,* \& n. ܙܽܘܥܳܡܳܐ; Ar. تَزَغَّمَ onomatop., of *roar* of camel, *angry speech,* v. Frey, De on [Psalm 7:12](https://ref.ly/Ps7:12))—*Pf.* ´ז [Nu 23:8](https://ref.ly/Num23:8) \+ 3 t.; זָעַמְתָּה [Zc 1:12](https://ref.ly/Zech1:12); *Impf.* אֶזְעֹם [Nu 23:8](https://ref.ly/Num23:8); 3 pl. sf. יִזְעָמוּהוּ [Pr 24:24](https://ref.ly/Prov24:24); *Imv.* זֹעֲמָה [Nu 23:7](https://ref.ly/Num23:7) (for זָֽעָמֳה Ges§ 64, (2\)); *Pt. act.* זֹעֵם [Psalm 7:12](https://ref.ly/Ps7:12); *pass.* cstr. זְעוּם [Pr 22:14](https://ref.ly/Prov22:14); f. זְעוּמָה [Mi 6:10](https://ref.ly/Mic6:10); —**1\.** *be indignant, have indignation,* of hostile prince עַל בְּרִית קוֺדֶשׁ [Dn 11:30](https://ref.ly/Dan11:30); elsewhere of Yahweh, who is אֵל זֹעֵם [Psalm 7:12](https://ref.ly/Ps7:12) (‖שׁוֺפֵט), c. acc. of enemy [Is 66:14](https://ref.ly/Isa66:14); cities of Judah [Zc 1:12](https://ref.ly/Zech1:12); people [Mal 1:4](https://ref.ly/Mal1:4); a man [Pr 22:14](https://ref.ly/Prov22:14). **2\.** *express indignation in speech, denounce, curse* ‖יִקְּבֻהוּ [Pr 24:24](https://ref.ly/Prov24:24); ‖אֶקֹּב and אָֽרָ־ה [Nu 23:7](https://ref.ly/Num23:7), [23:8](https://ref.ly/Num23:8), [23:8](https://ref.ly/Num23:8) (E); זְעוּמָה אֵיפַת רָזוֺן [Mi 6:10](https://ref.ly/Mic6:10) *an ephah of scantiness, denounced,* or *cursed*. **Niph.** *Pt.* pl. פָּנִים נִזְעָמִים [Pr 25:23](https://ref.ly/Prov25:23) *face stirred with indignation*.

276:b. זָעַם **2** is prob. denom. fr. זַעַם (v. Buhl).

> \[Str2195] † זַ֫עַם **n.m.**:[Is 10:25](https://ref.ly/Isa10:25) indignation;—´ז [Is 10:25](https://ref.ly/Isa10:25) \+ 7 t.; זָעַָם [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20) \+ 2 t.; sf. זַעְמִי [Is 10:5](https://ref.ly/Isa10:5) \+ 3 t.; זַעַמְךָ [Psalm 102:11](https://ref.ly/Ps102:11); זַעְמֶָךָ [Psalm 38:4](https://ref.ly/Ps38:4), [69:25](https://ref.ly/Ps69:25); זַעְמוֺ [Is 13:5](https://ref.ly/Isa13:5) \+ 3 t.;—*indignation* of men [Ho 7:16](https://ref.ly/Hos7:16), [Je 15:17](https://ref.ly/Jer15:17); elsewh. of ´י [Is 26:20](https://ref.ly/Isa26:20), [30:27](https://ref.ly/Isa30:27), [Na 1:6](https://ref.ly/Nah1:6), Hb [3:12](https://ref.ly/Hab3:12), [Psalm 38:4](https://ref.ly/Ps38:4), [102:11](https://ref.ly/Ps102:11), [Dn 11:36](https://ref.ly/Dan11:36); ‖חרון אף [Psalm 69:25](https://ref.ly/Ps69:25) [Zp 3:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:8); ‖עברה [Psalm 78:49](https://ref.ly/Ps78:49), Ez [21:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:31), [22:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:31); ‖קצף [Je 10:10](https://ref.ly/Jer10:10), [Psalm 102:11](https://ref.ly/Ps102:11); ‖אף [Is 10:5](https://ref.ly/Isa10:5), [10:25](https://ref.ly/Isa10:25), [30:27](https://ref.ly/Isa30:27); cf. בזעם אפו [La 2:6](https://ref.ly/Lam2:6); על ´שׁפך ז [Psalm 69:25](https://ref.ly/Ps69:25), Ez [21:36](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:31), [22:31](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:31), [Zp 3:8](https://ref.ly/Zeph3:8); כְּלֵי זַעַם [Is 13:5](https://ref.ly/Isa13:5), [Je 50:25](https://ref.ly/Jer50:25); יום זעם Ez [22:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:24); אחרית הזעם [Dn 8:19](https://ref.ly/Dan8:19).

* **Associated Passages:** Zechariah 1:12; Isaiah 30:27; Ezekiel 22:31; Isaiah 26:20; Daniel 11:30; Psalms 102:11; Numbers 23:8; Daniel 11:36; Malachi 1:4; Isaiah 10:25; Ezekiel 22:24; Ezekiel 21:31; Zephaniah 3:8; Psalms 69:25; Jeremiah 15:17; Lamentations 2:6; Proverbs 22:14; Habakkuk 3:12; Hosea 7:16; Proverbs 25:23; Jeremiah 50:25; Isaiah 10:5; Psalms 78:49; Micah 6:10; Psalms 38:4; Isaiah 66:14; Psalms 7:12; Numbers 23:7; Nahum 1:6; Daniel 8:19; Isaiah 13:5; Jeremiah 10:10; Proverbs 24:24


## זַעַף (id: BDB02689)

\[Str2196] † \[זַעַף] **vb.** be out of humour, vexed; be enraged (Aram. ܙܥܰܦ *rage against*, זְעַף *to storm*, זַעֲפָא *storm;* cf. Sam. ࠆࠏࠐ *blow, breathe*)—**Qal** *Impf.* יִזְעַף [Pr 19:3](https://ref.ly/Prov19:3); וַיִּזְעַף [2 Ch 26:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:19); *Inf.* זַעְפּוֺ [2 Ch 26:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:19); *Pt.* pl. זֹעֲפִים [Gn 40:6](https://ref.ly/Gen40:6), [Dn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10); —**1\.** *be out of humour* [Gn 40:6](https://ref.ly/Gen40:6) (E); *dejected*, of face [Dn 1:10](https://ref.ly/Dan1:10); sq. עַל [Pr 19:3](https://ref.ly/Prov19:3) *fret against* (AV). **2\.** *be enraged* (late: cf. Aram. above) [2 Ch 26:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:19); sq. עִם v:[19](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:19).

> \[Str2197] † זַ֫עַף **n.m.** storming, raging, rage (poet. and late)—´ז abs. [2 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:10), [28:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:9); cstr. [Is 30:30](https://ref.ly/Isa30:30) \+ 2 t.; sf. זַעְפּוֺ [Jon 1:15](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:15); —**1\.** *rage* of king, leading to violence [Pr 19:12](https://ref.ly/Prov19:12) (‖כּפִיר), [2 Ch 16:10](https://ref.ly/2Chr16:10) (עִמּוֺ עַל־זאת ´בְּ; ‖כעס), [28:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr28:9); אַף ´ז [Is 30:30](https://ref.ly/Isa30:30) *raging* of anger (of ´י); ´י ´ז [Mi 7:9](https://ref.ly/Mic7:9). **2\.** *raging* of sea [Jon 1:15](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:15).

> \[Str2198] † זָעֵף **adj.** out of humour, vexed, only of Ahab [1 K 20:43](https://ref.ly/1Kgs20:43), [21:4](https://ref.ly/1Kgs21:4) (‖סַר).

* **Associated Passages:** Micah 7:9; Proverbs 19:3; 2 Chronicles 26:19; 1 Kings 20:43; 1 Kings 21:4; Proverbs 19:12; Jonah 1:15; Daniel 1:10; Genesis 40:6; 2 Chronicles 28:9; 2 Chronicles 16:10; Isaiah 30:30


## זָעַק (id: BDB02692)

\[Str2199] † \[זָעַק] **vb.** cry, cry out, call (‖ form of צעק q.v.; Ar. زَعَقَ, Aram. ܙܥܰܩ)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 fs. וְזָעָקָה [2 S 13:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:19); 1 S. זָעַקְתִּי [Psalm 142:6](https://ref.ly/Ps142:6); 3 pl. זָֽעֲקוּ Ju [6:7](https://ref.ly/Judg6:7) \+ 3 t.; וְזָעֲקוּ consec. Ju [11:11](https://ref.ly/Judg11:11) \+ 2 t.; 2 mpl. וּזְעַקְתֶּם consec. [1 S 8:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:18); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִזְעָק [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5); וַיִּזְעַק [1 S 7:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:9) \+ 5 t., etc.; *Imv.* זְעַק Ez [21:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:17); fs. זַעֲקְ־י [Is 14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31), cf. [Je 48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20) Kt; וְזַעֲקוּ Ju [10:14](https://ref.ly/Judg10:14) \+ 2 t.; וּזְעָקוּ [Je 48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20) Qr; *Inf. cstr.* מִזְּעֹק [1 S 7:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:8); וְלִזְעֹק [2 S 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:29); sf. זַעְקֶָךָ [Is 30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19); בְּזַעְקֵךְ [Is 57:13](https://ref.ly/Isa57:13) (O1:§ 245 b Ges§ 61\. 1\. R 1\);—**1\.** *call,* to one's aid, sq. acc. Pers. Ju [12:2](https://ref.ly/Judg12:2). **2\.** *cry, cry out,* in need: **a.** unto God (´י), sq. אֶ־ל Ju [3:9](https://ref.ly/Judg3:9), [3:15](https://ref.ly/Judg3:15), [6:6](https://ref.ly/Judg6:6), [6:7](https://ref.ly/Judg6:7), [10:10](https://ref.ly/Judg10:10), [1 S 7:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:8), [7:9](https://ref.ly/1Sam7:9) (\+ בעד of intercession), [12:8](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:8), [12:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:10), [15:11](https://ref.ly/1Sam15:11), [Mi 3:4](https://ref.ly/Mic3:4), [Je 11:11](https://ref.ly/Jer11:11), Hb [1:2](https://ref.ly/Hab1:2) (c. acc. חמס, cf. [Ho 8:2](https://ref.ly/Hos8:2) infr.), [2 Ch 20:9](https://ref.ly/2Chr20:9), [Psalm 22:6](https://ref.ly/Ps22:6), [107:13](https://ref.ly/Ps107:13), [107:19](https://ref.ly/Ps107:19), [142:6](https://ref.ly/Ps142:6), [Jo 1:14](https://ref.ly/Joel1:14); sq. ´אֶל־י \+ בְּקוֺל גָּדוֺל [Ne 9:4](https://ref.ly/Neh9:4); \+ קוֺלִי [Psalm 142:2](https://ref.ly/Ps142:2) (‖התחנן); \+ בְּלִבָּם [Ho 7:14](https://ref.ly/Hos7:14) (opp. הֵילִיל); sq. לְ [Ho 8:2](https://ref.ly/Hos8:2) (\+ obj. of words uttered), [1 Ch 5:20](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:20); sq. sf. [Ne 9:28](https://ref.ly/Neh9:28); sq. הַשָּׁמַיִם (without divine name) [2 Ch 32:20](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:20) (‖התפלל); abs., but with implication of cry to ´י [1 S 8:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:18), [Is 30:19](https://ref.ly/Isa30:19). **b.** unto other gods, sq. אֶ־ל Ju [10:14](https://ref.ly/Judg10:14), [Je 11:12](https://ref.ly/Jer11:12), [Jon 1:5](https://ref.ly/Jonah1:5). **c.** unto king, sq. אֶ־ל [2 S 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:29). **d.** abs., utterance of horror, anxiety, alarm, distress, sorrow, etc. [1 S 4:13](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:13), [5:10](https://ref.ly/1Sam5:10), [28:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:12) (\+ בקול גדול), [2 S 13:19](https://ref.ly/2Sam13:19), [19:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:5) (\+ קול גדול), [1 K 22:32](https://ref.ly/1Kgs22:32) \= [2 Ch 18:31](https://ref.ly/2Chr18:31), [Is 15:4](https://ref.ly/Isa15:4), [26:17](https://ref.ly/Isa26:17), [57:13](https://ref.ly/Isa57:13), [Je 20:8](https://ref.ly/Jer20:8) (‖קרא, cf. Hb [1:2](https://ref.ly/Hab1:2) supr.), [La 3:8](https://ref.ly/Lam3:8) (‖אֲשַׁוֵּעַ) Ez [9:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:8), [11:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek11:13), [27:30](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:30); also of cry heard by God [Ex 2:23](https://ref.ly/Exod2:23) (P); sq. לְ of that in behalf of, or for which one cries [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5), [Je 48:31](https://ref.ly/Jer48:31); so sq. עַ־ל [Je 30:15](https://ref.ly/Jer30:15); sq. acc. cogn. זְעָקָה [Est 4:1](https://ref.ly/Esth4:1); ‖היליל [Je 47:2](https://ref.ly/Jer47:2); and esp. Imv. [Is 14:31](https://ref.ly/Isa14:31), [Je 25:34](https://ref.ly/Jer25:34), [48:20](https://ref.ly/Jer48:20), Ez [21:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek21:17). **e.** *cry out* against one, sq. עַ־ל [Jb 31:38](https://ref.ly/Job31:38) *if my land cry out against me* (‖יִבְכָּיוּן); cf. also (abs., and without prep.) Hb [2:11](https://ref.ly/Hab2:11) *the stone shall cry out of* (מִן) *the wall*. **Niph.** *Pf.* 2 ms. נִזְעָקְתָּ Ju [18:23](https://ref.ly/Judg18:23); 3 mpl. נִזְעֲקוּ Ju [18:22](https://ref.ly/Judg18:22); *Impf.* וַיִּזָּעֵק Ju [6:34](https://ref.ly/Judg6:34) \+ 2 t.; וַיִּזָּעֲקוּ [Jos 8:16](https://ref.ly/Josh8:16); —*be called together, assemble, join* [Jos 8:16](https://ref.ly/Josh8:16) (JE), Ju [18:22](https://ref.ly/Judg18:22), [18:23](https://ref.ly/Judg18:23), [1 S 14:20](https://ref.ly/1Sam14:20); sq. אַחֲרָיו Ju [6:34](https://ref.ly/Judg6:34), [6:35](https://ref.ly/Judg6:35) *assembled after him,* i.e. joined him as his followers. **Hiph.** *Impf.* וַיַּזְעֵק Ju [4:10](https://ref.ly/Judg4:10) \+ 3 t.; יַזְעִיקוּ [Jb 35:9](https://ref.ly/Job35:9); *Imv.* הַזְעֶ־ק [2 S 20:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:4); *Inf. cstr.* לְהַזְעִיק [2 S 20:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:5); —**1\.** *call, call out,* or *together,* for military service, sq. acc. Ju [4:10](https://ref.ly/Judg4:10), [4:13](https://ref.ly/Judg4:13), [2 S 20:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:4), [20:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:5). **2\.** *make a crying* [Jb 35:9](https://ref.ly/Job35:9) (יְשַׁוְּעוּ in ‖ cl.) **3\.** *have proclamation made* ויאמר ´ויז [Jon 3:7](https://ref.ly/Jonah3:7). **4\.** *call out to, or at,* sq. acc. וַיַּזְעֵק אֹתִי וַיְדַבֵּר [Zc 6:8](https://ref.ly/Zech6:8) (si vera l.; Gr queries וַיָּעַר ?).

277:b. זָעַק **Niph.**: \= *be called out* for military service, pass. of **Hiph. 1\.**; cf. GFMJu [18:28](https://ref.ly/Judg18:28).

> \[Str2201] † זְעָקָה **n.f.** cry, outcry, abs. ´ז [Je 18:22](https://ref.ly/Jer18:22) \+ 5 t.; cstr. זַעֲקַת [Gn 18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20) \+ 3 t.; sf. זַעֲקָתִי [Jb 16:18](https://ref.ly/Job16:18); זַעֲקָתָם [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5) \+ 3 t.;—**1\.** *outcry,* against, זַעֲקַת ´סְדֹם וגו [Gn 18:20](https://ref.ly/Gen18:20) (J). **2\.** *cry of distress,* concerning something זַעֲקַת־שֶׁבֶר [Is 15:5](https://ref.ly/Isa15:5) (obj. of יְעֹעֵָרוּ; cf. בִּבְכִי in prev. cl.); abs. v:[8](https://ref.ly/Isa15:8) (subj. of הִקִּיפָה, ‖יְלָלָה); [Je 18:22](https://ref.ly/Jer18:22) (´תִּשָּׁמַע ז), [20:16](https://ref.ly/Jer20:16), [48:4](https://ref.ly/Jer48:4), [50:46](https://ref.ly/Jer50:46), [Ne 5:6](https://ref.ly/Neh5:6), [9:9](https://ref.ly/Neh9:9) (all c. שׁמע), [Je 48:34](https://ref.ly/Jer48:34) (jointed with נתן קול), [Jb 16:18](https://ref.ly/Job16:18), [Est 9:31](https://ref.ly/Esth9:31) (lamentation), cf. [4:1](https://ref.ly/Esth4:1) (גְּדוֺלָה וּמָרָה ´ז, acc. cogn. c. זעק); specif. זעקת־דָּל [Pr 21:13](https://ref.ly/Prov21:13) *cry of poor;* ´קול ז [Is 65:19](https://ref.ly/Isa65:19) (‖קול בכי), [Je 51:54](https://ref.ly/Jer51:54), Ez [27:28](https://ref.ly/Ezek27:28). **3\.** *outcry, clamour* זעקת מושׁל בַּכְּ סִילִים [Ec 9:17](https://ref.ly/Eccl9:17) (opp. דברי חכמים בְּנַחַת נשׁמעים).

## זער (id: BDB02694)

זער (Ar. زَعِرَ *be scanty,* of hair, plumage, etc.; Aram. ܙܥܰܪ, זְעַר *be* or *grow small;* cf. צער).

> \[Str2191] † זְעֵיר **n.\[m.]** a little (diminutive form O1:§ 180 LagBN 85\)—**1\.** of quantity, amount (of instruction) שָׁם ´שָׁם ז ´ז [Is 28:10](https://ref.ly/Isa28:10), [28:13](https://ref.ly/Isa28:13) (in both ‖צַו, קַו). **2\.** of time, וַאֲחֶַ\+וּךָּ ´כַּתַּר־לִי ז [Jb 36:2](https://ref.ly/Job36:2).

> \[Str4213] † מִזְעָר **n.\[m.]** a little, a trifle, a few. **1\.** of time עוֺד מְעַט מִזְעָר [Is 10:25](https://ref.ly/Isa10:25), [29:17](https://ref.ly/Isa29:17) *yet a trifle, a little* \= a very little while. **2\.** of number מְעַט מִזְעָר שְׁאָר [Is 16:14](https://ref.ly/Isa16:14) *a remnant, a very few* (opp. לוֺא כַּ בִּיר). In Is:3 without מעט:—ונשׁאר אנושׁ:מִזְעָֽר [Is 24:6](https://ref.ly/Isa24:6).

> \[Str2202] † \[זִפְרֹן] **n.pr.loc.** only c. ה loc. זִפְרֹ֫נָה [Nu 34:9](https://ref.ly/Num34:9), place on northern boundary of Canaan; LXX Δεφρωνα, Ἐφρωνα; LXXL Ζεφρωνα; site dub., WetzstHauran 88 prop. Ζιφρἀν, NE. fr. Damascus; FurrerZPV viii. 28 BDPal 397 (‘ perh. ’) *Za‘ferâne*, between Ḥumṣ and Hamath; Di rejects both.

> \[Str2203] † זֶ֫פֶת **n.f.**:[Is 34:9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9) pitch (cf. on format. Ol:§ 164 c) Sta§ 187 a. Word is Armenian acc. to LagArm. Stud. 1351, BN 219; on Egypt. deriv. cf. CookSpeaker's Comm. i. 484; Ar. زِفْتٌ acc. to Frä151, if Shemit., is Aram. loan\-wd.; v. also Eth. ዝፍት፡ DI1068)—*pitch:*—וַתַּחְמְרָה בַחֵמָר וּבַָ\+זּפֶת [Ex 2:3](https://ref.ly/Exod2:3) of ark of bulrushes; [Is 34:9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9) a וְנֶחֶפְכוּ נְחָלֶיהָ לְזֶפֶת וַעֲפָרָהּ לְגָפְרִָית, v:[9](https://ref.ly/Isa34:9) b :והיתה אַרְצָהּ לְזֶפֶת בֹּעֵרָֽה.

> \[Str2131] † I. \[זֵק] **n.\[m.]** missile, spark (√ dub.; cf. NH זִיקִין *fiery arrows*, Aram. זִיקָא *id.*, זִיקוּקָא *id.* \+ *spark*, ܙܺܝܩܳܐ *shooting star, ray of light*, ܙܺܝܩܬܳܐ *shooting star*)—only pl. זִקִּים of (fire\- ?) missiles [Pr 26:18](https://ref.ly/Prov26:18) (‖חִצִּים, c. ירה); so זִיקוֺת *sparks, brands* (as leaping, springing, forth ?) ´מְאַזְּרֵי ז (v. אזר) [Is 50:11](https://ref.ly/Isa50:11) (‖קֹדְחֵי אֵשׁ), בִּעַרְתֶּם ´ז v:[11](https://ref.ly/Isa50:11) (‖אוּר אֶשְׁכֶם).

> \[Str2131] II. \[זֵק], זִקִּים *fetters*, v. sub II. זקק. p. 279
