## זקן (id: BDB02701)

זקן (√ of foll.; meaning dubious).

> \[Str2206] † זָקָן **n.m.**:[2 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:5) \+ and **f.**:[Is 15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) \+ chin, beard (As. *ziḳnu*, AsrbAnnals iv. 29; Ar. ذَقَنٌ, Aram. דְּקַן, ܕܩܰܢ)—abs. ´ז [Lv 13:20](https://ref.ly/Lev13:20) \+; cstr. זְקַן [2 S 20:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:9) \+; sf. זְקָנִי [Ezr 9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:3); זְקֶָ\+נךָ [Lv 19:27](https://ref.ly/Lev19:27), Ez [5:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:1); זְקָנוֺ [1 S 21:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:14) \+ 2 t.; זְקַנְכֶם [2 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:5), [1 Ch 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:5); זְקָנָם [2 S 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:4), [Lv 21:5](https://ref.ly/Lev21:5); never. pl.—**1\.** *chin* (opp. ראֹשׁ, top of head) [Lv 13:29](https://ref.ly/Lev13:29), [13:30](https://ref.ly/Lev13:30), [14:9](https://ref.ly/Lev14:9) (all P), [2 S 20:9](https://ref.ly/2Sam20:9), Ez [5:1](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:1); cf. also [1 S 21:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam21:14), [Psalm 133:4](https://ref.ly/Ps133:4) (where however chin as *bearded* may be meant); chin, or lower jaw, of lion and bear [1 S 17:35](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:35). **2\.** *beard*, as growing (צמח) [2 S 10:5](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:5) \= [1 Ch 19:5](https://ref.ly/1Chr19:5); as cut off [Is 7:20](https://ref.ly/Isa7:20) (ספה), [15:2](https://ref.ly/Isa15:2) \= [Je 48:37](https://ref.ly/Jer48:37) (גרע); cf. מְגֻלְּחֵי זָקָן [Je 41:5](https://ref.ly/Jer41:5), [2 S 10:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam10:4) (גלח); שער ראשׁי זּזְקָנִי [Ezr 9:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra9:3); here belongs (ך)פאת זקנ [Lv 19:27](https://ref.ly/Lev19:27), [21:5](https://ref.ly/Lev21:5) (both H; opp. ראשׁ).

> \[Str2204] † זָקֵן **vb.** be or become old (cf., acc. to Thes, Ar. ذَقُونٌ *a she\-camel that lets her lower lip hang down*, v. Lane968; ذِقْنٌ *decrepit man*)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. זָקֵן [Gn 18:12](https://ref.ly/Gen18:12) \+ 14 t. (often hard to disting. fr. pred. adj. זָקֵן q.v.); 3 fs. זָֽקְנָה [Pr 23:22](https://ref.ly/Prov23:22); 2 ms. זָקַנְתָּ [1 S 8:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:5); זָקַ֫נְתָּה [Jos 13:1](https://ref.ly/Josh13:1); 1 S. זָקַנְתִּי [Jos 23:2](https://ref.ly/Josh23:2) \+ 3 t.; זָקַ\+נְתִּי [Gn 18:13](https://ref.ly/Gen18:13), [27:2](https://ref.ly/Gen27:2); *Impf.* וַיִּזְקַן [2 Ch 24:15](https://ref.ly/2Chr24:15); —*be* (or *become*) *old* [Gn 18:12](https://ref.ly/Gen18:12), [18:13](https://ref.ly/Gen18:13), [19:31](https://ref.ly/Gen19:31), [27:1](https://ref.ly/Gen27:1), [27:2](https://ref.ly/Gen27:2) (all J), [1 S 2:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:22), [4:18](https://ref.ly/1Sam4:18), [8:1](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:1), [8:5](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:5), [12:2](https://ref.ly/1Sam12:2), [17:12](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:12), [2 S 19:33](https://ref.ly/2Sam19:33), [1 K 1:1](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:1), [1:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs1:15), [2 K 4:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:14); also [Gn 24:1](https://ref.ly/Gen24:1) (‖בא בימים), so [Jos 13:1](https://ref.ly/Josh13:1), [13:1](https://ref.ly/Josh13:1), [23:1](https://ref.ly/Josh23:1), [23:2](https://ref.ly/Josh23:2) (‖ here בָּאתִי בימים), [1 Ch 23:1](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:1) (‖שָבַע ימים), [24:15](https://ref.ly/1Chr24:15) (‖ *id.*); [Pr 23:22](https://ref.ly/Prov23:22), [Psalm 37:25](https://ref.ly/Ps37:25) (opp. ‖היה נער); note esp. זָקַנְתִּי מִהְיוֺת לאישׁ [Ru 1:12](https://ref.ly/Ruth1:12) *I am too old to belong to a man (husband)*. **Hiph.** only *Impf.* 3 ms. יַזְקִין inchoat. *shew age, grow old* \= *senescere* Ew§ 122 c, cf. Di; of youth [Pr 22:6](https://ref.ly/Prov22:6); of tree\-root [Jb 14:8](https://ref.ly/Job14:8).

> \[Str2205] זָקֵן **adj.** old, abs. ´ז [Gn 19:4](https://ref.ly/Gen19:4) \+ 30 t. (on distinct. fr. vb. v. supr.); cstr. זְקַן [Gn 24:2](https://ref.ly/Gen24:2); pl. זְקֵנִים [Gn 18:11](https://ref.ly/Gen18:11) \+ 44 t.; זְקֵנוֺת [Zc 8:4](https://ref.ly/Zech8:4); cstr. זִקְנֵי [Gn 50:7](https://ref.ly/Gen50:7) \+ 85 t.; sf. זְקֵנַי [La 1:19](https://ref.ly/Lam1:19), זְקֵנֶיךָ [Dt 21:2](https://ref.ly/Deut21:2), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Deut32:7), זְקֵנָיו [Jos 8:33](https://ref.ly/Josh8:33) \+ 3 t., זְקֵנֶיהָ Ju [8:14](https://ref.ly/Judg8:14), זְקֵנֵינוּ [Ex 10:9](https://ref.ly/Exod10:9), [Jos 9:11](https://ref.ly/Josh9:11), זִקְנֵיכֶם [Dt 5:20](https://ref.ly/Deut5:20) \+ 2 t.;—**1\.** *old*, of human beings, as adj. pred. [Gn 18:11](https://ref.ly/Gen18:11) (J), [Jb 32:4](https://ref.ly/Job32:4) (מִמֶּנּוּ לְיָמִים ´ז, in compar.), or attrib. אָב זָקֵן [Gn 44:20](https://ref.ly/Gen44:20) (J), ´אִישׁ ז Ju [19:16](https://ref.ly/Judg19:16), [19:17](https://ref.ly/Judg19:17), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Judg19:20), [19:22](https://ref.ly/Judg19:22), ´נָבִיא ז [1 K 13:11](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:11), [13:25](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:25), [13:29](https://ref.ly/1Kgs13:29), ´מֶלֶךְ ז [Ec 4:13](https://ref.ly/Eccl4:13); prob. also וּשְבַע ימים ´ז [Gn 35:29](https://ref.ly/Gen35:29) (P), [Jb 42:17](https://ref.ly/Job42:17) and [Gn 25:8](https://ref.ly/Gen25:8) (P) acc. to LXX Sam Di; cf. also [Ezr 3:12](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:12). **2\.** usu. as subst.: **a.** *old man* (or woman), **b.** *elder;* **a.** *old man* [Gn 43:27](https://ref.ly/Gen43:27) (J), [Lv 19:32](https://ref.ly/Lev19:32) (H), [Dt 28:50](https://ref.ly/Deut28:50), [32:7](https://ref.ly/Deut32:7) (‖אָב), [1 S 2:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:31), [2:32](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:32), [28:14](https://ref.ly/1Sam28:14), [Is 47:6](https://ref.ly/Isa47:6), [Jb 12:20](https://ref.ly/Job12:20), [32:9](https://ref.ly/Job32:9), [Pr 17:6](https://ref.ly/Prov17:6), [20:29](https://ref.ly/Prov20:29), [Psalm 119:100](https://ref.ly/Ps119:100), [Jo 1:2](https://ref.ly/Joel1:2); *old men and old women* [Zc 8:4](https://ref.ly/Zech8:4), [8:4](https://ref.ly/Zech8:4); cf. זְקַן בֵּיתוֺ [Gn 24:2](https://ref.ly/Gen24:2) (J), זִקְנֵי בֵיתוֺ [2 S 12:17](https://ref.ly/2Sam12:17); esp. opp. נַעַר [Ex 10:9](https://ref.ly/Exod10:9) (E), [Is 3:5](https://ref.ly/Isa3:5), [20:4](https://ref.ly/Isa20:4), [Je 51:22](https://ref.ly/Jer51:22), [La 2:21](https://ref.ly/Lam2:21), [Psalm 148:12](https://ref.ly/Ps148:12), and in phr. מִנַּעַר וְעַד זָקֵן [Gn 19:4](https://ref.ly/Gen19:4) (J), [Jos 6:21](https://ref.ly/Josh6:21) (JE), [Est 3:13](https://ref.ly/Esth3:13); opp. ילדים [1 K 12:6](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:6), [12:8](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:8), [12:13](https://ref.ly/1Kgs12:13) \= [2 Ch 10:6](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:6), [10:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:8), [10:13](https://ref.ly/2Chr10:13); opp. בַּחוּרִים [Je 31:13](https://ref.ly/Jer31:13), Ez [9:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek9:6), [Jo 3:1](https://ref.ly/Joel3:1), cf. ## זָקַף (id: BDB02708)

\[Str2210] † \[זָקַף] **vb.** (late) raise up (NH *id.;* Aram. זְקַף, ܙܩܰܦ; As. *zaḳâpu*, AsrbHunting Inscr. IR 7, ix. A, 3; NöZMG 1886, 725 suggests Ar. زَقَفَ *carry off* (Ḳam.), orig. *lift up*)—only **Qal** *Pt. act.* and only fig. of ´י's dealing with prostrate men:—וְזוֺקֵף לְכָל־הַכְּפוּפִים [Psalm 145:14](https://ref.ly/Ps145:14) (‖סוֺמֵךְ); זֹקֵף כְּפוּפִים [146:8](https://ref.ly/Ps146:8) ´י *raiseth up prostrate ones*.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 145:14; Psalms 146:8


## I זָקַק (id: BDB02709)

\[Str2212] † I. \[זָקַק] **vb.** refine, purify (Aram. זְקַק; perh. kindr. with As. *zaḳiḳu, wind*, AsrbAnnals vi. 64 and Aram. ܙܺܝܩܳܐ, זִיקָּא *violent wind, violent rain with whirlwind*)—**Qal** *Impf.* 3 mpl. יָזֹ֫קּוּ [Jb 28:1](https://ref.ly/Job28:1) obj. זָהָב, [36:27](https://ref.ly/Job36:27) obj. מָטָר (v. Di). **Pi.** *Pf.* וְזִקַּק consec. [Mal 3:3](https://ref.ly/Mal3:3) (‖וְטִהַר) fig., of purifying sons of Levi, like gold and silver כְּזָהָב etc. **Pu.** *Pt.* מְזֻקָּק *refined*, of gold [1 Ch 28:18](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:18), of silver [1 Ch 29:4](https://ref.ly/1Chr29:4): [Psalm 12:7](https://ref.ly/Ps12:7) (שׁבעתים ´מ); of settled wines שְׁמָרִים מְזֻקָּקִים [Is 25:6](https://ref.ly/Isa25:6).

279:a. I. \[זָקַק]: perh. prop. *strain* or *filter through*, cf. [Jb 36:27](https://ref.ly/Job36:27), [Is 25:6](https://ref.ly/Isa25:6).

## זָרַב (id: BDB02715)

\[Str2215] † \[זָרַב] **vb.** only **Pu.**, meaning dub.; prob. (from context) be burnt, scorched—**Pu.** *Impf.* in rel. cl., בְּעֵת יְזֹֽרְבוּ נִצְמָתוּ [Jb 6:17](https://ref.ly/Job6:17) (‖בְּחֻמּוֺ נִדְעֲכוּ מִמְּקוֺמָם), of brooks *scorched* and drying up (‖ form of צָרַב q.v.; so Ew Di De Hoffm Kau AV RV MV; \>Thes RobGes who comp. Ar. زَرِبَ *flow away*, cf. NH זְרִיבָה; and Mich DlPr 36 f. VBm (*are straitened*) RVm (*shrink*) cf. Aram. ܙܪܰܒ *compress*, (Ar. زَرَبَ is to *make a wooden enclosure*), As. *zarâbu*, ZimBP 32 n. 56, 70, 95\).

> \[Str2216] † זְרֻבָּבֶ֫ל **n.pr.m.** (acc. to MV perh. \= זְרוּעַ בָּבֶל *begotten* in Babylon, v. also Thes)—grandson of king Jehoiachin, and son of Pedaiah [1 Ch 3:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:19), [3:19](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:19) (but son of Shealtiel [Ezr 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:2), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:8) etc., cf. שְׁאַלְתִּיאֵל brother (?) of פְּדָיָה [1 Ch 3:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr3:17)); leader of first returning exiles [Ezr 2:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:2) \= [Ne 7:7](https://ref.ly/Neh7:7), [Ezr 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:2), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra3:8), [4:2](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:2), [4:3](https://ref.ly/Ezra4:3), [Ne 12:1](https://ref.ly/Neh12:1), [12:47](https://ref.ly/Neh12:47), [Hg 1:12](https://ref.ly/Hag1:12), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Hag2:4), [Zc 4:6](https://ref.ly/Zech4:6), [4:7](https://ref.ly/Zech4:7), [4:9](https://ref.ly/Zech4:9), [4:10](https://ref.ly/Zech4:10), called פַּחַת יְהוּדָה [Hg 1:1](https://ref.ly/Hag1:1), [1:14](https://ref.ly/Hag1:14), [2:2](https://ref.ly/Hag2:2), [2:21](https://ref.ly/Hag2:21), called עַבְדִּי (by ´י) [Hg 2:23](https://ref.ly/Hag2:23).—LXX Ζοροβαβελ.
>

## זָרָה (id: BDB02718)

\[Str2219,4215] † \[זָרָה] **vb.** scatter, fan, winnow (Ar. دَرَاء *cause to fly, scatter* (of wind), *winnow;* Aram. דְּרָא, ܕܪܳܐ; Eth. ዘረወ፡—**Qal** *Impf.* וַיִּ֫זֶר [Ex 32:20](https://ref.ly/Exod32:20); 2 ms. תִּזְרֶה Ez [5:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:2); sf. תִּזְרֵם [Is 30:22](https://ref.ly/Isa30:22) [41:16](https://ref.ly/Isa41:16); וָאֶזְרֵם [Je 15:7](https://ref.ly/Jer15:7); *Imv.* זְרֵה [Nu 17:2](https://ref.ly/Num17:2); *Inf.* לִזְרוֺת [Je 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jer4:11); *Pt.* זֹרֶה [Ru 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:2), [Is 30:24](https://ref.ly/Isa30:24); —**1\.** *scatter*, the powder into which golden calf was ground [Ex 32:20](https://ref.ly/Exod32:20) (E, obj. not expr.); c. acc. (strange) fire from censers [Nu 17:2](https://ref.ly/Num17:2) (P); hair (symbol. act) Ez [5:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:2); [Is 30:22](https://ref.ly/Isa30:22). **2\.** *fan, winnow*, obj. גֹּרֶן הַשְּעוֺרִים [Ru 3:2](https://ref.ly/Ruth3:2), [Is 30:24](https://ref.ly/Isa30:24) (where *pt.* has indef. subj.), [41:16](https://ref.ly/Isa41:16) metaph., obj. mountains and hills, fig. of winnowing; fig. fan, winnow, in purification [Je 4:11](https://ref.ly/Jer4:11) (‖לְהָכַָר); in chastisement [Je 15:7](https://ref.ly/Jer15:7) (בְּמִזְרֶה ´ז). **Niph.** *Impf.* וַיִּזָּרוּ Ez [36:19](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:19) *be scattered, dispersed*, of Israel; *Inf.* בְּהִזָּרוֺתֵיכֶם Ez [6:8](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:8) *when ye shall be dispersed* (on pl. sf. Ew§ 259 b, but Co del. י). **Pi.** *Pf.* sf. וְזֵרָם [1 K 14:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:15); זִרֵָיתָ [Psalm 139:3](https://ref.ly/Ps139:3), sf. זֵירִיתָ֫נוּ [Psalm 44:12](https://ref.ly/Ps44:12); וְזֵרִיתִ֫י Ez [5:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:10) etc.; *Inf.* זָרוֺת Ez [20:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:23) etc.;—**1\.** (intens. of Qal) *scatter, disperse*, esp. of peoples, c. acc. (Israel and Judah) [1 K 14:15](https://ref.ly/1Kgs14:15), [Lv 26:33](https://ref.ly/Lev26:33) (H), [Psalm 44:12](https://ref.ly/Ps44:12), [Je 31:10](https://ref.ly/Jer31:10), Ez [5:10](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:10), [5:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek5:12), [12:14](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:14), [12:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek12:15), [20:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:23), [22:15](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:15) cf. [Zc 2:2](https://ref.ly/Zech2:2), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Zech2:4), [2:4](https://ref.ly/Zech2:4), [Psalm 106:27](https://ref.ly/Ps106:27); bones of Israel Ez [6:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek6:5); people of Hazor [Je 49:32](https://ref.ly/Jer49:32); of Elam [49:36](https://ref.ly/Jer49:36); of Babylon [51:2](https://ref.ly/Jer51:2) (זָרִים \= strangers ?); Egyptians Ez [29:12](https://ref.ly/Ezek29:12), [30:26](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:26) \+ [30:23](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:23) (del. Co, intern. grounds); in general the wicked [Pr 20:26](https://ref.ly/Prov20:26); evil [20:8](https://ref.ly/Prov20:8); pt. מְזָרִים \= subst. *scatterers*, epith. of winds [Jb 37:9](https://ref.ly/Job37:9) (cf. Qor 51:1\); וְזֵרִיתִי פֶּרֶשׁ על־פניכם [Mal 2:3](https://ref.ly/Mal2:3) *scatter* dung on your faces (token of shame and curse); fig. *disperse* knowledge (of lips) [Pr 15:7](https://ref.ly/Prov15:7). **2\.** *winnow, sift* (cf. **Qal 2**) only fig. אָרְחִי וְרִבְעִי זֵרִָיתָ [Psalm 139:3](https://ref.ly/Ps139:3) *my path and my couch thou siftest* (Che; BaZMG 1887, 607 *measurest, determinest*, whence זֶרֶת q.v.) **Pu.** *be scattered,Impf.* יְזֹרֶה [Jb 18:15](https://ref.ly/Job18:15), subj. גָּפְרִית sq. עַל; *Pt.* f. מְזֹרָה (agreeing with רָשֶָׁת *net*) [Pr 1:17](https://ref.ly/Prov1:17).

> \[Str4214] † מִזְרֶה **n.\[m.]** pitch\-fork, with six prongs (\= مذراة) used in winnowing, cf. WetzstZPV 1891, xiv. 2; for winnowing provender ´זֹרֶה בָרַחַת וּבְמ [Is 30:24](https://ref.ly/Isa30:24); fig. for winnowing, i.e. chastising the people ´וָאֶזְרֵם בְּמ [Je 15:7](https://ref.ly/Jer15:7).

> \[Str2222] זַרְזִיף [Psalm 72:6](https://ref.ly/Ps72:6) v. זרף

> \[Str2223] זַרְזִיר [Pr 30:31](https://ref.ly/Prov30:31) v. sub III. זוּר. p. 267

DHMZMG 1875, 605; 1883, 327 MordtZMG 1876, 37\)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ז [Dt 33:2](https://ref.ly/Deut33:2), [Psalm 112:4](https://ref.ly/Ps112:4); זָרָח [Is 60:1](https://ref.ly/Isa60:1); וְזָרַח consec. [Is 58:10](https://ref.ly/Isa58:10), [Ec 1:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:5); 3 fs. זָרְֽחָה [Ex 22:2](https://ref.ly/Exod22:2) \+ 3 t.; וְזָרְֽחָה consec. [Mal 3:20](https://ref.ly/Mal3:20); *Impf.* יִזְרַח [2 S 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:4), [Is 60:2](https://ref.ly/Isa60:2); יִזְרָח [Jb 9:7](https://ref.ly/Job9:7); וַיִּזְרַח [Gn 32:32](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32); 3 fs. תִּזְרח [Psalm 104:22](https://ref.ly/Ps104:22); *Inf. cstr.* זְרֹחַ Ju [9:33](https://ref.ly/Judg9:33), [Jo 4:8](https://ref.ly/Joel4:8); *Pt.* זוֺרֵחַ [Ec 1:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:5); —**1\.** *rise:* **a.** (of sun) Ju [9:33](https://ref.ly/Judg9:33), [Na 3:7](https://ref.ly/Nah3:7), [2 S 23:4](https://ref.ly/2Sam23:4), [Jon 4:8](https://ref.ly/Jonah4:8), [Jb 9:7](https://ref.ly/Job9:7), [Psalm 104:22](https://ref.ly/Ps104:22), [Ec 1:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:5), [1:5](https://ref.ly/Eccl1:5); sq. על [Ex 22:2](https://ref.ly/Exod22:2) (JE), [2 K 3:22](https://ref.ly/2Kgs3:22); sq. לְ [Gn 32:32](https://ref.ly/Gen32:32) (J). **b.** fig., sq. לְ, שֶׁמֶשׁ צְדָקָה [Mal 3:20](https://ref.ly/Mal3:20); of ´י, וְזָרַח מִשֵּעִיר לָמוֺ [Dt 33:2](https://ref.ly/Deut33:2); sq. על, [Is 60:2](https://ref.ly/Isa60:2) (‖כבודו יראה); ´כבוד י [Is 60:1](https://ref.ly/Isa60:1); subj. אוֺד, fig. ( \= happiness, blessedness) [Is 58:10](https://ref.ly/Isa58:10), [Psalm 112:4](https://ref.ly/Ps112:4) (sq. לְ). **2\.** *come out, appear*, of appearance of leprosy (הַצָּרַ֫עַת) [2 Ch 26:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:19).

H; opp. *id.*), [26:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:17) (\+ לַצָּפ֫וֺנָה, לַנֶּ֫גְבָּה), [Ne 3:26](https://ref.ly/Neh3:26); לַמִּזְרָ֫חָה [2 Ch 31:14](https://ref.ly/2Chr31:14); (4\) ´אֶל־הַמּ [Dn 8:9](https://ref.ly/Dan8:9) (\+ אֶל־הַנֶּגֶב, אֶל־הַצֶּבִָי); (5\) מִיָּם עָד־יָם וּמִצָּפוֺן עַד־מִזְ רָךְ [Am 8:12](https://ref.ly/Amos8:12). **d.** (on) *the east side* of, cstr. קְצֵה מִזְרַה יְרִיחוֺ [Jos 4:19](https://ref.ly/Josh4:19) (P); also c. לְמִזְרַח הַָ\+נּיְא לְ [1 Ch 4:39](https://ref.ly/1Chr4:39), הַיַּרְדֵּן ´לְמ [6:63](https://ref.ly/1Chr6:63); c. מִן, ´מִמִּז [Jos 11:3](https://ref.ly/Josh11:3) (opp. מִיָּם) cf. [17:10](https://ref.ly/Josh17:10) (\+ מצָּפוֺן; both JE), also [16:6](https://ref.ly/Josh16:6) (P). **e.** after a cstr. לגלעד ´עַל־כָּל־פְּנֵי מ [1 Ch 5:10](https://ref.ly/1Chr5:10); ´רְחוֺב הַמּ [2 Ch 29:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:4); ´שַׁעַר הַמּ [Ne 3:29](https://ref.ly/Neh3:29); ´אֶרֶץ מ [Zc 8:7](https://ref.ly/Zech8:7) (opp. ארץ מְבוֺא הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ).

## זָרַם (id: BDB02732)

\[Str2229] † \[זָרַם] **vb.** pour forth in floods, flood away (As. *zarâmu, overwhelm*, VR36, 57 c DlPr 73 \& in ZimBP 119\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 2 ms. sf. זְרַמְתָּם [Psalm 90:5](https://ref.ly/Ps90:5) *thou floodest them with rain, sweepest them* (men) *away*. **Po.** *Pf.* 3 pl. זֹ֫רְמוּ מַ֫יִם עָבוֺת [Psalm 77:18](https://ref.ly/Ps77:18) the *cloud masses* (Che) *poured forth water*.

> \[Str2230] † זֶ֫רֶם **n.m.**:Hb. [3:10](https://ref.ly/Hab3:10) flood of rain, rain\-storm, downpour (Aram. זַרְמִית *id.*)—abs. ´ז [Is 4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6) \+ 2 t.; ָ\+זרֶם [Is 32:2](https://ref.ly/Isa32:2); cstr. זֶרֶם [Jb 24:8](https://ref.ly/Job24:8) \+ 4 t.; [Is 4:6](https://ref.ly/Isa4:6) (‖מָטָר)); מַיִם ´ז [Is 28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2), Hb [3:10](https://ref.ly/Hab3:10); הָרִם ´ז [Jb 24:8](https://ref.ly/Job24:8), i.e. such as falls among mts. cf. Di; בָּרָד ´ז [Is 28:2](https://ref.ly/Isa28:2); מַחְסֶה מִזֶּרֶם [25:4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4), זֶרֶם קִיר v:[4](https://ref.ly/Isa25:4) i.e. such as dashes against a wall, [32:2](https://ref.ly/Isa32:2) (‖רוּחַ), נֶפֶץ וָזֶרֶם וְאֶבֶן בָּרָד [30:30](https://ref.ly/Isa30:30).

> \[Str2231] † זִרְמָה **n.f.** issue (of *semen virile*), in fig. of the paramours of Jerusalem וְזִרְמַת סוּסִים זִרְמָתָם Ez [23:20](https://ref.ly/Ezek23:20).

* **Associated Passages:** Isaiah 32:2; Isaiah 4:6; Job 24:8; Isaiah 25:4; Psalms 77:18; Isaiah 28:2; Ezekiel 23:20; Psalms 90:5; Habakkuk 3:10; Isaiah 30:30


## I זָרַע (id: BDB02735)

\[Str2232] † I. זָרַע **vb.** sow, scatter seed (NH *id.;* As. *zirû* TPPrism vi. 15; Ar. زَرَعَ; Aram. ܙܪܰܥ, זְרַע; Eth. ዘርእ፡ ዘርዕ but in Sab. דַרא DHMZMG 1883, 15 and in n.pr., Id. in MV10, pp. 242, 983\)—**Qal** *Pf.* 3 ms. זָרַע Ju [6:3](https://ref.ly/Judg6:3); 1 S. וְזָרַעְתִּ֫י [Je 31:27](https://ref.ly/Jer31:27); sf. וּזְרַעְתִּיהָ consec [Ho 2:25](https://ref.ly/Hos2:25); 3 pl. זָֽרְעוּ [Je 12:13](https://ref.ly/Jer12:13); 2 mpl. זְרַעַתֶּם [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6); וּזְרַעְתֶּם consec. [Gn 47:23](https://ref.ly/Gen47:23) \+ 2 t.; *Impf.* יִזְרָע [Ec 11:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:4); וַיִּזְרַע [Gn 26:12](https://ref.ly/Gen26:12); sf. וַיִּזְרָעֶהָ Ju [9:45](https://ref.ly/Judg9:45); 1 S. אֶזְדְעָה Jb [31:8](https://ref.ly/Job31:8); sf. וְאֶזְרָעֵם [Zc 10:9](https://ref.ly/Zech10:9), etc.; *Imv.* ms. זְרַע [Ec 11:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:6); mpl. זִרְעוּ [Ho 10:12](https://ref.ly/Hos10:12) \+2 t.; *Inf.* לִזְרֹעַ [Is 28:24](https://ref.ly/Isa28:24); *Pt. act.* זֹרֵעַ [Is 55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10) \+ 3 t.; זזֹרֵעַ [Pr 22:8](https://ref.ly/Prov22:8), [Je 50:16](https://ref.ly/Jer50:16), etc.; *Pt. pass.* זָרֻעַ [Psalm 97:11](https://ref.ly/Ps97:11) (but on text v. infr.); f. זְרוּעָה [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2); —**1\.** lit. *sow:* **a.** abs. *sow* (seed), do one's sowing Ju [6:3](https://ref.ly/Judg6:3), [Gn 26:12](https://ref.ly/Gen26:12) (J), [Is 28:24](https://ref.ly/Isa28:24) (after ploughing חרשׁ); זִרְעוּ וקצרו ונטעו כרמים [Is 37:30](https://ref.ly/Isa37:30) \= [2 K 19:29](https://ref.ly/2Kgs19:29); ‖קצר also [Mi 6:15](https://ref.ly/Mic6:15), [Lv 25:11](https://ref.ly/Lev25:11) (H), [Ec 11:4](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:4); ‖אסף [Lv 25:20](https://ref.ly/Lev25:20) (H); opp. אכל [Jb 31:8](https://ref.ly/Job31:8); זֹרְעֵי על־כל־מים [Is 32:20](https://ref.ly/Isa32:20) (on cstr. cf. Ges§ 130, 1\); c. את־השּׁנה הַשׁמינִת [Lv 25:22](https://ref.ly/Lev25:22); sq. הַרְבֵּה [Hg 1:6](https://ref.ly/Hag1:6) (opp. הָבֵא מְעָט). **b.** c. acc. of land אדמה [Ex 23:10](https://ref.ly/Exod23:10) (JE; ‖אסף), [Gn 47:23](https://ref.ly/Gen47:23) (J); field (שדה) [Lv 25:3](https://ref.ly/Lev25:3) (‖אסף), cf. v:[4](https://ref.ly/Lev25:4), [Psalm 107:37](https://ref.ly/Ps107:37); as pt. pass. אֶרֶץ לאֹ זְרוּעָה [Je 2:2](https://ref.ly/Jer2:2) (‖מִדְבָּר). **c.** c. acc. of seed אֶת־זַרְעֲךָ ´ז [Dt 11:10](https://ref.ly/Deut11:10), cf. [22:9](https://ref.ly/Deut22:9), [Je 35:7](https://ref.ly/Jer35:7), [Lv 26:16](https://ref.ly/Lev26:16) (H) [Ec 11:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:6); sq. חִטִּים [Je 12:13](https://ref.ly/Jer12:13) (‖קצר); cf. also בִּכּוּרֵי מַעֲשֶיךָ אשׁר תזרע בַּשָּדֶה [Ex 23:16](https://ref.ly/Exod23:16) (JE). **d.** sq. 2 acc. לאֹ תזרע כַּרְמְךָ כִּלְאַָיִם [Dt 22:9](https://ref.ly/Deut22:9); cf. שָדְךָ לאֹ תִזְרַע כִּלְאַיִם [Lv 19:19](https://ref.ly/Lev19:19) (H); זַרְעֲךָ אשׁר־תזדע את־האדמה [Is 30:23](https://ref.ly/Isa30:23); also of destroyed city וַיִּזְרָעֶהָ מֶָלַח Ju [9:45](https://ref.ly/Judg9:45). **e.** pt. act. as subst. זוֺרֵעַ [Je 50:16](https://ref.ly/Jer50:16) (‖תֹּפֵש מַגָּל בְּעֵת קָציר); לַזֹּרֵעַ [Is 55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10) (‖לָאֹכֵל). **2\.** of shrub and tree *producing, yielding seed*, c. acc. cogn. [Gn 1:29](https://ref.ly/Gen1:29), [1:29](https://ref.ly/Gen1:29) (P), cf. **Hiph. 3\.** fig.: **a.** of Yahweh's *sowing* (planting) Isr. in the land וזרעתיה לי בָארץ [Ho 2:25](https://ref.ly/Hos2:25) (We וזרעתיהו, obj. being Jezreel); on the other hand וְאֶזְרָעֵם בָּעַמִּים [Zc 10:9](https://ref.ly/Zech10:9). **b.** c. 2 acc., of Yahweh's fructifying Isr. and Judah יהודה זֶרַע אדם וזרע ´וזרעתי את־בית ישראל ואת־ב בהמה [Je 31:27](https://ref.ly/Jer31:27). **c.** of moral action, זִרְעוּ לָכֶם לִצְדָקָה [Ho 10:12](https://ref.ly/Hos10:12) (‖קצר); צדקה´ז [Pr 11:18](https://ref.ly/Prov11:18); אַל־תִּזְרְעוּ אֶל־קֹצִים [Je 4:3](https://ref.ly/Jer4:3) *sow not in among thorns* (said of a few righteous deeds amid much wickedness); esp. of evil\-doing רוּחַ יזרעו וְסוּפָתָה יִקְצָֹרוּ [Ho 8:7](https://ref.ly/Hos8:7); זֹרְעֵי עָמָל [Jb 4:8](https://ref.ly/Job4:8) (‖קצר); זוֺרֵעַ עַוְלָה יקצור־אָוֶן [Pr 22:8](https://ref.ly/Prov22:8). **d.** of Israel's practising idolatry זְמֹרַת זָר תִּזְרָעֶָנּוּ [Is 17:10](https://ref.ly/Isa17:10) (2 acc.) **e.** הזרעים בדמעה בְּרִנָּה יִקְצָֹרוּ [Psalm 126:5](https://ref.ly/Ps126:5) metaph. of distress followed by joy. **f.** אוֺר זָרֻעַ לַצַּדִּיק [Psalm 97:11](https://ref.ly/Ps97:11) rd. prob. זָרַח, Vrss Ol Hu Bae Che al.

 **Niph.** be sown, fructified, *Pf.* 3 fs. וְנִזְרְעָה consec. [Nu 5:28](https://ref.ly/Num5:28); 2 mpl. וְנִזְרַעְתֶּם consec. Ez [36:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:9); *Impf.* 3 ms. יִזָּרַע [Na 1:14](https://ref.ly/Nah1:14); יִזָּרֵָעַ [Dt 21:4](https://ref.ly/Deut21:4), [Lv 11:37](https://ref.ly/Lev11:37); 3 fs. תִּזָּרַע [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22); —**1\.** *be sown:* **a.** subj. אֶרֶץ, ולא תַּצְמִחַ ´לאֹ תִזּ [Dt 29:22](https://ref.ly/Deut29:22), cf. (of נַחַכ אֵיתָן) לא יֵעָבֵּד ´בּוֺ ולא יִזּ [Dt 21:4](https://ref.ly/Deut21:4); in address to הָרֵי ישראל, וְנֶעֱבַּדְתֶּם וְנִזְרַעְתֶּם Ez [36:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:9). **b.** subj. זֶרַע, זֶרַע זֵרוּעַ אשׁר יִזָּרֵעַ [Lv 11:37](https://ref.ly/Lev11:37) (P); fig. מִשִּׁמְךָ עוֺד ´לאֹ יִזּ [Na 1:14](https://ref.ly/Nah1:14) *no more of thy name be sown*, in judgment against Nineveh. **2\.** *be fructified, made pregnant* וְנִזְרְעָה זָרַָע [Nu 5:28](https://ref.ly/Num5:28) (P; of woman). **Pu.** *Pf.* 3 pl. זֹרָעוּ [Is 40:24](https://ref.ly/Isa40:24) of princes and judges under fig. of trees (‖נִטָּעוּ, שֹׁרֵשׁ בָּאָרֶץ גִּזְעָם). **Hiph.** *Impf.* 3 fs. תַּזְרִיעַ [Lv 12:2](https://ref.ly/Lev12:2); *Pt.* מַזְרִיעַ [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12); —**1\.** *produce seed*, of herb, c. acc. cogn. [Gn 1:11](https://ref.ly/Gen1:11), [1:12](https://ref.ly/Gen1:12); appar. denom. fr. זֶרַע; cf. **Qal 2\. 2\.** of a woman, \= *bear a child* תַּזְרִיעַ וְיָלְֽדָה זָכָר [Lv 12:2](https://ref.ly/Lev12:2). 

> \[Str2233] † זֶ֫רַע **n.m.**:[Dt. 28:38](https://ref.ly/Deut28:38) sowing, seed, offspring (NH *id.*, Aram. זְרַע, ܙܪܰܥ; Ph. זרע; Zinj. *id.;* Ar. زَرْعٌ *seed\-produce, seed;* Eth. ዘርእ፡ As. *zêru, cultivated land*, BelserBAS ii. 130, cf. Ar. زَرْعَةٌ)—abs. ´ז [Gn 47:23](https://ref.ly/Gen47:23) \=; ָ\+זרַע [Gn 1:29](https://ref.ly/Gen1:29) \+; cstr. זֶרַע [Is 5:10](https://ref.ly/Isa5:10) \+; זְרַ־ע [Nu 11:7](https://ref.ly/Num11:7); sf. זַרְעִי [1 S 20:42](https://ref.ly/1Sam20:42), [24:22](https://ref.ly/1Sam24:22); זַרְעֲךָ [Dt 11:10](https://ref.ly/Deut11:10) \+; זַרְעֶָךָ [Ec 11:6](https://ref.ly/Eccl11:6) \+; etc.; pl. sf. זַרְעֵיכֶם [1 S 8:15](https://ref.ly/1Sam8:15); —**1\.** lit.: **a.** a *sowing* הַשָּדֶה ´לְז [Gn 47:24](https://ref.ly/Gen47:24) (J; \+ אָכְלְכֶם; otherwise [Lv 27:16](https://ref.ly/Lev27:16) b, v. infr. **2 c**); ´לאֹ מְקוֺם ז [Nu 20:5](https://ref.ly/Num20:5) *no place for sowing* (JE; \+ וּתְאֵנָה וְגֶפֶן וְרִמּוֺן); cf. ´שְדֵה ז Ez [17:5](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:5) *a field* suitable *for sowing;* hence **b.** *sowing* as regularly recurring at its season [Gn 8:22](https://ref.ly/Gen8:22) (J; ‖קָצִיר, \+ קֹר וָחֹם וְקַיִץ וָחֹרֶף וְיוֺם וָלַיְלָה), [Lv 26:5](https://ref.ly/Lev26:5) (H; ‖דַּיִשׁ, בָּצִיר;—cf. [Am 9:13](https://ref.ly/Amos9:13) infr. **2 a**). **2\.** *seed:* **a.** lit., sown, to raise crops for food, usu. corn (wheat, barely, etc.) [Gn 47:19](https://ref.ly/Gen47:19), [47:23](https://ref.ly/Gen47:23) (J; cf. v:[14](https://ref.ly/Gen47:14) f.), [Nu 24:7](https://ref.ly/Num24:7) (JE); נָתַן לַזֹּרֵעַ ´ז [Is 55:10](https://ref.ly/Isa55:10) (‖לֶחֶם), [Dt 28:38](https://ref.ly/Deut28:38) (רַב ´ז), [Lv 26:16](https://ref.ly/Lev26:16) (H; c. זָרַע), [27:16](https://ref.ly/Lev27:16) a, חֹמֶר שְעֹרִים ´ז v:[16](https://ref.ly/Lev27:16) b (both P), cf. > \[Str2221] † זֵרוּעַ **n.\[m.]** sowing, thing sown (intensive format. acc. to Ol:§ 186 b Sta§ 228, yet on this and foll. cf. BaNB 42, 178 Anm. 4\)—´זֶרַע ז [Lv 11:37](https://ref.ly/Lev11:37) (P), כְּגַנָּה זֵֽרוּעֶיהָ חַחְמִיךְַ [Is 61:11](https://ref.ly/Isa61:11) in sim. of Yahweh's causing righteousness to spring up.

## II זרע (id: BDB02743)

II. זרע (cf. Ar. ذَرَعَ *stretch out, extend,* esp. arm or leg; ذَرْعٌ *power,* ذِرَاعٌ *forearm*).

> \[Str2220] † זְרוֺעַ, זְרֹעַ **n.f.**:[Ex 6:6](https://ref.ly/Exod6:6) and (rare) **m.**:[Is 51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5), cf. infr. arm, shoulder, strength (NH *id.;* As. (Tel el Amarna102, 1\. 12, etc.) *zurû*, cf. JastrowJBL. xi, 1892, 123; Ar. ذِرَاعٌ; Aram. דְּרָעָא, ܕܪܳܥܳܐ; Eth. መዝራዕት፡)—abs. זְרוֺעַ [Ex 6:6](https://ref.ly/Exod6:6) \+, זְרֹעַ [Dt 7:19](https://ref.ly/Deut7:19) \+; cstr. זְרוֺעַ [Psalm 10:15](https://ref.ly/Ps10:15) \+, זְרֹעַ [Psalm 89:11](https://ref.ly/Ps89:11) \+; sf. זְרוֺעִי [Je 27:5](https://ref.ly/Jer27:5), [Psalm 89:2](https://ref.ly/Ps89:2), זְרֹעִי [Is 51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5), [63:5](https://ref.ly/Isa63:5); זְרוֺעֲךָ [Psalm 71:18](https://ref.ly/Ps71:18) \+, זְרֹעֲךָ [1 S 3:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam3:31) \+, etc.; pl. זְרֹעִים [Dn 11:31](https://ref.ly/Dan11:31); cstr. זְרֹעֵי [Gn 49:24](https://ref.ly/Gen49:24); sf. זְרֹעַי [Is 51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5), זְרֹעָיו [2 K 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:24); elsewh. fem. form, cstr. זְרוֺעוֺת [Psalm 37:17](https://ref.ly/Ps37:17), זְרֹעוֺת [Jb 22:9](https://ref.ly/Job22:9) \+ 6 t., זְרֹעֹת [Dt 33:27](https://ref.ly/Deut33:27); sf. זְרוֺעֹתָי [Psalm 18:35](https://ref.ly/Ps18:35), זְרֹעֹתָי [2 S 22:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:35), etc. (In abs. and cstr. sg. more oft. *plene,* in pl. and c. sf. more oft. *defect*. In three instances where ´ז is masculine it means a political or military *force* [Dn 11:15](https://ref.ly/Dan11:15), [11:22](https://ref.ly/Dan11:22), [11:31](https://ref.ly/Dan11:31)).—**1\.** *arm,* **a.** lit., of a man Ju [15:14](https://ref.ly/Judg15:14), [16:12](https://ref.ly/Judg16:12), [2 S 1:10](https://ref.ly/2Sam1:10), [Is 9:19](https://ref.ly/Isa9:19), [17:5](https://ref.ly/Isa17:5) (prob. acc. instr.), [Dt 33:20](https://ref.ly/Deut33:20), [Zc 11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17), [11:17](https://ref.ly/Zech11:17), Ct [8:6](https://ref.ly/Song8:6), [Dn 10:6](https://ref.ly/Dan10:6); Ez 13:30 (del. Co); כֹּחוֺ ´ז [Is 44:12](https://ref.ly/Isa44:12) (of a smith, forging); חֲשוּפָה ´ז Ez [4:7](https://ref.ly/Ezek4:7) (of prophet); in fig. of ´י teaching Ephr. to walk [Ho 11:3](https://ref.ly/Hos11:3); זְרֹעֵי יָדָיו [Gn 49:24](https://ref.ly/Gen49:24) *arms of his hands,* i.e. arms that by their strength enable him (Joseph) to draw the bow; וַיַּח אֶת־יְהוֺרָם בֵּין זְרֹעָיו [2 K 9:24](https://ref.ly/2Kgs9:24), i.e. his arms seen from behind, *his shoulders.* **b.** *arm* as *seat of* (human) *strength* [Psalm 18:35](https://ref.ly/Ps18:35) \= [2 S 22:35](https://ref.ly/2Sam22:35); לאֹעֹז ´ז [Jb 26:2](https://ref.ly/Job26:2), ´כּוֺחַ הַזּ [Dn 11:6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6) cf. v:[6](https://ref.ly/Dan11:6); לאֹ עֹז ´ז [Jb 26:2](https://ref.ly/Job26:2); ´חִוַּקְתִּי ז [Ho 7:15](https://ref.ly/Hos7:15) cf. Ez [30:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:24), [30:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:25); also c. שׁבר [Psalm 10:15](https://ref.ly/Ps10:15), [37:17](https://ref.ly/Ps37:17), [Je 48:25](https://ref.ly/Jer48:25) (‖קֶרֶן), Ez [30:21](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:21), [30:22](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:22), [30:24](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:24) cf. v:[25](https://ref.ly/Ezek30:25); רָמָה תִּשָּׁבֵר ´ז [Jb 38:15](https://ref.ly/Job38:15).—On [Mal 2:3](https://ref.ly/Mal2:3) cf. VB. Esp. **c.** *Yahweh's arm* as instrument of deliverance and judgment (oft. Dt Je Is:2 Psalm):—נְטוּיָה ´ז [Dt 4:34](https://ref.ly/Deut4:34), [5:15](https://ref.ly/Deut5:15), [26:8](https://ref.ly/Deut26:8), Ez [20:33](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:33), [20:34](https://ref.ly/Ezek20:34), [Psalm 136:12](https://ref.ly/Ps136:12), cf. [Dt 7:19](https://ref.ly/Deut7:19), [11:2](https://ref.ly/Deut11:2), [1 K 8:42](https://ref.ly/1Kgs8:42) \= [2 Ch 6:32](https://ref.ly/2Chr6:32) (all ‖יָד חֲזָקָה), [Dt 9:29](https://ref.ly/Deut9:29), [2 K 17:36](https://ref.ly/2Kgs17:36); (of creation) [Je 27:5](https://ref.ly/Jer27:5), [32:17](https://ref.ly/Jer32:17) (all ‖כֹּחַ גָּדוֺל); נְטוּיָה ´בִּז [Ex 6:6](https://ref.ly/Exod6:6) (P, or D's revision; no ‖); חֲזָקָה ´זְ [Je 21:5](https://ref.ly/Jer21:5) (‖יָד נְטוּיָה); גְּדֹל זרועך [Ex 15:16](https://ref.ly/Exod15:16) (song), ´גֹּדֶל ז [Psalm 79:11](https://ref.ly/Ps79:11); זְרוֺעִי תְאַמְּצֶנּוּ [Psalm 89:22](https://ref.ly/Ps89:22) (‖יָדִי); ´בִּזְ [Psalm 77:16](https://ref.ly/Ps77:16); ´ז [Psalm 44:4](https://ref.ly/Ps44:4) (‖יְמִינְךָ), קָדְשׁוֺ ´ז [Psalm 98:1](https://ref.ly/Ps98:1) (‖יְמִינוֺ), cf. [Is 52:10](https://ref.ly/Isa52:10); עֻוָּךָ ´ז [Psalm 89:11](https://ref.ly/Ps89:11) cf. [Is 62:8](https://ref.ly/Isa62:8) (‖יְמִינוֺ); ´לִבְּשִׁי־עֹז זְרוֺעַ י [Is 51:9](https://ref.ly/Isa51:9); ´לְךָ ז עִם־גְּבוּרָה [Psalm 89:14](https://ref.ly/Ps89:14); תִּפְאַרְתּוֺ ´ז [Is 63:12](https://ref.ly/Isa63:12); as support of weak [Is 40:11](https://ref.ly/Isa40:11) (fig. of shepherd), cf. זְרֹעֹת עוֺלָם [Dt 33:27](https://ref.ly/Deut33:27); נַחַת זְרוֺעוֺ יַרְאֶה [Is 30:30](https://ref.ly/Isa30:30) (of lightning stroke, ‖השׁמיע הוֺד קולו of thunder); also אִם־וְרוֺעַ כָּאֵל לָח [Jb 40:9](https://ref.ly/Job40:9).
> 
>  Hence, **2\.** *arm,* as symbol of *strength:* **a.** human [1 S 2:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:31), [2:31](https://ref.ly/1Sam2:31) (LXX rd. זַרְעֲךָ and זֶרַע, but v. Klo Dr), Ez [22:6](https://ref.ly/Ezek22:6), [31:17](https://ref.ly/Ezek31:17); וַתְּאַמֵּץ זְרוֺעתֶיהָ [Pr 31:17](https://ref.ly/Prov31:17); בָּשָר ´ז [2 Ch 32:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr32:8); שָׁם בָּשָר זְרֹעוֺ [Je 17:5](https://ref.ly/Jer17:5) (‖יִבְטַח בָּאָדָם); cf. [Psalm 44:4](https://ref.ly/Ps44:4) (‖חַרְבָּם); אישׁ זרוע [Jb 22:8](https://ref.ly/Job22:8); זְרֹעוֺת יְתֹמִים [Jb 22:9](https://ref.ly/Job22:9) (i.e. strength, resources); הָיוּ זרוע לִבְּנֵי־לוֺט [Psalm 83:9](https://ref.ly/Ps83:9); רַבִּים ´ז [Jb 35:9](https://ref.ly/Job35:9); זְרֹעַ גְּדוֺלָה Ez [17:9](https://ref.ly/Ezek17:9) (for pulling up a tree, in metaph.). **b.** \= divine strength [Psalm 71:18](https://ref.ly/Ps71:18) (‖גְּבוּרָה), זְרֹעוֺ משְׁלָה לוֺ [Is 40:10](https://ref.ly/Isa40:10) (‖חָזָק); זְרֹעַי עַמִּים יִשְׁפָֹּטוּ [51:5](https://ref.ly/Isa51:5) \[Str thinks masc. to avoid verbal ending נָה, cf. [49:11](https://ref.ly/Isa49:11)]; and help [Is 33:2](https://ref.ly/Isa33:2) (‖יְשׁוּעָה) cf. [59:16](https://ref.ly/Isa59:16), [63:5](https://ref.ly/Isa63:5); see also [53:1](https://ref.ly/Isa53:1). **3\.** Pl. *forces*, political and military, c. יִעֲמָֹדוּ [Dn 11:15](https://ref.ly/Dan11:15), [11:31](https://ref.ly/Dan11:31); v:[22](https://ref.ly/Dan11:22) (see Bev). **4\.** *Shoulder* of animal sacrificed, belonging to priest [Nu 6:19](https://ref.ly/Num6:19) (P) [Dt 18:3](https://ref.ly/Deut18:3).

> \[Str248] † אֶזְרוֺעַ **n.f.** arm (\= foregoing c. א prosthet.)—*arm* of man כְּתֵפִי מִשִּׁכְמָה תִפָּוֺל וְאֶזְרֹעִי מִקָּנָה :תִשָּׁבֵֽר [Jb 31:22](https://ref.ly/Job31:22); of ´וּבְיָר חֲזָקָה וּבְאֶזְרוֺעַ נְטוּיָה י [Je 32:21](https://ref.ly/Jer32:21), cf. זְרוֺעַ **1 c.**

## זָרַף (id: BDB02746)

\[Str2222] † \[זָרַף] **vb.** prob. drip (cf. either Ar. ذَرَفَ *shed* tears, or Aram. ܙܳܪܺܝܦܬܳܐ *imber vehemens*)—**Hiph.** יַזְרִיף rd. in [Psalm 72:6](https://ref.ly/Ps72:6) by Hup BI Che (for זַרְזִיף) sq. אָרֶץ *cause to drip, irrigate.*

> \[Str2222] † זַרְזִיף **n.\[m.]** drop, dripping (NH *id.;* on form (if genuine) cf. NöM 85, NS 191\)—[Psalm 72:6](https://ref.ly/Ps72:6) אָרֶץ ´כִּרְבִיבִים ז as showers, a *dripping* of the earth, but v. foregoing.

* **Associated Passages:** Psalms 72:6


## זָרַק (id: BDB02748)

\[Str2236] † זָרַק **vb.** to toss or throw (in a volume), scatter abundantly (NH *id.;* Aram. זְרַק *id.,* ܙܪܰܩ *disperse;* As. *zarâḳu* LotzTP138 JägerBAS ii. 292 Anm. \*\*; cf. also Ar. زَرَقَ *cast at, pierce*, زَرَّاقَةٌ and مِزْرَاقٌ *javelin* \= Eth. ምዝርኣቅ፡)—**Qal** *Pf.* ´ז [Ex 24:6](https://ref.ly/Exod24:6), [Lv 17:6](https://ref.ly/Lev17:6), sf. זְרָקוֺ [Ex 9:8](https://ref.ly/Exod9:8), זָֽרְקָה [Ho 7:9](https://ref.ly/Hos7:9) (12 t. Pf.); *Impf.* יִזְרֹק [Lv 7:2](https://ref.ly/Lev7:2) \+ 16 t.; *Inf.* לִזְרֹק Ez [43:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:18); *Imv.* זְרֹק Ez [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:2); *Pt.* זֹרֵק [Lv 7:14](https://ref.ly/Lev7:14), pl. זֹרְקִים [2 Ch 30:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:16); **1\.** *toss* (in handfuls, v. [Ex 9:8](https://ref.ly/Exod9:8); or from a *bowl*, מִזְרָק; opp. הִזָּה to *sprinkle* with the finger): **a.** cummin [Is 28:25](https://ref.ly/Isa28:25), dust, sq. עַל [2 Ch 34:4](https://ref.ly/2Chr34:4), [Jb 2:12](https://ref.ly/Job2:12), ashes השׁמימה, heavenward, [Ex 9:8](https://ref.ly/Exod9:8), [9:10](https://ref.ly/Exod9:10) (P), coals of fire על העיר Ez [10:2](https://ref.ly/Ezek10:2). **b.** blood, מזבה(ה)על *throw* (from a bowl) against the altar [Ex 24:6](https://ref.ly/Exod24:6) (JE), [Lv 17:6](https://ref.ly/Lev17:6) (H), [Nu 18:17](https://ref.ly/Num18:17) (P), [2 K 16:13](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:13), [16:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs16:15), Ez [43:18](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:18), esp. in the ritual, על חמזבת סביב against the altar round about [Ex 29:16](https://ref.ly/Exod29:16), [29:20](https://ref.ly/Exod29:20), [Lv 1:5](https://ref.ly/Lev1:5), [1:11](https://ref.ly/Lev1:11), [3:2](https://ref.ly/Lev3:2), [3:8](https://ref.ly/Lev3:8), [3:13](https://ref.ly/Lev3:13), [7:2](https://ref.ly/Lev7:2), [8:19](https://ref.ly/Lev8:19), [8:24](https://ref.ly/Lev8:24), [9:12](https://ref.ly/Lev9:12), [9:18](https://ref.ly/Lev9:18) (all P); sq. על העם [Ex 24:8](https://ref.ly/Exod24:8) (JE), sq. המזבחה [2 Ch 29:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:22), [29:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:22), [29:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr29:22); without designation of place [Lv 7:14](https://ref.ly/Lev7:14) (P), [2 Ch 30:16](https://ref.ly/2Chr30:16), [35:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr35:11). **c.** *water* for purification, sq. על, Ez [36:25](https://ref.ly/Ezek36:25). **2\.** intrans. (si vera l.) [Ho 7:9](https://ref.ly/Hos7:9) גַּם שֵיבָה זָֽרְקָה בּוֺ grey hairs are *profuse* upon him (? rd. זָֽרְחָה, as in [2 Ch 26:19](https://ref.ly/2Chr26:19)). **Pu.** *Pf.* זֹרַק, of water of purification *poured* over (עַל) one defiled by a corpse [Nu 19:18](https://ref.ly/Num19:18), [19:20](https://ref.ly/Num19:20) (P).

> \[Str4219] † מִזְרָק **n.m.**:[Nu 7:84](https://ref.ly/Num7:84) bowl, bason (prop. vessel for *throwing* or *tossing* a liquid); ´מ [Nu 7:13](https://ref.ly/Num7:13) \+ 13 t., pl. מִזְרָקִים [Zc 14:20](https://ref.ly/Zech14:20), cstr. מִזְרְקֵי [Nu 7:84](https://ref.ly/Num7:84) \+ 2 t., מִזְרָקוֺת [Ne 7:70](https://ref.ly/Neh7:70) \+ 9 t., מִזְרָקֹת [Ex 38:3](https://ref.ly/Exod38:3) \+ 2 t., sf. מִזְרְקֹתָיו [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3); —**1\.** *bowl* for wine [Am 6:6](https://ref.ly/Amos6:6). **2\.** elsewh. for use in ritual of sacrifice: **a.** for use at altar [Ex 27:3](https://ref.ly/Exod27:3), [38:3](https://ref.ly/Exod38:3), [Nu 4:14](https://ref.ly/Num4:14) (all P), [1 K 7:40](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:40), [7:45](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:45), [Je 52:18](https://ref.ly/Jer52:18), [Zc 9:15](https://ref.ly/Zech9:15), [14:20](https://ref.ly/Zech14:20). **b.** given by the princes full of flour mingled with oil for a מנחה [Nu 7:13](https://ref.ly/Num7:13), [7:19](https://ref.ly/Num7:19), [7:25](https://ref.ly/Num7:25), [7:31](https://ref.ly/Num7:31), [7:37](https://ref.ly/Num7:37), [7:43](https://ref.ly/Num7:43), [7:49](https://ref.ly/Num7:49), [7:55](https://ref.ly/Num7:55), [7:61](https://ref.ly/Num7:61), [7:67](https://ref.ly/Num7:67), [7:73](https://ref.ly/Num7:73), [7:79](https://ref.ly/Num7:79), [7:84](https://ref.ly/Num7:84), [7:85](https://ref.ly/Num7:85) (P). **c.** golden basons for use in the temple [1 K 7:50](https://ref.ly/1Kgs7:50), [2 K 12:14](https://ref.ly/2Kgs12:14), [25:15](https://ref.ly/2Kgs25:15), [1 Ch 28:17](https://ref.ly/1Chr28:17), [2 Ch 4:8](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:8), [4:11](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:11), [4:22](https://ref.ly/2Chr4:22), [Je 52:19](https://ref.ly/Jer52:19). **d.** golden basons for the second temple [Ne 7:70](https://ref.ly/Neh7:70).

## זָרַר (id: BDB02750)

\[Str2237] † \[זָרַר] **vb.** only **Po.** sneeze (cf. י(ה)זְרִירוֺ Targum [Jb 41:10](https://ref.ly/Job41:10) for Heb. עֲטִישֹׁתָיו; vb. prob. onomatop.);—וַיְזוֺרֵר הַנַּעַר עד שׁבע פעמים [2 K 4:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:35).

284:b. \[זָרַר]: וַיְזוֺרֵר [2 K 4:35](https://ref.ly/2Kgs4:35) om. LXX; Gr Bur think MT arose (by dittogr.) fr. וַיִּגְהַר עַל־הַנַּעַר.

> \[Str2238] † זֶ֫רֶשׁ **n.pr.f.** wife of Haman [Est 5:10](https://ref.ly/Esth5:10), [5:14](https://ref.ly/Esth5:14), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Esth6:13), [6:13](https://ref.ly/Esth6:13) (acc. to OppEsth. 25 \= Pers. *zaris*, Zend *zairis, la dorêe*, cf. زرّشته *aurum ductile*, Vullii. 128 b; JenVOJ, 1892, 64 comp. Elamit. goddess *Kiriša*).

> \[Str2239] זֶ֫רֶת **n.f.** span (NH *id.* (Targum זַרְתָּא, Syr. ܙܰܪܬܳܐ; appar. fr. Heb.), etym. dub.; Thes and most der. fr. √ זרה, vid. Ol:§ 146 b but also Sta§ 187 a; BaZMG 1887, 607 prop. √ זרה *measure off, determine*, cf. p. 280\)—*span,* as standard of measurement, תִּכֵּן ´שָׁמַיִם בַּז [Is 40:12](https://ref.ly/Isa40:12); elsewh. of actual size, *a span,* distance between ends of thumb and little finger of spread hand; \= 1/2 cubit (v. אַמָּה \& reff.; cf. [Ex 25:10](https://ref.ly/Exod25:10) with JosephAnt. iii. 6, 5\), ´ז [Ex 28:16](https://ref.ly/Exod28:16), [28:16](https://ref.ly/Exod28:16), [39:9](https://ref.ly/Exod39:9), [39:9](https://ref.ly/Exod39:9) (all P) Ez [43:13](https://ref.ly/Ezek43:13); ָ\+זרֶת [1 S 17:4](https://ref.ly/1Sam17:4) six cubits and *a span.* Vid. HultschMetrol. 1882, 443 BenzArcäol. 179\.

> \[Str2240] † זַתּוּא **n.pr.m.** head of a family of returned exiles [Ezr 2:8](https://ref.ly/Ezra2:8) \= [Ne 7:13](https://ref.ly/Neh7:13); cf. also [Ezr 10:27](https://ref.ly/Ezra10:27), [Ne 10:15](https://ref.ly/Neh10:15).

> \[Str2241] † זֵתָם **n.pr.m.** a Levite [1 Ch 23:8](https://ref.ly/1Chr23:8), [26:28](https://ref.ly/1Chr26:28). p. 268

> \[Str2242] † זֵתַר **n.pr.m.** an eunuch of Ahasuerus [Est 1:10](https://ref.ly/Esth1:10) (OppEsth. 25 comp. Pers. *zaitar, conqueror*).
